# Digivice v3.1 (battle and Expedition) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Replace the v1 HP battle and the "SOLO EN MODO HACKER" dead end with the v3.1 battle (attribute triangle, charge, aim, first to 3 hits) and the Expedition (8 areas walked at 1 pace per awake minute, Hike, encounters, midway events, bosses), on a v3 save that keeps the pet already on the device.

**Architecture:** Two new pure-C modules sit next to `digivice_rules.c`: `digivice_expedition.c` (areas, paces, encounters, Hike and scan bonus, midway, boss gate) and `digivice_battle.c` (hit chance, charge, guard, shots, outcome, rewards). The tick walks paces inside the existing awake segments, so 60 s ticks and one long catch-up tick stay byte-identical, and every random roll comes from a xorshift32 `rng` stored in the state (the service mixes hardware entropy in before each tick and message). The service gains a v1 -> v2 -> v3 load chain, three blocking calls and two notification sequences; the draw kit gains trail monsters, bosses, projectiles, effects and cached seeded backgrounds; the app gains the encounter transition, the new battle screen and the trail, map, Hike, midway and gate screens.

**Tech Stack:** C11, Flipper Zero firmware (furi, gui/canvas, notification, saved_struct), u8g2 `u8g2_font_4x6_tf`, host `cc` for tests, GitHub Actions (`fbt`) on the private remote `digivice`.

**Spec (source of truth):** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-digivice-v3-1-battle-expedition-design.md`. Mockups: `docs/superpowers/mockups/v3/v3-combate.html` and `docs/superpowers/mockups/v3/v3-expedicion.html`. Previous plan (conventions): `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-14-digivice-v2-1.md`.

## Global Constraints

- Work continues on the existing branch `digivice-v3.1` (made from `digivice-v2.1`). Do not create a branch. Never commit to `main` or `digivice-v2.1`.
- Never run `./fbt` locally. CI (`.github/workflows/digivice.yml` on remote `digivice` = `pausf/flipper-digivice`) runs the host tests and `./fbt COMPACT=1 DEBUG=0 updater_package` on every push of a `digivice-*` branch. The controller pushes and watches CI; a task executor never pushes.
- Every tuning number lives in `applications/services/digivice/digivice_config.h`. The v1-only constants `DIGIVICE_BASE_DAMAGE`, `DIGIVICE_PLAYER_HP`, `DIGIVICE_VIRUX_HP`, `DIGIVICE_VIRUX_ATK`, `DIGIVICE_TYPE_ADV_X10`, `DIGIVICE_TIMING_HIT_X10`, `DIGIVICE_TIMING_MISS_X10` and `DIGIVICE_FLEE_PCT` are deleted (Task 8).
- `digivice_rules.c/.h`, `digivice_expedition.c/.h`, `digivice_battle.c/.h`, `digivice_i18n.c/.h` and `digivice_config.h` stay pure C with no furi includes. They are host-tested by `applications/services/digivice/test/run.sh` (`cc -std=c11 -Wall -Wextra -Werror`, binaries written to `$TMPDIR`). Rules are written test-first.
- Source files are ASCII-only. Spanish accents live only in `digivice_i18n.c`, as the UTF-8 escape macros already there (`A_ACUTE`, `E_ACUTE`, `I_ACUTE`, `O_ACUTE`, `U_ACUTE`, `N_TILDE`, `EXCL_INV`, `QUES_INV`). The i18n host test scans every digivice source, app file and `desktop_view_digivice.c` for non-ASCII bytes.
- fbt builds with `-Wall -Wextra -Werror -Wstrict-prototypes -Wredundant-decls -Wdouble-promotion -Wundef`, including `-Wformat-truncation`. Size every `snprintf` buffer for the worst case: u32 = 10 digits, u16 = 5, u8 = 3, and every `%s` at its full UTF-8 byte length (2 bytes per accented glyph). Prefer small integer types gcc can bound.
- u8g2 coordinates are unsigned 16 bit: never pass a negative x or y to `canvas_draw_*` directly. Use the clipped `digivice_draw_line`, `digivice_draw_box`, `digivice_draw_dot` (Task 6) or keep the coordinates on screen.
- Conventional Commits. No `Co-Authored-By` lines and no AI attribution anywhere. Stage explicit paths only (`git add <path>...`); never `git add .` or `git add -A`.
- Repo style: 4-space indent, `if(` / `for(` / `while(` / `switch(` with no space, column limit 99, comments only on non-obvious code, matching the neighbours' comment density.
- No Bandai names, art or sprites anywhere. Every monster, boss, area and attribute is original; names come from the approved mockups. Monster, foe and boss names are proper nouns and are never translated.
- Stock-file diff stays limited to the digivice files, `applications/services/digivice/test/run.sh` and `.github/workflows/digivice.yml`. `applications/services/desktop/views/desktop_view_digivice.c` needs no change in this delivery (decision D18).
- Flash ceiling: **at most 40 KB (40,960 bytes) of `text + data` added** over the v2.1 build. Task 1 records the baseline from CI, Task 12 the delta; above 40 KB, stop and re-scope (merge boss variants, cut foe frames) before pushing more.
- RAM: the service stack is 2 KB. No new large locals in the service: the load chain holds at most the 56 B v1 and 84 B v2 temporaries; `digivice_rules_same_visible` copies one state, not two. `DigiviceScene` (about 420 bytes) lives on the app heap only, never on a stack.
- Save format: magic `0xD1`, version `0x03`, 116 bytes. `DigiviceStateV1` (56 B) and `DigiviceStateV2` (84 B) are frozen copies that never change. The user's device holds a real v2 save: v1 -> v2 -> v3 must keep it intact, and a migrated pet's `last_tick` is re-anchored to now so it earns no free paces or care penalties for the time spent on the old firmware.
- Every firmware-touching task ends with a "CI/device check (controller)" step. Device checks use the CI artifact `digivice-update-package` of the pushed commit, flashed over USB (`scripts/selfupdate.py` or qFlipper), only after CI is green. The final task's push is a "Step: push (controller only)".

## Decisions taken while planning (spec ambiguities)

- **D1. The Baby fights.** Per the controller edit, only the Egg is refused (`DigiviceResultTooYoung`). The Baby earns paces and fights trail foes with `P0 = 8` and a **2x2** projectile (`DIGIVICE_STAGE_SHOT {2, 2, 3, 5}`). VIRUX still spawn only for Rookie+ (unchanged `on_deed` rule) and bosses stay Champion-gated.
- **D2. Shield bookkeeping.** The spec's `digivice_battle_finish(digivice, kind, result)` cannot know whether the fight used the Shield, so both it and `digivice_rules_battle_result` take an extra `bool shield_used`. The service draws only one `rng_pct` (the drop roll); nothing else in the result needs one.
- **D3. Result enums.** `DigiviceBattleResult` keeps `Win`, `Lose`, `Fled` and adds `Draw`; `DigiviceBattleOutcome` adds `Drawn`. Both move from `digivice_rules.h` to the new `digivice_battle.h`. `DigiviceResultTooYoung` is appended to `DigiviceResult`, and `digivice_rules_can_battle(s, now)` gains `now` for the asleep refusal.
- **D4. Pace instants and equivalence.** Pace k of an awake step that ends at `t_end` happens at `t_end - (acc - 60k)`. The pace that reaches distance 0 also zeroes `pace_acc_s`, so 60 s ticks and one long tick end byte-identical (`test_one_hour_tick_matches_sixty_minute_ticks`, plus the existing `test_60s_ticks_match_one_tick_with_care`, which now also walks).
- **D5. Hike timing.** Beats fall at `(k + 1) * 500` ms, k = 0..23, measured from the session start; a tap is good when `|phase| <= 150` ms (inclusive: 150 is good, 151 is not). The session ends at 12.5 s. Hike is refused asleep, sick or as an Egg; scan paces follow the spec literally (stage and sickness only, not sleep).
- **D6. All clear.** After the eighth boss, `cleared == 0xFF && distance == 0` means "all areas clear", not "boss ready", so area 8's boss cannot be farmed from that screen. Any cleared area can still be selected from the map and replayed (repeat clears pay half).
- **D7. Midway result.** `DigiviceMidwayResult { kind, ok }`: `kind == DigiviceMidwayCount` when nothing was available. Cache `ok` = Shield (false = EXP). A skipped or lost shortcut both return `ok = false`; the UI knows which it asked. Spring also clears `starving_since`, since hunger becomes full.
- **D8. Sanitize.** `mid_state > 2` clamps to 2 (never a free event); `enc_foe` 2..0xFE becomes none; an out-of-range `area` becomes 0; `distance` clamps to the area length.
- **D9. Exclusivity.** `on_deed` also refuses a VIRUX while a trail encounter is pending, and a pace never rolls while a VIRUX is active.
- **D10. Auto-start memory.** The app remembers the last offered encounter as `(kind, key)`: `enc_expires` for a trail foe, `virux_expires` for a VIRUX, `cleared` for a boss. Auto-start runs when the app opens and once per second on the main screen, only when `can_battle` is OK.
- **D11. Battle HUD.** Names are up to 9 glyphs (WAVEDRAKE, GUSAFERRO), so the hit boxes follow each name instead of the spec's fixed `x = 32 + i*6` / `87 - i*6`; "VS" sits at x 58.
- **D12. Trail view.** The area scene scrolls behind the trail HUD; the progress strip (y 18..33), the columns (y 34..50) and the footer panel (y 51..63) sit on white boxes.
- **D13. Scene API.** `digivice_draw_scene(canvas, scene, scroll, frame, day_minute)` takes a scroll counter and the minute of the day (0..1439) instead of the hour, so the 06:00 to 07:30 dusk band works. Area 8 (NÚCLEO NULO) always uses the night palette, as the mockup's negative world.
- **D14. Draw kit files.** The draw kit is split into `digivice_draw_world.c` (monsters, bosses, shots, effects) and `digivice_draw_scene.c` (backgrounds). `digivice_draw_boss` takes the pet's form, because NEGATIVO is the player's pet in negative.
- **D15. Strings.** One row beyond the spec table: `EncAlert` ("ALERTA" / "ALERT") for the boss intro, which the spec shows but does not list. Home labels (`HomeEncounter`, `HomeBoss`) get a 42 px budget: the 46 px alert box minus its margin.
- **D16. No negative rows.** The victory banner drops from y 0 to 10 (not -14) and "DERROTA" slides in from x 5, because labels and frames go through unclipped u8g2 calls.
- **D17. Red build between Tasks 8 and 9.** Removing the v1 battle rules (Task 8) breaks the v1 battle screen until Task 9 replaces it. The host tests stay green; the controller pushes Tasks 8 and 9 together.
- **D18. Home view.** The home drawing lives in `digivice_draw_home` (`digivice_draw.c`), so the attention bubble and labels go there (Task 11) and `desktop_view_digivice.c` stays untouched.
- **D19. Flash measurement.** Task 1 adds a CI step that prints `arm-none-eabi-size build/f7-firmware-C/firmware.elf`; its first run is the v2.1 baseline.
- **D20. Stack and draw-time evidence.** The service logs `Stack free after load` and `Stack free after a catch-up tick` (`furi_thread_get_stack_space`), and the trail view logs `Scene drawn in N ms` every 50 frames, at info level.

### Task 1: Commit the spec and plan, report the firmware size in CI

**Files:**
- Add: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-digivice-v3-1-battle-expedition-design.md` (already in the tree, untracked)
- Add: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-digivice-v3-1.md` (this plan, untracked)
- Modify: `.github/workflows/digivice.yml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces: a CI log line with the `text`, `data` and `bss` of `build/f7-firmware-C/firmware.elf` on every push. Task 12 compares against the number this task's push prints.

- [ ] **Step 1: Check the branch**

```bash
git -C <repo> branch --show-current
git -C <repo> status --short
```

Expected: `digivice-v3.1`, and only the two untracked docs (plus untracked files you must leave alone). Do not create a branch.

- [ ] **Step 2: Print the firmware size after the build**

In `.github/workflows/digivice.yml`, replace:

```yaml
      - name: Build update package
        run: ./fbt COMPACT=1 DEBUG=0 updater_package
```

with:

```yaml
      - name: Build update package
        run: ./fbt COMPACT=1 DEBUG=0 updater_package
      - name: Report firmware size
        # fbt downloads its toolchain into toolchain/<arch>-<os>; text + data is the flash used.
        run: toolchain/x86_64-linux/bin/arm-none-eabi-size build/f7-firmware-C/firmware.elf
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Commit**

```bash
git add docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-digivice-v3-1-battle-expedition-design.md \
    docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-digivice-v3-1.md .github/workflows/digivice.yml
git commit -m "docs(digivice): add the v3.1 battle and Expedition spec and plan" \
    -m "CI now prints the firmware section sizes so the v3.1 flash budget can be checked."
```

- [ ] **Step 4: CI/device check (controller)**

The controller pushes `digivice-v3.1` and watches the `digivice` workflow. `rules-tests` and `firmware` must be green, and the new "Report firmware size" step prints one line such as `text data bss dec hex filename`. The controller writes down `text + data` of this run: it is the **v2.1 baseline** for Task 12. Nothing changes on the device.

### Task 2: v3 state, v1 -> v2 -> v3 migration, rng and attributes

**Files:**
- Modify: `applications/services/digivice/digivice_config.h`
- Modify: `applications/services/digivice/digivice_rules.h`
- Modify: `applications/services/digivice/digivice_rules.c`
- Modify: `applications/services/digivice/digivice_srv.c` (load chain, entropy, stack logs)
- Test: `applications/services/digivice/test/digivice_rules_test.c`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the v2.1 `DigiviceState`, `DigiviceStateV1`, `digivice_rules_init`, `digivice_rules_reset`, `digivice_rules_sanitize`, `digivice_rules_same_visible` (all in `digivice_rules.h`).
- Produces (used by every later task):
  - `typedef enum { DigiviceItemNone, DigiviceItemShield, DigiviceItemCount } DigiviceItem;`
  - `typedef enum { DigiviceAttrPortador, DigiviceAttrRuido, DigiviceAttrCifrado, DigiviceAttrNulo, DigiviceAttrCount } DigiviceAttr;`
  - `#define DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE 0xFF`
  - `DigiviceStateV2`: the frozen 84-byte v2 layout. `DigiviceState` (116 bytes) = the v2 fields, then `uint8_t area, cleared, enc_foe, mid_state, item, hike_count, scan_count, reserved3; uint16_t distance, paces_since_fight; uint32_t pace_acc_s, enc_expires, bonus_window_start, injured_until, rng;`
  - `void digivice_rules_migrate_v1(const DigiviceStateV1* v1, DigiviceStateV2* v2);` (output type changed)
  - `void digivice_rules_migrate_v2(const DigiviceStateV2* v2, DigiviceState* s);`
  - `uint16_t digivice_rules_area_length(uint8_t area);`
  - `uint32_t digivice_rules_rng(DigiviceState* s);` `uint8_t digivice_rules_rng_pct(DigiviceState* s);` `void digivice_rules_mix_entropy(DigiviceState* s, uint32_t entropy);`
  - `int8_t digivice_rules_attr_adv(uint8_t attacker, uint8_t defender);` `DigiviceAttr digivice_rules_form_attr(const DigiviceState* s);` `DigiviceAttr digivice_rules_type_attr(DigiviceType type);`
  - Config: `DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT`, `DIGIVICE_AREA_LENGTHS`, `DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE`, `DIGIVICE_HIKE_CAP`, `DIGIVICE_SCAN_CAP`.
  - Service: save version `0x03`; v2 and v1 files migrate and re-anchor `last_tick`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

In `applications/services/digivice/test/digivice_rules_test.c`, replace:

```c
    CHECK(s.poop == 0 && s.sick == 0 && s.care_mistakes == 0 && s.doses_needed == 0);
    CHECK(s.poop_acc_s == 0 && s.poop_dirty_since == 0 && s.starving_since == 0);
    CHECK(s.sick_since == 0 && s.light_on_asleep_s == 0);
    // The v1 file is 56 bytes; v2 appends 28 and keeps every v1 offset.
    CHECK(sizeof(DigiviceStateV1) == 56);
    CHECK(sizeof(DigiviceState) == 84);
    CHECK(offsetof(DigiviceState, corrupt_acc_s) == offsetof(DigiviceStateV1, corrupt_acc_s));
}

static void test_normal_mode_ignores_deeds(void) {
```

with:

```c
    CHECK(s.poop == 0 && s.sick == 0 && s.care_mistakes == 0 && s.doses_needed == 0);
    CHECK(s.poop_acc_s == 0 && s.poop_dirty_since == 0 && s.starving_since == 0);
    CHECK(s.sick_since == 0 && s.light_on_asleep_s == 0);
    // The v1 file is 56 bytes, v2 appends 28 and v3 another 32, each keeping the older offsets.
    CHECK(sizeof(DigiviceStateV1) == 56);
    CHECK(sizeof(DigiviceStateV2) == 84);
    CHECK(sizeof(DigiviceState) == 116);
    CHECK(offsetof(DigiviceState, corrupt_acc_s) == offsetof(DigiviceStateV1, corrupt_acc_s));
    CHECK(offsetof(DigiviceState, light_on_asleep_s) ==
          offsetof(DigiviceStateV2, light_on_asleep_s));
    CHECK(offsetof(DigiviceState, area) == sizeof(DigiviceStateV2));
}

static void check_v3_defaults(const DigiviceState* s) {
    CHECK(s->area == 0 && s->cleared == 0 && s->enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE);
    CHECK(s->mid_state == 0 && s->item == DigiviceItemNone && s->reserved3 == 0);
    CHECK(s->hike_count == 0 && s->scan_count == 0 && s->paces_since_fight == 0);
    CHECK(s->distance == 480 && s->distance == digivice_rules_area_length(0));
    CHECK(s->pace_acc_s == 0 && s->enc_expires == 0 && s->bonus_window_start == 0);
    CHECK(s->injured_until == 0);
    CHECK(s->rng == ((s->last_tick ^ s->born_at ^ 0x9E3779B9UL) | 1UL));
    CHECK(s->rng != 0);
}

static void test_v3_defaults(void) {
    DigiviceState s = fresh(T0);
    check_v3_defaults(&s);
    s.area = 3;
    s.cleared = 7;
    s.item = DigiviceItemShield;
    digivice_rules_reset(&s, T0 + 10);
    check_v3_defaults(&s);
}

static void test_area_lengths(void) {
    const uint16_t lengths[DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT] = DIGIVICE_AREA_LENGTHS;
    uint32_t total = 0;
    for(uint8_t a = 0; a < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT; a++) {
        CHECK(digivice_rules_area_length(a) == lengths[a]);
        total += lengths[a];
    }
    CHECK(total == 7320);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_area_length(0) == 480 && digivice_rules_area_length(7) == 1440);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_area_length(200) == 1440);
}

static DigiviceStateV2 v2_fixture(void) {
    DigiviceStateV2 v2;
    memset(&v2, 0, sizeof(v2));
    v2.form = DigiviceFormTagdrake;
    v2.prev_form = DigiviceFormBytemon;
    v2.evo_from = DigiviceFormNone;
    v2.hunger = 2;
    v2.mood = 3;
    v2.corruption = 15;
    v2.virux_type = DigiviceTypeSubGhz;
    v2.strength = 17;
    v2.wins = 4;
    v2.losses = 5;
    v2.data[0] = 31;
    v2.data[1] = 32;
    v2.data[2] = 33;
    v2.data[3] = 34;
    v2.exp = 777;
    v2.virux_expires = T0 + 900;
    v2.last_tick = T0 - 60;
    v2.born_at = T0 - DAY(9);
    v2.clean_since = 0;
    v2.hunger_acc_s = 101;
    v2.mood_acc_s = 102;
    v2.corrupt_acc_s = 103;
    v2.lang = DigiviceLangEn;
    v2.mode = DigiviceModeHacker;
    v2.poop = 2;
    v2.sick = 1;
    v2.care_mistakes = 3;
    v2.light_on = 0;
    v2.doses_needed = 2;
    v2.poop_acc_s = 104;
    v2.poop_dirty_since = T0 - H(2);
    v2.starving_since = T0 - H(3);
    v2.sick_since = T0 - H(4);
    v2.light_on_asleep_s = 105;
    return v2;
}

static void check_v2_prefix(const DigiviceState* s, const DigiviceStateV2* v2) {
    CHECK(s->form == v2->form && s->prev_form == v2->prev_form && s->evo_from == v2->evo_from);
    CHECK(s->hunger == v2->hunger && s->mood == v2->mood && s->corruption == v2->corruption);
    CHECK(s->virux_type == v2->virux_type && s->virux_expires == v2->virux_expires);
    CHECK(s->strength == v2->strength && s->wins == v2->wins && s->losses == v2->losses);
    CHECK(memcmp(s->data, v2->data, sizeof(s->data)) == 0);
    CHECK(s->exp == v2->exp && s->last_tick == v2->last_tick && s->born_at == v2->born_at);
    CHECK(s->clean_since == v2->clean_since && s->hunger_acc_s == v2->hunger_acc_s);
    CHECK(s->mood_acc_s == v2->mood_acc_s && s->corrupt_acc_s == v2->corrupt_acc_s);
    CHECK(s->lang == v2->lang && s->mode == v2->mode && s->poop == v2->poop);
    CHECK(s->sick == v2->sick && s->care_mistakes == v2->care_mistakes);
    CHECK(s->light_on == v2->light_on && s->doses_needed == v2->doses_needed);
    CHECK(s->poop_acc_s == v2->poop_acc_s && s->poop_dirty_since == v2->poop_dirty_since);
    CHECK(s->starving_since == v2->starving_since && s->sick_since == v2->sick_since);
    CHECK(s->light_on_asleep_s == v2->light_on_asleep_s);
}

static void test_migrate_v2_field_by_field(void) {
    DigiviceStateV2 v2 = v2_fixture();
    DigiviceState s;
    memset(&s, 0xAA, sizeof(s));
    digivice_rules_migrate_v2(&v2, &s);
    check_v2_prefix(&s, &v2);
    check_v3_defaults(&s);
}

static void test_migrate_chain_v1_to_v3(void) {
    DigiviceStateV1 v1 = {
        .form = DigiviceFormBeamdrake,
        .prev_form = DigiviceFormBytemon,
        .evo_from = DigiviceFormNone,
        .hunger = 1,
        .mood = 4,
        .corruption = 0,
        .virux_type = DigiviceTypeNone,
        .strength = 9,
        .wins = 2,
        .losses = 1,
        .data = {1, 2, 30, 4},
        .exp = 400,
        .virux_expires = 0,
        .last_tick = T0,
        .born_at = T0 - DAY(8),
        .clean_since = T0 - DAY(1),
    };
    DigiviceStateV2 v2;
    DigiviceState s;
    digivice_rules_migrate_v1(&v1, &v2);
    digivice_rules_migrate_v2(&v2, &s);
    CHECK(s.form == DigiviceFormBeamdrake && s.exp == 400 && s.strength == 9);
    CHECK(s.data[DigiviceTypeIr] == 30 && s.wins == 2 && s.losses == 1);
    CHECK(s.last_tick == T0 && s.born_at == T0 - DAY(8));
    CHECK(s.lang == DigiviceLangEs && s.mode == DigiviceModeNormal && s.light_on == 1);
    check_v3_defaults(&s);
}

static void test_sanitize_clamps_v3(void) {
    DigiviceState s = fresh(T0);
    s.area = 9;
    s.enc_foe = 2;
    s.mid_state = 7;
    s.item = 5;
    s.distance = 60000;
    s.hike_count = 200;
    s.scan_count = 200;
    s.paces_since_fight = 999;
    s.rng = 0;
    digivice_rules_sanitize(&s);
    CHECK(s.area == 0 && s.enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE && s.mid_state == 2);
    CHECK(s.item == DigiviceItemNone && s.distance == 480);
    CHECK(s.hike_count == DIGIVICE_HIKE_CAP && s.scan_count == DIGIVICE_SCAN_CAP);
    CHECK(s.paces_since_fight == DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE && s.rng != 0);

    // A distance inside the area length is kept; each in-range pool index is kept.
    s.area = 7;
    s.distance = 1440;
    s.enc_foe = 1;
    s.enc_expires = T0 + 60;
    digivice_rules_sanitize(&s);
    CHECK(s.distance == 1440 && s.enc_foe == 1 && s.enc_expires == T0 + 60);

    // Both encounter slots set: the trail one goes.
    s.virux_type = DigiviceTypeNfc;
    s.virux_expires = T0 + 60;
    digivice_rules_sanitize(&s);
    CHECK(s.enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE && s.enc_expires == 0);
    CHECK(s.virux_type == DigiviceTypeNfc);
}

static void test_same_visible_ignores_pace_bookkeeping(void) {
    DigiviceState a = fresh(T0);
    DigiviceState b = a;
    b.pace_acc_s = 59;
    b.paces_since_fight = 12;
    b.rng ^= 0x1234;
    b.hike_count = 10;
    b.scan_count = 3;
    b.bonus_window_start = T0;
    b.distance = 300;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_same_visible(&a, &b));
    b.distance = 0;
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_same_visible(&a, &b));
    b = a;
    b.enc_foe = 1;
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_same_visible(&a, &b));
    b = a;
    b.mid_state = 1;
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_same_visible(&a, &b));
    b = a;
    b.item = DigiviceItemShield;
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_same_visible(&a, &b));
    b = a;
    b.injured_until = T0 + 3600;
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_same_visible(&a, &b));
}

static void test_rng(void) {
    DigiviceState a = fresh(T0);
    DigiviceState b = a;
    for(int i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
        uint32_t x = digivice_rules_rng(&a);
        CHECK(x != 0 && x == a.rng);
        CHECK(digivice_rules_rng_pct(&b) == x % 100);
    }
    a.rng = 0;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_rng(&a) != 0);
    a.rng = 0x55;
    digivice_rules_mix_entropy(&a, 0x55);
    CHECK(a.rng == 1);
    digivice_rules_mix_entropy(&a, 0xF0);
    CHECK(a.rng == 0xF1);
}

static void test_attr_triangle(void) {
    CHECK(digivice_rules_attr_adv(DigiviceAttrPortador, DigiviceAttrRuido) == 1);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_attr_adv(DigiviceAttrRuido, DigiviceAttrCifrado) == 1);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_attr_adv(DigiviceAttrCifrado, DigiviceAttrPortador) == 1);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_attr_adv(DigiviceAttrRuido, DigiviceAttrPortador) == -1);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_attr_adv(DigiviceAttrCifrado, DigiviceAttrRuido) == -1);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_attr_adv(DigiviceAttrPortador, DigiviceAttrCifrado) == -1);
    for(uint8_t a = 0; a < DigiviceAttrCount; a++) {
        CHECK(digivice_rules_attr_adv(a, a) == 0);
        CHECK(digivice_rules_attr_adv(a, DigiviceAttrNulo) == 0);
        CHECK(digivice_rules_attr_adv(DigiviceAttrNulo, a) == 0);
        CHECK(digivice_rules_attr_adv(a, 9) == 0 && digivice_rules_attr_adv(9, a) == 0);
    }
}

static void test_form_and_type_attr(void) {
    static const uint8_t expected[DigiviceFormVirubyte] = {
        [DigiviceFormByteegg] = DigiviceAttrNulo,
        [DigiviceFormBitling] = DigiviceAttrNulo,
        [DigiviceFormBytemon] = DigiviceAttrNulo,
        [DigiviceFormTagdrake] = DigiviceAttrCifrado,
        [DigiviceFormWavedrake] = DigiviceAttrPortador,
        [DigiviceFormBeamdrake] = DigiviceAttrRuido,
        [DigiviceFormPlugdrake] = DigiviceAttrNulo,
    };
    DigiviceState s = fresh(T0);
    for(uint8_t f = 0; f < DigiviceFormVirubyte; f++) {
        s.form = f;
        CHECK(digivice_rules_form_attr(&s) == expected[f]);
    }
    // Virubyte is static whatever it was before.
    s.form = DigiviceFormVirubyte;
    for(uint8_t f = 0; f < DigiviceFormVirubyte; f++) {
        s.prev_form = f;
        CHECK(digivice_rules_form_attr(&s) == DigiviceAttrRuido);
    }
    CHECK(digivice_rules_type_attr(DigiviceTypeNfc) == DigiviceAttrCifrado);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_type_attr(DigiviceTypeSubGhz) == DigiviceAttrPortador);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_type_attr(DigiviceTypeIr) == DigiviceAttrRuido);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_type_attr(DigiviceTypeUsb) == DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_type_attr(DigiviceTypeNone) == DigiviceAttrNulo);
}
```

```c
void digivice_rules_migrate_v2(const DigiviceStateV2* v2, DigiviceState* s) {
    memset(s, 0, sizeof(DigiviceState));
    // The v3 struct starts with the exact v2 layout (static_assert in digivice_rules.h).
    memcpy(s, v2, sizeof(DigiviceStateV2));
    v3_defaults(s);
}

void digivice_rules_sanitize(DigiviceState* s) {
    if(s->lang >= DigiviceLangCount) s->lang = DigiviceLangEs;
    if(s->mode >= DigiviceModeCount) s->mode = DigiviceModeNormal;
    if(s->area >= DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT) s->area = 0;
    if(s->enc_foe > 1) s->enc_foe = DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE;
    if(s->mid_state > 2) s->mid_state = 2;
    if(s->item >= DigiviceItemCount) s->item = DigiviceItemNone;
    uint16_t length = digivice_rules_area_length(s->area);
    if(s->distance > length) s->distance = length;
    if(s->hike_count > DIGIVICE_HIKE_CAP) s->hike_count = DIGIVICE_HIKE_CAP;
    if(s->scan_count > DIGIVICE_SCAN_CAP) s->scan_count = DIGIVICE_SCAN_CAP;
    if(s->paces_since_fight > DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE) {
        s->paces_since_fight = DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE;
    }
    if(s->rng == 0) s->rng = 1;
    // Only one encounter can be pending: the VIRUX keeps its slot.
    if(s->virux_type != DigiviceTypeNone && s->enc_foe != DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE) {
        s->enc_foe = DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE;
        s->enc_expires = 0;
    }
}

uint32_t digivice_rules_rng(DigiviceState* s) {
    uint32_t x = s->rng ? s->rng : 1;
    x ^= x << 13;
    x ^= x >> 17;
    x ^= x << 5;
    s->rng = x;
    return x;
}

uint8_t digivice_rules_rng_pct(DigiviceState* s) {
    return (uint8_t)(digivice_rules_rng(s) % 100);
}

void digivice_rules_mix_entropy(DigiviceState* s, uint32_t entropy) {
    s->rng ^= entropy;
    if(s->rng == 0) s->rng = 1;
}

// The attribute each attribute beats; Nulo beats nothing and loses to nothing.
static const uint8_t attr_beats[DigiviceAttrNulo] = {
    [DigiviceAttrPortador] = DigiviceAttrRuido,
    [DigiviceAttrRuido] = DigiviceAttrCifrado,
    [DigiviceAttrCifrado] = DigiviceAttrPortador,
};

int8_t digivice_rules_attr_adv(uint8_t attacker, uint8_t defender) {
    if(attacker >= DigiviceAttrNulo || defender >= DigiviceAttrNulo) return 0;
    if(attr_beats[attacker] == defender) return 1;
    if(attr_beats[defender] == attacker) return -1;
    return 0;
}

DigiviceAttr digivice_rules_form_attr(const DigiviceState* s) {
    switch(s->form) {
    case DigiviceFormWavedrake:
        return DigiviceAttrPortador;
    case DigiviceFormTagdrake:
        return DigiviceAttrCifrado;
    case DigiviceFormBeamdrake:
    case DigiviceFormVirubyte:
        return DigiviceAttrRuido;
    default:
        return DigiviceAttrNulo;
    }
}

DigiviceAttr digivice_rules_type_attr(DigiviceType type) {
    switch(type) {
    case DigiviceTypeNfc:
        return DigiviceAttrCifrado;
    case DigiviceTypeSubGhz:
        return DigiviceAttrPortador;
    case DigiviceTypeIr:
        return DigiviceAttrRuido;
    default:
        return DigiviceAttrNulo;
    }
}

bool digivice_rules_set_lang(DigiviceState* s, uint8_t lang) {
```

```c
}

bool digivice_rules_same_visible(const DigiviceState* a, const DigiviceState* b) {
    // One copy, not two: the service runs this on a 2 KB stack.
    DigiviceState y = *b;
    y.last_tick = a->last_tick;
    y.hunger_acc_s = a->hunger_acc_s;
    y.mood_acc_s = a->mood_acc_s;
    y.corrupt_acc_s = a->corrupt_acc_s;
    y.poop_acc_s = a->poop_acc_s;
    y.light_on_asleep_s = a->light_on_asleep_s;
    y.pace_acc_s = a->pace_acc_s;
    y.paces_since_fight = a->paces_since_fight;
    y.rng = a->rng;
    y.hike_count = a->hike_count;
    y.scan_count = a->scan_count;
    y.bonus_window_start = a->bonus_window_start;
    // Paces are bookkeeping; only reaching the boss (distance 0) is visible.
    if((a->distance == 0) == (b->distance == 0)) y.distance = a->distance;
    return memcmp(a, &y, sizeof(DigiviceState)) == 0;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing Expedition tests**

Create `applications/services/digivice/test/digivice_expedition_test.c` with exactly this content:

```c
// Host-side test for the Expedition rules. Run: applications/services/digivice/test/run.sh
#include "../digivice_expedition.h"
#include "../digivice_config.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

static int failures = 0;

#define CHECK(cond)                                                \
    do {                                                           \
        if(!(cond)) {                                              \
            printf("FAIL %s:%d: %s\n", __FILE__, __LINE__, #cond); \
            failures++;                                            \
        }                                                          \
    } while(0)

#define H(n)   ((uint32_t)(n) * 3600UL)
#define DAY(n) ((uint32_t)(n) * 86400UL)

// Day 10 at 08:00, a wide-awake starting point.
#define T0 (DAY(10) + H(8))

static DigiviceState with_form(uint8_t form) {
    DigiviceState s;
    digivice_rules_init(&s, T0);
    s.form = form;
    return s;
}

// A seed whose first xorshift32 draw is `pct` modulo 100.
static uint32_t seed_with_pct(uint8_t pct) {
    for(uint32_t seed = 1;; seed++) {
        DigiviceState probe;
        memset(&probe, 0, sizeof(probe));
        probe.rng = seed;
        if(digivice_rules_rng(&probe) % 100 == pct) return seed;
    }
}

static void test_tables(void) {
    static const uint16_t trail_q[DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT][2] = {
        {10, 10}, {16, 16}, {24, 24}, {34, 34}, {59, 46}, {78, 60}, {98, 76}, {122, 122}};
    static const uint16_t boss_q[DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT] = {14, 22, 33, 47, 64, 84, 106, 131};
    for(uint8_t a = 0; a < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT; a++) {
        for(uint8_t i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
            DigiviceFoe foe = digivice_expedition_pool_foe(a, i);
            CHECK(foe < DigiviceFoeCount);
            CHECK(digivice_expedition_foe_power(a, foe) == trail_q[a][i]);
        }
        CHECK(digivice_expedition_boss_power(a) == boss_q[a]);
        CHECK(digivice_expedition_streak_area(a) == (a >= 4));
    }
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_pool_foe(0, 0) == DigiviceFoeEstatik);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_pool_foe(7, 1) == DigiviceFoeFuzzcan);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_foe_attr(DigiviceFoeKorno) == DigiviceAttrPortador);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_foe_attr(DigiviceFoeChipko) == DigiviceAttrCifrado);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_foe_attr(DigiviceFoeChatarro) == DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(!digivice_expedition_foe_champion(DigiviceFoeMokobit));
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_foe_champion(DigiviceFoeFuzzcan));
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_boss_attr(2) == DigiviceAttrPortador);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_boss_attr(7) == DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(strcmp(digivice_expedition_boss_name(0), "CHISPAPO") == 0);
    CHECK(strcmp(digivice_expedition_foe_name(DigiviceFoeVampixel), "VAMPIXEL") == 0);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_midway_kind(0) == DigiviceMidwaySpring);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_midway_kind(1) == DigiviceMidwayCache);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_midway_kind(2) == DigiviceMidwayShortcut);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_midway_kind(7) == DigiviceMidwayCache);
}

static void test_one_pace_per_awake_minute(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    digivice_rules_tick(&s, T0 + 59);
    CHECK(s.distance == 480 && s.pace_acc_s == 59);
    digivice_rules_tick(&s, T0 + 60);
    CHECK(s.distance == 479 && s.pace_acc_s == 0);
    // 30 s + 30 s carry into one pace.
    digivice_rules_tick(&s, T0 + 90);
    CHECK(s.distance == 479 && s.pace_acc_s == 30);
    digivice_rules_tick(&s, T0 + 120);
    CHECK(s.distance == 478 && s.paces_since_fight <= 2);
}

static void test_baby_walks_egg_and_sick_do_not(void) {
    DigiviceState baby = with_form(DigiviceFormBitling);
    digivice_rules_tick(&baby, T0 + 600);
    CHECK(baby.distance == 470);

    DigiviceState egg = with_form(DigiviceFormByteegg);
    digivice_rules_tick(&egg, T0 + 600);
    CHECK(egg.distance == 480 && egg.pace_acc_s == 0);

    DigiviceState sick = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    sick.sick = 1;
    sick.doses_needed = 1;
    digivice_rules_tick(&sick, T0 + 600);
    CHECK(sick.distance == 480);

    DigiviceState boss = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    boss.distance = 0;
    uint32_t rng = boss.rng;
    digivice_rules_tick(&boss, T0 + 600);
    CHECK(boss.distance == 0 && boss.rng == rng && boss.enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE);
}

static void test_no_paces_asleep(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.distance = 1000;
    uint32_t night = DAY(10) + H(23);
    s.last_tick = night;
    digivice_rules_tick(&s, night + H(8));
    CHECK(s.distance == 1000);
    digivice_rules_tick(&s, night + H(8) + 600);
    CHECK(s.distance == 990);
}

static void test_one_hour_tick_matches_sixty_minute_ticks(void) {
    DigiviceState a = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
    DigiviceState b = a;
    for(uint32_t t = T0 + 60; t <= T0 + H(10); t += 60)
        digivice_rules_tick(&a, t);
    digivice_rules_tick(&b, T0 + H(10));
    CHECK(a.distance == b.distance && a.distance < 480);
    CHECK(memcmp(&a, &b, sizeof(a)) == 0);
}

static void test_48h_catch_up_is_clamped(void) {
    DigiviceState a = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
    a.area = 7;
    a.distance = 1440;
    DigiviceState b = a;
    a.last_tick = T0 - DAY(5);
    b.last_tick = T0;
    digivice_rules_tick(&a, T0 + H(48));
    digivice_rules_tick(&b, T0 + H(48));
    CHECK(memcmp(&a, &b, sizeof(a)) == 0);
    CHECK(a.distance < 1440);

    // Without care rules in the way, the 32 awake hours of a 48 h window are 1,920 paces.
    DigiviceState c = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
    c.distance = 5000;
    digivice_expedition_walk(&c, H(32), T0 + H(32));
    CHECK(c.distance == 5000 - 1920);
}

static void test_exact_pace_instant(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.pace_acc_s = 30;
    s.paces_since_fight = DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE - 1;
    digivice_rules_tick(&s, T0 + 90);
    // The pace fell at T0 + 30, so that is when the guaranteed encounter spawned.
    CHECK(s.enc_foe <= 1);
    CHECK(s.enc_expires == T0 + 30 + DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_TTL_S);
    CHECK(s.paces_since_fight == 1);
}

static void test_encounter_roll(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.rng = seed_with_pct(DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_PCT - 1);
    s.paces_since_fight = 10;
    digivice_expedition_walk(&s, 60, T0 + 60);
    CHECK(s.enc_foe <= 1 && s.paces_since_fight == 0);
    CHECK(s.enc_expires == T0 + 60 + DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_TTL_S);

    DigiviceState t = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    t.rng = seed_with_pct(DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_PCT);
    t.paces_since_fight = 10;
    digivice_expedition_walk(&t, 60, T0 + 60);
    CHECK(t.enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE && t.paces_since_fight == 11);
}

static void test_guarantee_at_pace_50(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.rng = seed_with_pct(99);
    s.paces_since_fight = DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE - 2;
    digivice_expedition_walk(&s, 60, T0 + 60);
    CHECK(s.enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE);
    s.rng = seed_with_pct(99);
    digivice_expedition_walk(&s, 60, T0 + 120);
    CHECK(s.enc_foe <= 1 && s.paces_since_fight == 0);
}

static void test_no_roll_while_pending(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.enc_foe = 1;
    s.enc_expires = T0 + H(1);
    s.paces_since_fight = DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE;
    uint32_t rng = s.rng;
    digivice_expedition_walk(&s, 600, T0 + 600);
    CHECK(s.enc_foe == 1 && s.enc_expires == T0 + H(1) && s.rng == rng);
    CHECK(s.distance == 470 && s.paces_since_fight == DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE);

    DigiviceState v = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    v.virux_type = DigiviceTypeIr;
    v.virux_expires = T0 + H(1);
    v.paces_since_fight = DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE;
    digivice_expedition_walk(&v, 600, T0 + 600);
    CHECK(v.enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE);
}

static void test_pool_index_range(void) {
    bool seen[2] = {false, false};
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.area = 7;
    s.distance = 1440;
    for(int i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
        s.enc_foe = DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE;
        s.paces_since_fight = DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE;
        digivice_expedition_walk(&s, 60, T0 + 60);
        CHECK(s.enc_foe <= 1);
        if(s.enc_foe <= 1) seen[s.enc_foe] = true;
    }
    CHECK(seen[0] && seen[1]);
}

static void test_encounter_expires_without_penalty(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.enc_foe = 0;
    s.enc_expires = T0 + DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_TTL_S;
    uint16_t losses = s.losses;
    uint8_t mood = s.mood;
    digivice_rules_tick(&s, T0 + DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_TTL_S - 1);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_trail_pending(&s, T0 + DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_TTL_S - 1));
    digivice_rules_tick(&s, T0 + DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_TTL_S);
    CHECK(s.enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE && s.enc_expires == 0);
    CHECK(s.losses == losses && s.mood == mood && s.corruption == 0);
}

static void test_offline_catch_up_leaves_no_stale_encounter(void) {
    for(uint32_t seed = 1; seed < 40; seed++) {
        DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
        s.area = 7;
        s.distance = 1440;
        s.rng = seed * 2654435761UL;
        digivice_rules_tick(&s, T0 + H(14));
        CHECK(s.enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE || s.enc_expires > T0 + H(14));
    }
}

static void test_virux_and_trail_never_coexist(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.mode = DigiviceModeHacker;
    s.enc_foe = 0;
    s.enc_expires = T0 + 600;
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_on_deed(&s, DolphinDeedNfcRead, 1, T0, 0));
    CHECK(s.virux_type == DigiviceTypeNone);
    s.enc_foe = DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE;
    s.enc_expires = 0;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_on_deed(&s, DolphinDeedNfcRead, 1, T0, 0));
    CHECK(s.virux_type == DigiviceTypeNfc);
}

static void test_hike_judge(void) {
    uint8_t beat = 0xFF;
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike_phase(500, &beat) == 0 && beat == 0);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike_phase(740, &beat) == 240 && beat == 0);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike_phase(760, &beat) == -240 && beat == 1);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike_phase(0, &beat) == -500 && beat == 0);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike_phase(13000, &beat) == 1000 && beat == 23);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike_tap_good(149) && digivice_expedition_hike_tap_good(-149));
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike_tap_good(150) && digivice_expedition_hike_tap_good(-150));
    CHECK(!digivice_expedition_hike_tap_good(151) && !digivice_expedition_hike_tap_good(-151));
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike_tap_good(0));
}

static void test_hike_cap_and_window(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.area = 7;
    s.distance = 1440;
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike_left(&s, T0) == DIGIVICE_HIKE_CAP);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike(&s, 24, T0) == 24);
    CHECK(s.bonus_window_start == T0 && s.distance == 1416);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike(&s, 24, T0 + 60) == 24);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike(&s, 24, T0 + 120) == 12);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike_left(&s, T0 + 120) == 0);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike(&s, 24, T0 + 3599) == 0);
    CHECK(s.distance == 1440 - 60);
    // The window rolls over exactly one hour after it opened.
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike_left(&s, T0 + 3600) == DIGIVICE_HIKE_CAP);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike(&s, 200, T0 + 3600) == DIGIVICE_HIKE_CAP);
    CHECK(s.bonus_window_start == T0 + 3600);

    // Asleep, sick or an Egg earns nothing.
    DigiviceState night = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike(&night, 10, DAY(10) + H(23) + 60) == 0);
    DigiviceState sick = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    sick.sick = 1;
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike(&sick, 10, T0) == 0);
    DigiviceState egg = with_form(DigiviceFormByteegg);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike(&egg, 10, T0) == 0);

    // The last paces of an area stop at the boss.
    DigiviceState end = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    end.distance = 5;
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike(&end, 24, T0) == 5);
    CHECK(end.distance == 0 && end.hike_count == 5);
}

static void test_scan_paces(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.mode = DigiviceModeHacker;
    s.area = 7;
    s.distance = 1440;
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_on_deed(&s, DolphinDeedNfcRead, 1, T0, 99));
    CHECK(s.distance == 1440 - DIGIVICE_SCAN_PACES && s.scan_count == DIGIVICE_SCAN_PACES);
    // A deed that does not scan grants no paces.
    digivice_rules_on_deed(&s, DolphinDeedIrSend, 1, T0, 99);
    CHECK(s.distance == 1440 - DIGIVICE_SCAN_PACES);
    for(int i = 0; i < 20; i++)
        digivice_rules_on_deed(&s, DolphinDeedSubGhzReceiverInfo, 1, T0 + 10, 99);
    CHECK(s.scan_count == DIGIVICE_SCAN_CAP && s.distance == 1440 - DIGIVICE_SCAN_CAP);

    // Scans and Hike share the window: the scan opened it, the Hike keeps it.
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_hike(&s, 10, T0 + 20) == 10);
    CHECK(s.bonus_window_start == T0);

    DigiviceState normal = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_on_deed(&normal, DolphinDeedNfcRead, 1, T0, 99));
    CHECK(normal.distance == 480 && normal.scan_count == 0);
}

static void test_midway(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.distance = 241;
    s.paces_since_fight = 0;
    s.rng = seed_with_pct(99);
    digivice_expedition_walk(&s, 60, T0 + 60);
    CHECK(s.distance == 240 && s.mid_state == 1);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_attention(&s, T0 + 60));

    // Spring: area 1.
    s.hunger = 1;
    s.mood = 0;
    s.corruption = 30;
    s.injured_until = T0 + 600;
    s.starving_since = 0;
    DigiviceMidwayResult r = digivice_expedition_midway_claim(&s, true, 0, T0 + 60);
    CHECK(r.kind == DigiviceMidwaySpring && r.ok);
    CHECK(s.hunger == DIGIVICE_HUNGER_MAX && s.mood == DIGIVICE_MOOD_MAX);
    CHECK(s.corruption == 20 && s.injured_until == 0 && s.mid_state == 2);
    // Claimed once: it never comes back in this area.
    r = digivice_expedition_midway_claim(&s, true, 0, T0 + 60);
    CHECK(r.kind == DigiviceMidwayCount && !r.ok);
    s.distance = 100;
    digivice_expedition_walk(&s, 60, T0 + 120);
    CHECK(s.mid_state == 2);

    // Cache: area 2, a Shield, then EXP once one is held.
    DigiviceState c = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    c.area = 1;
    c.mid_state = 1;
    r = digivice_expedition_midway_claim(&c, true, 0, T0);
    CHECK(r.kind == DigiviceMidwayCache && r.ok && c.item == DigiviceItemShield);
    c.mid_state = 1;
    uint32_t exp = c.exp;
    r = digivice_expedition_midway_claim(&c, true, 0, T0);
    CHECK(!r.ok && c.exp == exp + DIGIVICE_CACHE_EXP && c.item == DigiviceItemShield);

    // Shortcut: area 3, length 720, a jump of 144.
    DigiviceState k = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    k.area = 2;
    k.mid_state = 1;
    k.distance = 360;
    r = digivice_expedition_midway_claim(&k, false, 0, T0);
    CHECK(r.kind == DigiviceMidwayShortcut && !r.ok && k.distance == 360 && k.mid_state == 2);
    k.mid_state = 1;
    r = digivice_expedition_midway_claim(&k, true, DIGIVICE_SHORTCUT_WIN_PCT - 1, T0);
    CHECK(r.ok && k.distance == 216);
    k.mid_state = 1;
    r = digivice_expedition_midway_claim(&k, true, DIGIVICE_SHORTCUT_WIN_PCT, T0);
    CHECK(!r.ok && k.distance == 360);
    k.mid_state = 1;
    k.distance = 700;
    digivice_expedition_midway_claim(&k, true, 99, T0);
    CHECK(k.distance == 720);
    k.mid_state = 1;
    k.distance = 100;
    digivice_expedition_midway_claim(&k, true, 0, T0);
    CHECK(k.distance == 0 && digivice_expedition_boss_ready(&k));
}

static void test_boss_gate_and_clear(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.distance = 1;
    s.mid_state = 2;
    digivice_expedition_walk(&s, 60, T0 + 60);
    CHECK(s.distance == 0 && digivice_expedition_boss_ready(&s));
    CHECK(!digivice_expedition_boss_gate_open(&s));
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_attention(&s, T0 + 60));
    s.form = DigiviceFormVirubyte;
    s.prev_form = DigiviceFormBytemon;
    CHECK(!digivice_expedition_boss_gate_open(&s));
    s.prev_form = DigiviceFormWavedrake;
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_boss_gate_open(&s));
    s.form = DigiviceFormWavedrake;
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_boss_gate_open(&s));
    CHECK(digivice_rules_attention(&s, T0 + 60));

    digivice_expedition_boss_won(&s);
    CHECK(s.cleared == 0x01 && s.area == 1 && s.distance == 600 && s.mid_state == 0);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_area_unlocked(&s, 1) && !digivice_expedition_area_unlocked(&s, 2));

    // A repeat clear of area 1 moves on to the lowest uncleared area, 2.
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_select_area(&s, 0));
    s.distance = 0;
    digivice_expedition_boss_won(&s);
    CHECK(s.cleared == 0x01 && s.area == 1);

    s.cleared = 0x7F;
    s.area = 7;
    s.distance = 0;
    digivice_expedition_boss_won(&s);
    CHECK(s.cleared == 0xFF && s.area == 7 && s.distance == 0);
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_all_clear(&s) && !digivice_expedition_boss_ready(&s));
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_attention(&s, T0));
}

static void test_select_area(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.distance = 100;
    s.mid_state = 2;
    CHECK(!digivice_expedition_select_area(&s, 1));
    CHECK(!digivice_expedition_select_area(&s, 8));
    CHECK(s.area == 0 && s.distance == 100);
    // The current area changes nothing.
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_select_area(&s, 0));
    CHECK(s.distance == 100 && s.mid_state == 2);
    s.cleared = 0x03;
    s.enc_foe = 1;
    s.enc_expires = T0 + 60;
    s.paces_since_fight = 20;
    CHECK(digivice_expedition_select_area(&s, 2));
    CHECK(s.area == 2 && s.distance == 720 && s.mid_state == 0);
    CHECK(s.enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE && s.paces_since_fight == 0);
}

static void test_streak_lost(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
    s.area = 3;
    s.distance = 0;
    s.mid_state = 2;
    digivice_expedition_streak_lost(&s);
    CHECK(s.distance == 0 && s.mid_state == 2);
    s.area = 4;
    s.paces_since_fight = 9;
    digivice_expedition_streak_lost(&s);
    CHECK(s.distance == 960 && s.mid_state == 0 && s.paces_since_fight == 0);
}

static void test_attention(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_attention(&s, T0));
    s.enc_foe = 0;
    s.enc_expires = T0 + 10;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_attention(&s, T0));
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_attention(&s, T0 + 10));
    s.enc_foe = DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE;
    s.virux_type = DigiviceTypeUsb;
    s.virux_expires = T0 + 10;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_attention(&s, T0));
}

int main(void) {
    test_tables();
    test_one_pace_per_awake_minute();
    test_baby_walks_egg_and_sick_do_not();
    test_no_paces_asleep();
    test_one_hour_tick_matches_sixty_minute_ticks();
    test_48h_catch_up_is_clamped();
    test_exact_pace_instant();
    test_encounter_roll();
    test_guarantee_at_pace_50();
    test_no_roll_while_pending();
    test_pool_index_range();
    test_encounter_expires_without_penalty();
    test_offline_catch_up_leaves_no_stale_encounter();
    test_virux_and_trail_never_coexist();
    test_hike_judge();
    test_hike_cap_and_window();
    test_scan_paces();
    test_midway();
    test_boss_gate_and_clear();
    test_select_area();
    test_streak_lost();
    test_attention();

    if(failures) {
        printf("%d check(s) failed\n", failures);
        return EXIT_FAILURE;
    }
    printf("digivice_expedition: all checks passed\n");
    return EXIT_SUCCESS;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Build every rules test from one list**

Replace the whole content of `applications/services/digivice/test/run.sh` with exactly this content:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Builds and runs the host-side digivice tests with the system compiler.
set -e
here="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
services="$here/../.."
app="$services/../main/digivice"
out="${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"
flags="-std=c11 -Wall -Wextra -Werror -I $services"
rules="$here/../digivice_rules.c $here/../digivice_expedition.c"

for name in rules expedition; do
    # $rules is split on purpose: one argument per source file.
    cc $flags "$here/digivice_${name}_test.c" $rules -o "$out/digivice_${name}_test"
    "$out/digivice_${name}_test"
done

cc $flags "$here/digivice_i18n_test.c" "$here/../digivice_i18n.c" -o "$out/digivice_i18n_test"
# Every digivice source must stay ASCII-only: accents live in digivice_i18n.c as escapes.
"$out/digivice_i18n_test" "$here"/../*.c "$here"/../*.h "$here"/*.c "$app"/*.c "$app"/*.h \
    "$services/desktop/views/desktop_view_digivice.c"
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create the Expedition header**

Create `applications/services/digivice/digivice_expedition.h` with exactly this content:

```c
#pragma once

// Pure Expedition rules: no furi includes, so the host test can compile this file with plain cc.

#include "digivice_rules.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

/** The eight trail monsters. Proper nouns: never translated. */
typedef enum {
    DigiviceFoeEstatik,
    DigiviceFoeChipko,
    DigiviceFoeKorno,
    DigiviceFoeMokobit,
    DigiviceFoeFuzzcan,
    DigiviceFoeVampixel,
    DigiviceFoeSilbapo,
    DigiviceFoeChatarro,
    DigiviceFoeCount,
} DigiviceFoe;

typedef enum {
    DigiviceMidwaySpring,
    DigiviceMidwayCache,
    DigiviceMidwayShortcut,
    DigiviceMidwayCount, /**< also "nothing was claimed" in DigiviceMidwayResult */
} DigiviceMidwayKind;

typedef struct {
    uint8_t kind; /**< DigiviceMidwayKind, DigiviceMidwayCount when nothing was available */
    bool ok; /**< Spring: always; Cache: a Shield (false: EXP); Shortcut: the gamble paid */
} DigiviceMidwayResult;

DigiviceFoe digivice_expedition_pool_foe(uint8_t area, uint8_t index);
DigiviceAttr digivice_expedition_foe_attr(DigiviceFoe foe);
/** FUZZCAN, VAMPIXEL, SILBAPO and CHATARRO are Champion-tier; the rest Rookie-tier. */
bool digivice_expedition_foe_champion(DigiviceFoe foe);
const char* digivice_expedition_foe_name(DigiviceFoe foe);
/** Trail foe power: area power x1.0 (Rookie-tier) or x1.3 (Champion-tier), floored. */
uint16_t digivice_expedition_foe_power(uint8_t area, DigiviceFoe foe);
uint16_t digivice_expedition_boss_power(uint8_t area);
DigiviceAttr digivice_expedition_boss_attr(uint8_t area);
const char* digivice_expedition_boss_name(uint8_t area);
DigiviceMidwayKind digivice_expedition_midway_kind(uint8_t area);
/** Areas 5 to 8: no retreat, and any loss restarts the area. */
bool digivice_expedition_streak_area(uint8_t area);
/** Area 1 is always open; area i needs boss i-1. */
bool digivice_expedition_area_unlocked(const DigiviceState* s, uint8_t area);

/** Paces count for an awake pet that is at least Baby, not sick and short of the boss. */
bool digivice_expedition_can_walk(const DigiviceState* s);
/** Called by the tick for every awake step that ends at t_end. */
void digivice_expedition_walk(DigiviceState* s, uint32_t step, uint32_t t_end);
/** Clears a trail encounter whose time ran out. No penalty. */
void digivice_expedition_expire(DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now);
bool digivice_expedition_trail_pending(const DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now);

/** Signed distance in ms from `ms` (since the session start) to the nearest beat. */
int32_t digivice_expedition_hike_phase(uint32_t ms, uint8_t* beat);
bool digivice_expedition_hike_tap_good(int32_t phase_ms);
/** Hike paces still available in the current hourly window. */
uint8_t digivice_expedition_hike_left(const DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now);
/** Grants up to `good_taps` paces within the hourly cap. Returns the paces granted. */
uint8_t digivice_expedition_hike(DigiviceState* s, uint8_t good_taps, uint32_t now);
/** Hacker scan deed: DIGIVICE_SCAN_PACES paces within the hourly cap. Returns the paces. */
uint8_t digivice_expedition_scan(DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now);

/** The boss waits at distance 0, except once every area is cleared. */
bool digivice_expedition_boss_ready(const DigiviceState* s);
bool digivice_expedition_all_clear(const DigiviceState* s);
/** Champion or above (Virubyte counts as its prev_form). */
bool digivice_expedition_boss_gate_open(const DigiviceState* s);
/** Marks the area cleared and moves to the lowest uncleared area. */
void digivice_expedition_boss_won(DigiviceState* s);
/** A lost fight in a streak area restarts it; areas 1 to 4 keep their progress. */
void digivice_expedition_streak_lost(DigiviceState* s);
/** False for a locked area. Switching areas restarts the new one from its start. */
bool digivice_expedition_select_area(DigiviceState* s, uint8_t area);
/** Claims the available midway event; `accept` only matters for the shortcut. */
DigiviceMidwayResult digivice_expedition_midway_claim(
    DigiviceState* s,
    bool accept,
    uint8_t rng_pct,
    uint32_t now);

/** Something waits for the player: an encounter, a VIRUX, a midway event or an open boss. */
bool digivice_rules_attention(const DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create the Expedition rules**

Create `applications/services/digivice/digivice_expedition.c` with exactly this content:

```c
#include "digivice_expedition.h"
#include "digivice_config.h"

static const uint8_t pools[DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT][2] = {
    {DigiviceFoeEstatik, DigiviceFoeKorno},
    {DigiviceFoeChipko, DigiviceFoeEstatik},
    {DigiviceFoeKorno, DigiviceFoeChipko},
    {DigiviceFoeMokobit, DigiviceFoeChipko},
    {DigiviceFoeFuzzcan, DigiviceFoeKorno},
    {DigiviceFoeVampixel, DigiviceFoeChipko},
    {DigiviceFoeSilbapo, DigiviceFoeMokobit},
    {DigiviceFoeChatarro, DigiviceFoeFuzzcan},
};

static const uint8_t foe_attrs[DigiviceFoeCount] = {
    [DigiviceFoeEstatik] = DigiviceAttrRuido,
    [DigiviceFoeChipko] = DigiviceAttrCifrado,
    [DigiviceFoeKorno] = DigiviceAttrPortador,
    [DigiviceFoeMokobit] = DigiviceAttrNulo,
    [DigiviceFoeFuzzcan] = DigiviceAttrRuido,
    [DigiviceFoeVampixel] = DigiviceAttrRuido,
    [DigiviceFoeSilbapo] = DigiviceAttrRuido,
    [DigiviceFoeChatarro] = DigiviceAttrNulo,
};

static const char* const foe_names[DigiviceFoeCount] = {
    "ESTATIK",
    "CHIPKO",
    "KORNO",
    "MOKOBIT",
    "FUZZCAN",
    "VAMPIXEL",
    "SILBAPO",
    "CHATARRO",
};

static const uint8_t boss_attrs[DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT] = {
    DigiviceAttrRuido,
    DigiviceAttrCifrado,
    DigiviceAttrPortador,
    DigiviceAttrCifrado,
    DigiviceAttrRuido,
    DigiviceAttrCifrado,
    DigiviceAttrRuido,
    DigiviceAttrNulo,
};

static const char* const boss_names[DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT] = {
    "CHISPAPO",
    "CABLEBOA",
    "MASTIRAM",
    "CRISTALOK",
    "GUSAFERRO",
    "CENTINELA",
    "DESBORDE",
    "NEGATIVO",
};

static const uint8_t midway_kinds[DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT] = {
    DigiviceMidwaySpring,
    DigiviceMidwayCache,
    DigiviceMidwayShortcut,
    DigiviceMidwaySpring,
    DigiviceMidwayCache,
    DigiviceMidwayShortcut,
    DigiviceMidwaySpring,
    DigiviceMidwayCache,
};

static const uint8_t area_powers[DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT] = DIGIVICE_AREA_POWER;

static uint8_t clamp_area(uint8_t area) {
    return area < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT ? area : DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT - 1;
}

DigiviceFoe digivice_expedition_pool_foe(uint8_t area, uint8_t index) {
    return (DigiviceFoe)pools[clamp_area(area)][index & 1];
}

DigiviceAttr digivice_expedition_foe_attr(DigiviceFoe foe) {
    return foe < DigiviceFoeCount ? (DigiviceAttr)foe_attrs[foe] : DigiviceAttrNulo;
}

bool digivice_expedition_foe_champion(DigiviceFoe foe) {
    return foe >= DigiviceFoeFuzzcan && foe < DigiviceFoeCount;
}

const char* digivice_expedition_foe_name(DigiviceFoe foe) {
    return foe < DigiviceFoeCount ? foe_names[foe] : "?";
}

uint16_t digivice_expedition_foe_power(uint8_t area, DigiviceFoe foe) {
    uint16_t power = area_powers[clamp_area(area)];
    if(digivice_expedition_foe_champion(foe)) power = power * DIGIVICE_CHAMP_FOE_X10 / 10;
    return power;
}

uint16_t digivice_expedition_boss_power(uint8_t area) {
    return area_powers[clamp_area(area)] * DIGIVICE_BOSS_X10 / 10;
}

DigiviceAttr digivice_expedition_boss_attr(uint8_t area) {
    return (DigiviceAttr)boss_attrs[clamp_area(area)];
}

const char* digivice_expedition_boss_name(uint8_t area) {
    return boss_names[clamp_area(area)];
}

DigiviceMidwayKind digivice_expedition_midway_kind(uint8_t area) {
    return (DigiviceMidwayKind)midway_kinds[clamp_area(area)];
}

bool digivice_expedition_streak_area(uint8_t area) {
    return area >= DIGIVICE_STREAK_FIRST_AREA;
}

bool digivice_expedition_area_unlocked(const DigiviceState* s, uint8_t area) {
    if(area >= DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT) return false;
    return area == 0 || (s->cleared & (1U << (area - 1)));
}

bool digivice_expedition_can_walk(const DigiviceState* s) {
    return digivice_rules_stage(s) >= DigiviceStageBaby && !s->sick && s->distance > 0;
}

bool digivice_expedition_trail_pending(const DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now) {
    return s->enc_foe != DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE && now < s->enc_expires;
}

void digivice_expedition_expire(DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now) {
    if(s->enc_foe != DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE && now >= s->enc_expires) {
        s->enc_foe = DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE;
        s->enc_expires = 0;
    }
}

// One pace at instant t: distance, midway, then the encounter roll.
static void pace(DigiviceState* s, uint32_t t) {
    digivice_expedition_expire(s, t);
    s->distance--;
    if(s->paces_since_fight < DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE) s->paces_since_fight++;
    if(s->mid_state == 0 && s->distance <= digivice_rules_area_length(s->area) / 2) {
        s->mid_state = 1;
    }
    if(s->distance == 0) {
        // The boss is ready: no more rolls, and no half-pace carried into the boss wait.
        s->pace_acc_s = 0;
        return;
    }
    if(s->enc_foe != DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE || digivice_rules_virux_active(s, t)) return;
    bool spawn = s->paces_since_fight >= DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_GUARANTEE ||
                 digivice_rules_rng(s) % 100 < DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_PCT;
    if(!spawn) return;
    s->enc_foe = (uint8_t)(digivice_rules_rng(s) % 2);
    s->enc_expires = t + DIGIVICE_ENCOUNTER_TTL_S;
    s->paces_since_fight = 0;
}

void digivice_expedition_walk(DigiviceState* s, uint32_t step, uint32_t t_end) {
    if(!digivice_expedition_can_walk(s)) return;
    uint32_t acc = s->pace_acc_s + step;
    s->pace_acc_s = acc % DIGIVICE_PACE_S;
    // Pace k happens when the carried awake seconds reach k minutes.
    for(uint32_t k = 1; k <= acc / DIGIVICE_PACE_S && s->distance > 0; k++) {
        pace(s, t_end - (acc - k * DIGIVICE_PACE_S));
    }
}

int32_t digivice_expedition_hike_phase(uint32_t ms, uint8_t* beat) {
    uint32_t n = (ms + DIGIVICE_HIKE_BEAT_MS / 2) / DIGIVICE_HIKE_BEAT_MS;
    if(n < 1) n = 1;
    if(n > DIGIVICE_HIKE_BEATS) n = DIGIVICE_HIKE_BEATS;
    if(beat) *beat = (uint8_t)(n - 1);
    return (int32_t)ms - (int32_t)(n * DIGIVICE_HIKE_BEAT_MS);
}

bool digivice_expedition_hike_tap_good(int32_t phase_ms) {
    return phase_ms >= -DIGIVICE_HIKE_GOOD_MS && phase_ms <= DIGIVICE_HIKE_GOOD_MS;
}

static bool window_expired(const DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now) {
    return s->bonus_window_start == 0 || now < s->bonus_window_start ||
           now - s->bonus_window_start >= DIGIVICE_BONUS_WINDOW_S;
}

static void bonus_window(DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now) {
    if(!window_expired(s, now)) return;
    s->bonus_window_start = now;
    s->hike_count = 0;
    s->scan_count = 0;
}

uint8_t digivice_expedition_hike_left(const DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now) {
    return window_expired(s, now) ? DIGIVICE_HIKE_CAP : DIGIVICE_HIKE_CAP - s->hike_count;
}

static uint8_t bonus_paces(DigiviceState* s, uint8_t want, uint32_t now) {
    uint8_t granted = 0;
    while(granted < want && s->distance > 0) {
        pace(s, now);
        granted++;
    }
    return granted;
}

uint8_t digivice_expedition_hike(DigiviceState* s, uint8_t good_taps, uint32_t now) {
    if(digivice_rules_is_asleep(now) || !digivice_expedition_can_walk(s)) return 0;
    bonus_window(s, now);
    uint8_t room = DIGIVICE_HIKE_CAP - s->hike_count;
    uint8_t granted = bonus_paces(s, good_taps < room ? good_taps : room, now);
    s->hike_count += granted;
    return granted;
}

uint8_t digivice_expedition_scan(DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now) {
    if(!digivice_expedition_can_walk(s)) return 0;
    bonus_window(s, now);
    uint8_t room = DIGIVICE_SCAN_CAP - s->scan_count;
    uint8_t granted =
        bonus_paces(s, DIGIVICE_SCAN_PACES < room ? DIGIVICE_SCAN_PACES : room, now);
    s->scan_count += granted;
    return granted;
}

bool digivice_expedition_all_clear(const DigiviceState* s) {
    return s->cleared == (1U << DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT) - 1 && s->distance == 0;
}

bool digivice_expedition_boss_ready(const DigiviceState* s) {
    return s->distance == 0 && !digivice_expedition_all_clear(s);
}

bool digivice_expedition_boss_gate_open(const DigiviceState* s) {
    return digivice_rules_stage(s) >= DigiviceStageChampion;
}

static void start_area(DigiviceState* s, uint8_t area) {
    s->area = area;
    s->distance = digivice_rules_area_length(area);
    s->mid_state = 0;
    s->paces_since_fight = 0;
    s->enc_foe = DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE;
    s->enc_expires = 0;
}

void digivice_expedition_boss_won(DigiviceState* s) {
    s->cleared |= (uint8_t)(1U << clamp_area(s->area));
    for(uint8_t a = 0; a < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT; a++) {
        if(!(s->cleared & (1U << a))) {
            start_area(s, a);
            return;
        }
    }
    s->area = DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT - 1;
    s->distance = 0;
}

void digivice_expedition_streak_lost(DigiviceState* s) {
    if(!digivice_expedition_streak_area(s->area)) return;
    s->distance = digivice_rules_area_length(s->area);
    s->mid_state = 0;
    s->paces_since_fight = 0;
}

bool digivice_expedition_select_area(DigiviceState* s, uint8_t area) {
    if(!digivice_expedition_area_unlocked(s, area)) return false;
    if(area != s->area) start_area(s, area);
    return true;
}

DigiviceMidwayResult digivice_expedition_midway_claim(
    DigiviceState* s,
    bool accept,
    uint8_t rng_pct,
    uint32_t now) {
    DigiviceMidwayResult result = {.kind = DigiviceMidwayCount, .ok = false};
    if(s->mid_state != 1) return result;
    s->mid_state = 2;
    result.kind = digivice_expedition_midway_kind(s->area);
    uint16_t length = digivice_rules_area_length(s->area);
    uint16_t jump = length / 5;
    switch(result.kind) {
    case DigiviceMidwaySpring:
        s->hunger = DIGIVICE_HUNGER_MAX;
        s->starving_since = 0;
        s->mood = DIGIVICE_MOOD_MAX;
        digivice_rules_add_corruption(s, -DIGIVICE_SPRING_CORRUPTION_DROP, now);
        s->injured_until = 0;
        result.ok = true;
        break;
    case DigiviceMidwayCache:
        if(s->item == DigiviceItemNone) {
            s->item = DigiviceItemShield;
            result.ok = true;
        } else {
            s->exp += DIGIVICE_CACHE_EXP;
        }
        break;
    case DigiviceMidwayShortcut:
        if(!accept) break;
        if(rng_pct < DIGIVICE_SHORTCUT_WIN_PCT) {
            s->distance = s->distance > jump ? s->distance - jump : 0;
            if(s->distance == 0) s->pace_acc_s = 0;
            result.ok = true;
        } else {
            s->distance = s->distance + jump < length ? s->distance + jump : length;
        }
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
    return result;
}

bool digivice_rules_attention(const DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now) {
    return digivice_expedition_trail_pending(s, now) || digivice_rules_virux_active(s, now) ||
           s->mid_state == 1 ||
           (digivice_expedition_boss_ready(s) && digivice_expedition_boss_gate_open(s));
}
```

```c
// Two red blinks with a buzz each: a trail encounter spawned or the boss is ready.
const NotificationSequence digivice_sequence_encounter = {
    &message_red_255,
    &message_vibro_on,
    &message_delay_100,

    &message_red_0,

// Red and a falling two-note phrase: the battle was lost.
const NotificationSequence digivice_sequence_lose = {
    &message_red_255,
    &message_note_e4,
    &message_delay_100,
    &message_note_c4,
    &message_delay_250,
    &message_sound_off,
    &message_red_0,
    NULL,
};
```

with:

```c
/** Thread safe, blocking. Replaces the pet with a fresh egg; language and mode stay. */
void digivice_reset(Digivice* digivice);

/** Thread safe, blocking. Grants Hike paces within the hourly cap; returns the paces granted.
 * Hike paces are bookkeeping, so the service saves them explicitly. */
uint8_t digivice_hike_finish(Digivice* digivice, uint8_t good_taps);

/** Thread safe, blocking. False (and nothing changes) when the area is locked. */
bool digivice_select_area(Digivice* digivice, uint8_t area);

/** Thread safe, blocking. Claims the available midway event; `accept` answers the shortcut. */
DigiviceMidwayResult digivice_midway_claim(Digivice* digivice, bool accept);

/** Thread safe, never blocks: if the queue is full the cutscene simply plays again later. */
void digivice_ack_evolution(Digivice* digivice);
```

In `applications/services/digivice/digivice_srv.c`, replace:

```c
    digivice_send_wait(digivice, &message);
}

void digivice_ack_evolution(Digivice* digivice) {

```

with:

```c
    digivice_send_wait(digivice, &message);
}

uint8_t digivice_hike_finish(Digivice* digivice, uint8_t good_taps) {
    furi_check(digivice);
    uint8_t granted = 0;
    DigiviceMessage message = {
        .type = DigiviceMessageTypeHike,
        .output = &granted,
        .value = good_taps,
    };
    digivice_send_wait(digivice, &message);
    return granted;
}

bool digivice_select_area(Digivice* digivice, uint8_t area) {
    furi_check(digivice);
    bool selected = false;
    DigiviceMessage message = {
        .type = DigiviceMessageTypeSelectArea,
        .result = &selected,
        .value = area,
    };
    digivice_send_wait(digivice, &message);
    return selected;
}

DigiviceMidwayResult digivice_midway_claim(Digivice* digivice, bool accept) {
    furi_check(digivice);
    DigiviceMidwayResult result = {.kind = DigiviceMidwayCount, .ok = false};
    DigiviceMessage message = {
        .type = DigiviceMessageTypeMidway,
        .output = &result,
        .success = accept,
    };
    digivice_send_wait(digivice, &message);
    return result;
}

void digivice_ack_evolution(Digivice* digivice) {
    furi_check(digivice);
    DigiviceMessage message = {.type = DigiviceMessageTypeAckEvolution};
```

```c
/** A trail encounter spawned (or was replaced), or the walk just reached the boss. */
static bool digivice_encounter_appeared(const DigiviceState* before, const DigiviceState* after) {
    bool spawned = after->enc_foe != DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE &&
                   (before->enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE ||
                    after->enc_expires != before->enc_expires);
    return spawned || (after->distance == 0 && before->distance > 0);
}

/** Runs `before` vs current state bookkeeping after any mutation done under the mutex. */
static void digivice_changed(Digivice* digivice, const DigiviceState* before) {
    DigiviceState after;
```

Then replace:

```c
    case DigiviceMessageTypeAckEvolution:
        digivice_rules_ack_evolution(state);
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
    furi_mutex_release(digivice->mutex);

    if(spawned) notification_message(digivice->notification, &digivice_sequence_virux);
```

with:

```c
    case DigiviceMessageTypeAckEvolution:
        digivice_rules_ack_evolution(state);
        break;
    case DigiviceMessageTypeHike:
        *(uint8_t*)message.output = digivice_expedition_hike(state, message.value, now);
        // Hike paces only move bookkeeping fields, which digivice_changed would not save.
        bookkeeping = true;
        break;
    case DigiviceMessageTypeSelectArea:
        *message.result = digivice_expedition_select_area(state, message.value);
        break;
    case DigiviceMessageTypeMidway:
        *(DigiviceMidwayResult*)message.output = digivice_expedition_midway_claim(
            state, message.success, digivice_rules_rng_pct(state), now);
        digivice_rules_evolve(state, now);
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
    furi_mutex_release(digivice->mutex);

    if(spawned) notification_message(digivice->notification, &digivice_sequence_virux);
    if(bookkeeping) {
        digivice->dirty = true;
        furi_event_loop_timer_start(digivice->flush_timer, DIGIVICE_FLUSH_S * 1000UL);
    }
    digivice_changed(digivice, &before);

    if(message.flag) furi_event_flag_set(message.flag, DIGIVICE_DONE_FLAG);
```

- [ ] **Step 6: CI/device check (controller)**

The controller pushes and watches CI: both jobs green. Device check: leave a Baby or older pet awake and healthy with the Flipper on; within about an hour (roughly one encounter every 33 paces) the LED blinks red twice with two short buzzes. It never buzzes between 23:00 and 07:00.

**Files:**
- Modify: `applications/services/digivice/digivice_config.h` (projectile sizes)
- Modify: `applications/services/digivice/digivice_draw.h`
- Modify: `applications/services/digivice/digivice_draw.c` (clipped primitives, `digivice_attr_name`)
- Create: `applications/services/digivice/digivice_draw_world.c`
- Modify: `applications/services/digivice/application.fam`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DigiviceAttr` (Task 2), `DigiviceFoe`, `digivice_expedition_foe_champion` (Task 3), `DigiviceStrAttrPortador` (Task 5), the existing `digivice_draw_fill_ellipse`, `digivice_draw_fill_triangle`, `digivice_draw_ring`, `digivice_draw_form`, `digivice_draw_text`.
- Produces (used by Tasks 7, 9, 10, 11):
  - Config: `DIGIVICE_STAGE_SHOT {2, 2, 3, 5}`, `DIGIVICE_SHOT_ROOKIE_FOE 3`, `DIGIVICE_SHOT_CHAMP_FOE 5`, `DIGIVICE_SHOT_BOSS 7`, `DIGIVICE_SHOT_BEAM 9`.
  - `DigivicePoseHurt` appended to `DigivicePose`.
  - `void digivice_draw_line(Canvas*, int32_t x0, int32_t y0, int32_t x1, int32_t y1);` `void digivice_draw_box(Canvas*, int32_t x, int32_t y, int32_t w, int32_t h);` `void digivice_draw_dot(Canvas*, int32_t x, int32_t y);` (clipped)
  - `const char* digivice_attr_name(DigiviceAttr attr, DigiviceLang lang);`
  - `void digivice_draw_foe(Canvas*, DigiviceFoe foe, int32_t cx, int32_t foot_y, uint32_t frame, DigivicePose pose, bool flip, bool silhouette);`
  - `void digivice_draw_boss(Canvas*, uint8_t area, DigiviceForm pet, int32_t cx, int32_t foot_y, uint32_t frame, bool silhouette);`
  - `void digivice_draw_shot(Canvas*, DigiviceAttr attr, int32_t x, int32_t y, uint8_t size, int8_t dir, uint32_t frame);`
  - `digivice_draw_attr_icon`, `digivice_draw_burst`, `digivice_draw_star5`, `digivice_draw_shield` (5x9), `digivice_draw_exclam_bubble` (9x11), `digivice_draw_bandage` (7x7), `digivice_draw_invert`, `digivice_draw_charge_meter(Canvas*, x, y, zone_start, zone_w, cursor)`, `digivice_draw_ok_button(Canvas*, x, y, bool down)`, `digivice_draw_arrow(Canvas*, x, y, bool up, bool on)`, `digivice_draw_hazard(Canvas*, int32_t y, uint32_t frame)`.
