- [ ] **Step 4: Create the monsters, bosses, projectiles and effects**

The sprites are simplified draw-kit versions of the approved mockup designs (decision 18 of the spec): monsters are about 24x24 (Rookie-tier) and 32x32 (Champion-tier), bosses at most 56x48, all from ellipses, triangles, lines and boxes. A small pen mirrors offsets for `flip` and turns every white detail black for `silhouette`.

Create `applications/services/digivice/digivice_draw_world.c` with exactly this content:

```c
#include "digivice_draw.h"
#include "digivice_config.h"

// cos and sin of i * 45 deg, x64, for the 8-ray effects.
static const int8_t cos8[8] = {64, 45, 0, -45, -64, -45, 0, 45};
static const int8_t sin8[8] = {0, 45, 64, 45, 0, -45, -64, -45};

static uint32_t dw_hash(uint32_t x) {
    x ^= x >> 16;
    x *= 0x7feb352dUL;
    x ^= x >> 15;
    return x;
}

// Sprite pen: offsets from (cx, cy), mirrored with `flip`, all black with `sil`.
typedef struct {
    Canvas* canvas;
    int32_t cx;
    int32_t cy;
    bool flip;
    bool sil;
} DvPen;

static int32_t pen_x(const DvPen* p, int32_t dx) {
    return p->flip ? p->cx - dx : p->cx + dx;
}

static void pen_ink(const DvPen* p, bool white) {
    canvas_set_color(p->canvas, white && !p->sil ? ColorWhite : ColorBlack);
}

static void pen_ell(const DvPen* p, int32_t dx, int32_t dy, int32_t rx, int32_t ry) {
    digivice_draw_fill_ellipse(p->canvas, pen_x(p, dx), p->cy + dy, rx, ry);
}

static void pen_tri(
    const DvPen* p,
    int32_t ax,
    int32_t ay,
    int32_t bx,
    int32_t by,
    int32_t cx,
    int32_t cy) {
    digivice_draw_fill_triangle(
        p->canvas, pen_x(p, ax), p->cy + ay, pen_x(p, bx), p->cy + by, pen_x(p, cx), p->cy + cy);
}

static void pen_line(const DvPen* p, int32_t ax, int32_t ay, int32_t bx, int32_t by) {
    digivice_draw_line(p->canvas, pen_x(p, ax), p->cy + ay, pen_x(p, bx), p->cy + by);
}

static void pen_box(const DvPen* p, int32_t dx, int32_t dy, int32_t w, int32_t h) {
    int32_t x = p->flip ? p->cx - dx - w + 1 : p->cx + dx;
    digivice_draw_box(p->canvas, x, p->cy + dy, w, h);
}

static void pen_dot(const DvPen* p, int32_t dx, int32_t dy) {
    digivice_draw_dot(p->canvas, pen_x(p, dx), p->cy + dy);
}

// White 2x2 eye with a black pupil; an X while hurt.
static void pen_eye(const DvPen* p, int32_t dx, int32_t dy, DigivicePose pose) {
    if(p->sil) return;
    if(pose == DigivicePoseHurt) {
        pen_ink(p, true);
        pen_line(p, dx - 1, dy - 1, dx + 2, dy + 2);
        pen_line(p, dx - 1, dy + 2, dx + 2, dy - 1);
        return;
    }
    pen_ink(p, true);
    pen_box(p, dx, dy, 2, 2);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_dot(p, dx + 1, dy + 1);
}

static void foe_estatik(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame, DigivicePose pose) {
    pen_ink(p, false);
    // Static hair: three spikes that crackle on alternate frames.
    int32_t f = (int32_t)(frame % 2);
    pen_tri(p, -5, -3, -3, -12 + f, -1, -4);
    pen_tri(p, -1, -4, 1, -13 - f, 3, -4);
    pen_tri(p, 3, -4, 6, -11 + f, 6, -2);
    // Broken wings behind.
    pen_tri(p, -6, -1, -12, -7, -10, 2);
    pen_tri(p, -10, 2, -13, 4, -8, 4);
    pen_ell(p, 0, 2, 7, 7);
    pen_line(p, -6, 7, -11, 10);
    pen_tri(p, -13, 8, -10, 12, -14, 12);
    pen_box(p, -4, 8, 2, 4);
    pen_box(p, 2, 8, 2, 4);
    pen_eye(p, 2, -1, pose);
    pen_eye(p, 5, -1, pose);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    if(pose == DigivicePoseAttack) {
        pen_ell(p, 4, 5, 2, 1);
    } else {
        pen_line(p, 3, 5, 6, 5);
    }
    pen_ink(p, false);
}

static void foe_chipko(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame, DigivicePose pose) {
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_box(p, -8, -1, 16, 9);
    pen_box(p, 6, -5, 7, 7);
    pen_line(p, -8, 4, -12, 7);
    pen_line(p, -12, 7, -11, 10);
    for(int32_t x = -6; x <= 3; x += 3)
        pen_box(p, x, -3 - (int32_t)((frame / 3 + (uint32_t)(x + 6)) % 2), 2, 2);
    pen_box(p, -6, 8, 2, 4);
    pen_box(p, 3, 8, 2, 4);
    pen_eye(p, 9, -4, pose);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    pen_line(p, 8, 1, pose == DigivicePoseAttack ? 11 : 12, pose == DigivicePoseAttack ? 0 : 1);
    if(!p->sil) {
        // The chip's die on the back.
        pen_box(p, -3, 1, 6, 4);
        pen_ink(p, false);
        pen_box(p, -2, 2, 4, 2);
    }
    pen_ink(p, false);
}

static void foe_korno(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame, DigivicePose pose) {
    pen_ink(p, false);
    int32_t wag = (int32_t)(frame % 4 < 2);
    pen_line(p, -9, 2, -12, -1 - wag);
    pen_ell(p, -1, 4, 8, 6);
    pen_ell(p, 5, -3, 6, 5);
    pen_tri(p, 6, -7, 8, -14, 9, -6);
    pen_tri(p, 1, -6, -2, 0, 2, -2);
    pen_box(p, -6, 8, 2, 4);
    pen_box(p, 3, 8, 2, 4);
    pen_eye(p, 7, -5, pose);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    // The wave on its chest.
    pen_line(p, 0, 5, 1, 3);
    pen_line(p, 1, 3, 2, 5);
    pen_line(p, 2, 5, 3, 3);
    if(pose == DigivicePoseAttack) pen_ell(p, 9, 0, 2, 1);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_dot(p, 11, -3);
}

static void foe_mokobit(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame, DigivicePose pose) {
    int32_t squash = (int32_t)(frame % 6 < 3);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_ell(p, 0, 3 + squash, 10, 9 - squash);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    pen_ell(p, 0, 3 + squash, 7, 6 - squash);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    if(pose == DigivicePoseHurt) {
        pen_line(p, -4, 0, -1, 3);
        pen_line(p, 2, 0, 5, 3);
    } else {
        pen_box(p, -3, 0, 2, 3);
        pen_box(p, 2, 0, 2, 3);
    }
    if(pose == DigivicePoseAttack) pen_ell(p, 0, 6, 2, 1);
    // Bubbles rising off its back.
    int32_t rise = (int32_t)(frame % 8);
    if(!p->sil) {
        digivice_draw_ring(p->canvas, pen_x(p, 8), p->cy - 8 - rise / 2, 2, 2);
        pen_dot(p, 11, -3 - rise / 3);
    }
}

static void foe_fuzzcan(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame, DigivicePose pose) {
    pen_ink(p, false);
    for(int32_t x = -12; x <= 4; x += 4)
        pen_tri(p, x, -1, x + 2, -7 - (int32_t)((frame + (uint32_t)x) % 2), x + 4, -1);
    pen_ell(p, -2, 4, 12, 7);
    pen_ell(p, 9, -4, 7, 6);
    pen_box(p, 13, -3, 5, 4);
    pen_tri(p, 6, -8, 9, -15, 11, -8);
    pen_tri(p, 9, -15, 13, -13, 11, -11);
    pen_box(p, -11, 9, 3, 7);
    pen_box(p, -5, 10, 3, 6);
    pen_box(p, 3, 10, 3, 6);
    pen_box(p, 8, 9, 3, 7);
    pen_line(p, -14, 2, -18, -2);
    pen_eye(p, 9, -6, pose);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    if(pose == DigivicePoseAttack) {
        pen_box(p, 13, 0, 5, 2);
    }
    if(!p->sil) {
        // TV noise crawling over the fur.
        for(uint32_t i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            uint32_t h = dw_hash(frame / 2 * 7 + i);
            pen_dot(p, -10 + (int32_t)(h % 18), 1 + (int32_t)((h >> 8) % 7));
        }
    }
    pen_ink(p, false);
}

static void foe_vampixel(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame, DigivicePose pose) {
    int32_t flap = (int32_t)(frame % 4 < 2) * 4;
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_tri(p, -3, -4, -16, -10 + flap, -12, 6);
    pen_tri(p, 3, -4, 16, -10 + flap, 12, 6);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    pen_tri(p, -12, 6, -10, 2, -8, 6);
    pen_tri(p, 12, 6, 10, 2, 8, 6);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_ell(p, 0, 0, 6, 7);
    pen_tri(p, -3, -6, -2, -13, 0, -6);
    pen_tri(p, 1, -6, 3, -13, 4, -6);
    pen_line(p, -2, 7, -3, 16);
    pen_line(p, 2, 7, 3, 16);
    pen_eye(p, -2, -3, pose);
    pen_eye(p, 2, -3, pose);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    pen_line(p, 1, 3, 1, pose == DigivicePoseAttack ? 6 : 4);
    pen_line(p, 3, 3, 3, pose == DigivicePoseAttack ? 6 : 4);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    // It glitches: one sliced row every few frames.
    if(!p->sil && frame % 8 == 0) {
        digivice_draw_invert(p->canvas, p->cx - 16, p->cy - 2 + (int32_t)(frame % 5), 32, 2);
    }
}

static void foe_silbapo(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame, DigivicePose pose) {
    int32_t sac = (int32_t)(frame % 10 < 5 ? frame % 5 : 4 - frame % 5);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_ell(p, -10, 14, 4, 2);
    pen_ell(p, 8, 14, 4, 2);
    pen_ell(p, 0, 6, 14, 9);
    pen_ell(p, 4, -3, 3, 3);
    pen_ell(p, 10, -2, 3, 3);
    pen_eye(p, 4, -4, pose);
    pen_eye(p, 10, -3, pose);
    pen_ell(p, 10, 9, 3 + sac, 2 + sac / 2);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    pen_ell(p, 10, 9, 1 + sac, sac / 2);
    pen_dot(p, -6, 4);
    pen_dot(p, -2, 8);
    pen_dot(p, -9, 9);
    pen_dot(p, 2, 2);
    if(pose == DigivicePoseAttack) pen_line(p, 6, 4, 13, 4);
    pen_ink(p, false);
}

static void foe_chatarro(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame, DigivicePose pose) {
    int32_t sway = (int32_t)(frame % 6 < 3);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_box(p, -8, -6, 16, 16);
    pen_box(p, -5, -14, 10, 8);
    pen_line(p, -8, -4, -14, 5 + sway);
    pen_line(p, -14, 5 + sway, -16, 9 + sway);
    pen_line(p, -14, 5 + sway, -12, 9 + sway);
    pen_box(p, 8, -4 + sway, 5, 12);
    pen_box(p, -6, 10, 4, 6);
    pen_box(p, 2, 10, 4, 6);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    pen_box(p, -6, -4, 12, 12);
    pen_box(p, -3, -11, 6, 2);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    if(pose == DigivicePoseHurt) {
        pen_line(p, -3, -12, 2, -10);
    } else {
        pen_dot(p, pose == DigivicePoseAttack ? 2 : 1, -11);
    }
    pen_dot(p, -3, -1);
    pen_dot(p, 3, 5);
    pen_dot(p, 10, 0 + sway);
}

void digivice_draw_foe(
    Canvas* canvas,
    DigiviceFoe foe,
    int32_t cx,
    int32_t foot_y,
    uint32_t frame,
    DigivicePose pose,
    bool flip,
    bool silhouette) {
    bool big = digivice_expedition_foe_champion(foe);
    int32_t bob = pose == DigivicePoseIdle && (frame / 3) % 2 ? 1 : 0;
    int32_t back = pose == DigivicePoseHurt ? 2 : 0;
    DvPen p = {
        .canvas = canvas,
        .cx = flip ? cx + back : cx - back,
        .cy = foot_y - (big ? 16 : 12) + bob,
        .flip = flip,
        .sil = silhouette,
    };
    switch(foe) {
    case DigiviceFoeEstatik:
        foe_estatik(&p, frame, pose);
        break;
    case DigiviceFoeChipko:
        foe_chipko(&p, frame, pose);
        break;
    case DigiviceFoeKorno:
        foe_korno(&p, frame, pose);
        break;
    case DigiviceFoeMokobit:
        foe_mokobit(&p, frame, pose);
        break;
    case DigiviceFoeFuzzcan:
        foe_fuzzcan(&p, frame, pose);
        break;
    case DigiviceFoeVampixel:
        foe_vampixel(&p, frame, pose);
        break;
    case DigiviceFoeSilbapo:
        foe_silbapo(&p, frame, pose);
        break;
    case DigiviceFoeChatarro:
        foe_chatarro(&p, frame, pose);
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
}

// Bosses face left, towards the pet. The pen origin is the centre of their feet.

static void boss_chispapo(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame) {
    int32_t puff = (int32_t)(frame % 8 < 4);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_ell(p, -18, -3, 8, 3);
    pen_ell(p, 18, -3, 8, 3);
    pen_ell(p, 0, -16, 26, 14 + puff);
    pen_ell(p, -10, -30, 6, 6);
    pen_ell(p, 10, -30, 6, 6);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    pen_box(p, -11, -33, 2, 6);
    pen_box(p, 9, -33, 2, 6);
    if((frame / 5) % 3 == 0) {
        pen_ell(p, 0, -14, 18, 3);
    } else {
        pen_line(p, -16, -15, 16, -15);
    }
    for(int32_t y = -8; y < -2; y += 3)
        pen_line(p, -10, y, 10, y);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    if(p->sil) return;
    // Crackle: four short lightning forks off the body.
    for(uint32_t i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        uint32_t h = dw_hash(frame * 4 + i);
        int32_t a = (int32_t)(h % 8);
        int32_t x = cos8[a] * 27 / 64;
        int32_t y = -16 + sin8[a] * 16 / 64;
        pen_line(p, x, y, x + cos8[a] / 12 + (int32_t)((h >> 8) % 5) - 2, y + sin8[a] / 16);
    }
}

static void boss_cableboa(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame) {
    int32_t sway = (int32_t)(frame % 8 < 4) * 2;
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_ell(p, 0, -5, 26, 5);
    pen_ell(p, 2, -13, 21, 5);
    pen_ell(p, 4, -21, 17, 4);
    for(int32_t i = 0; i < 6; i++)
        pen_ell(p, 10 - i * 4 + (i % 2) * sway, -27 - i * 3, 4, 3);
    pen_box(p, -26 + sway, -48, 18, 11);
    pen_box(p, -31 + sway, -46, 6, 2);
    pen_box(p, -31 + sway, -41, 6, 2);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    for(int32_t x = -20; x < 22; x += 6) {
        pen_line(p, x, -8, x + 2, -2);
        pen_line(p, x + 2, -16, x + 4, -10);
    }
    pen_box(p, -23 + sway, -46, 12, 7);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_ell(p, -17 + sway, -43, 2, 2);
    if(!p->sil && frame % 2) {
        pen_line(p, -32 + sway, -45, -36 + sway, -48);
        pen_line(p, -32 + sway, -40, -37 + sway, -37);
    }
}

static void boss_mastiram(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame) {
    pen_ink(p, false);
    for(int32_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        int32_t x = -6 + i * 10;
        int32_t top = -44 + i * 3;
        pen_line(p, x, top, x - 3, -22);
        pen_line(p, x, top, x + 3, -22);
        for(int32_t y = top + 4; y < -22; y += 5) {
            pen_line(p, x - 2, y, x + 2, y + 4);
            pen_line(p, x + 2, y, x - 2, y + 4);
        }
        if((frame + (uint32_t)i * 3) % 6 < 3) pen_ell(p, x, top - 2, 1, 1);
        if(!p->sil && (frame + (uint32_t)i) % 6 == 0) {
            digivice_draw_ring(p->canvas, pen_x(p, x), p->cy + top - 2, 4, 2);
        }
    }
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_ell(p, 2, -16, 22, 10);
    for(int32_t x = -14; x <= 16; x += 10)
        pen_box(p, x, -9, 4, 9);
    pen_ell(p, -20, -20, 8, 8);
    pen_ell(p, -27, -16, 5, 4);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    pen_ell(p, -18, -22, 5, 5);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_ell(p, -18, -22, 3, 3);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    pen_ell(p, -18, -22, 1, 1);
    pen_dot(p, -26, -20);
    pen_ink(p, false);
}

static void boss_cristalok(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame) {
    int32_t lift = (int32_t)(frame % 8 < 4);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    for(int32_t x = -16; x <= 16; x += 11) {
        pen_line(p, x, -10, x - 5, 0);
        pen_line(p, x + 1, -10, x - 4, 0);
    }
    pen_ell(p, 2, -16 - lift, 24, 12);
    pen_ell(p, -22, -14 - lift, 7, 6);
    pen_line(p, -28, -16 - lift, -33, -20 - lift);
    pen_line(p, -28, -12 - lift, -33, -8 - lift);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    // Facets.
    pen_line(p, -18, -18 - lift, -6, -26 - lift);
    pen_line(p, -6, -26 - lift, 8, -26 - lift);
    pen_line(p, 8, -26 - lift, 22, -18 - lift);
    pen_line(p, -6, -26 - lift, 2, -12 - lift);
    pen_line(p, 8, -26 - lift, 2, -12 - lift);
    pen_line(p, -18, -10 - lift, 22, -10 - lift);
    pen_ell(p, 2, -19 - lift, 4, 4);
    pen_box(p, -25, -16 - lift, 2, 2);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    int32_t a = (int32_t)(frame % 8);
    pen_line(p, 2, -19 - lift, 2 + cos8[a] * 3 / 64, -19 - lift + sin8[a] * 3 / 64);
}

static void boss_gusaferro(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame) {
    pen_ink(p, false);
    int32_t hx = 0;
    int32_t hy = 0;
    for(int32_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        int32_t wob = (int32_t)(((frame / 2 + (uint32_t)i) % 4 < 2) ? 2 : -2);
        int32_t x = 12 - i * 3 + wob;
        int32_t y = -6 - i * 5;
        int32_t r = 10 - i;
        pen_ell(p, x, y, r, r * 4 / 5);
        if(!p->sil) {
            pen_line(p, x - r - 2, y, x - r, y);
            pen_line(p, x + r, y - 1, x + r + 2, y - 2);
        }
        hx = x;
        hy = y;
    }
    pen_ell(p, hx - 6, hy - 2, 8, 8);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    pen_ell(p, hx - 8, hy - 2, 5, 6);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    for(int32_t t = 0; t < 8; t += 2) {
        int32_t a = (int32_t)((frame + (uint32_t)t) % 8);
        pen_tri(
            p,
            hx - 8 + cos8[a] * 5 / 64,
            hy - 2 + sin8[a] * 6 / 64,
            hx - 8 + cos8[(a + 1) % 8] * 5 / 64,
            hy - 2 + sin8[(a + 1) % 8] * 6 / 64,
            hx - 8,
            hy - 2);
    }
    if(!p->sil) {
        for(uint32_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            uint32_t h = dw_hash(frame / 2 * 8 + i);
            pen_dot(p, -24 + (int32_t)(h % 48), -(int32_t)((h >> 8) % 3));
        }
    }
}

static void boss_centinela(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame) {
    bool on = (frame / 3) % 2 == 0;
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_tri(p, -20, 0, 20, 0, 0, -34);
    pen_box(p, -8, -36, 16, 6);
    pen_ell(p, 0, -41, 7, 7);
    for(int32_t sd = -1; sd <= 1; sd += 2) {
        int32_t sway = (int32_t)(frame % 8 < 4);
        pen_line(p, sd * 6, -26, sd * 22, -22 + sway);
        pen_line(p, sd * 6, -25, sd * 22, -21 + sway);
        pen_line(p, sd * 22, -22 + sway, sd * 26, -27 + sway);
        pen_line(p, sd * 22, -22 + sway, sd * 27, -18 + sway);
    }
    pen_ink(p, true);
    for(int32_t y = -26; y < 0; y += 6) {
        int32_t w = (y + 34) * 20 / 34;
        pen_line(p, -w + 3, y, w - 3, y);
        pen_line(p, -w + 3, y, w - 6, y + 6);
    }
    if(on) {
        pen_ell(p, 0, -41, 5, 5);
        pen_ink(p, false);
        pen_ell(p, 0, -41, 2, 2);
        if(!p->sil) {
            for(int32_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
                pen_line(
                    p,
                    cos8[i] * 10 / 64,
                    -41 + sin8[i] * 9 / 64,
                    cos8[i] * 14 / 64,
                    -41 + sin8[i] * 12 / 64);
            }
        }
    } else {
        pen_ell(p, 0, -41, 5, 5);
        pen_ink(p, false);
        pen_ell(p, -2, -41, 2, 2);
    }
    pen_ink(p, false);
}

static void boss_desborde(const DvPen* p, uint32_t frame) {
    int32_t o = (int32_t)(frame % 8 < 4) * 2;
    pen_ink(p, false);
    for(int32_t i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        pen_tri(p, -6 + i * 8, -32 + o, 2 + i * 8, -32 + o, i * 8, -44 + o + i * 2);
    pen_ell(p, 4, -20 + o, 26, 13);
    pen_tri(p, 26, -22 + o, 30, -36 + o, 34, -14 + o);
    pen_ell(p, -20, -18 + o, 9, 7);
    pen_ink(p, true);
    for(int32_t x = -27; x < -14; x += 3) {
        pen_tri(p, x, -22 + o, x + 2, -22 + o, x + 1, -19 + o);
        pen_tri(p, x, -14 + o, x + 2, -14 + o, x + 1, -17 + o);
    }
    pen_ell(p, -8, -26 + o, 3, 2);
    pen_line(p, 6, -16 + o, 20, -16 + o);
    pen_line(p, 4, -12 + o, 22, -12 + o);
    pen_ink(p, false);
    pen_dot(p, -8, -26 + o);
    // The wave it rides; bits spill from its mouth.
    for(int32_t x = -28; x <= 28; x += 2)
        pen_dot(p, x, -1 - (int32_t)(((uint32_t)(x + 28) / 2 + frame) % 3));
    if(!p->sil) {
        for(int32_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            int32_t y = -16 + (int32_t)((frame * 2 + (uint32_t)i * 5) % 14);
            pen_dot(p, -26 - i * 2, y);
        }
    }
}

static void boss_negativo(
    Canvas* canvas,
    DigiviceForm pet,
    int32_t cx,
    int32_t foot_y,
    uint32_t frame,
    bool sil) {
    // The player's own pet in negative: its silhouette punched out of a black field.
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_form(canvas, pet, cx, foot_y - 22, frame, DigivicePoseAttack, true);
    if(sil) return;
    digivice_draw_invert(canvas, cx - 24, foot_y - 46, 48, 46);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    for(uint32_t i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        uint32_t h = dw_hash(frame / 2 * 10 + i);
        int32_t dx = (int32_t)(h % 44);
        int32_t dy = (int32_t)((h >> 8) % 42);
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, cx - 22 + dx, foot_y - 44 + dy);
    }
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
}

void digivice_draw_boss(
    Canvas* canvas,
    uint8_t area,
    DigiviceForm pet,
    int32_t cx,
    int32_t foot_y,
    uint32_t frame,
    bool silhouette) {
    DvPen p = {.canvas = canvas, .cx = cx, .cy = foot_y, .flip = false, .sil = silhouette};
    switch(area) {
    case 0:
        boss_chispapo(&p, frame);
        break;
    case 1:
        boss_cableboa(&p, frame);
        break;
    case 2:
        boss_mastiram(&p, frame);
        break;
    case 3:
        boss_cristalok(&p, frame);
        break;
    case 4:
        boss_gusaferro(&p, frame);
        break;
    case 5:
        boss_centinela(&p, frame);
        break;
    case 6:
        boss_desborde(&p, frame);
        break;
    default:
        boss_negativo(canvas, pet, cx, foot_y, frame, silhouette);
        break;
    }
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
}

void digivice_draw_shot(
    Canvas* canvas,
    DigiviceAttr attr,
    int32_t x,
    int32_t y,
    uint8_t size,
    int8_t dir,
    uint32_t frame) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    if(size >= DIGIVICE_SHOT_BEAM) {
        int32_t x0 = dir > 0 ? x - 8 : x;
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x0, y - 2, 9, 5);
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x0 + 1, y - 1, 7, 3);
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - dir * 4, y, 2, 1);
        for(int32_t i = 1; i < 4; i++) {
            int32_t h = 5 - 2 * i;
            digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - dir * (9 + i * 4), y - h / 2, 3, h < 1 ? 1 : h);
        }
        return;
    }
    if(size <= 2) {
        // The Baby's 2x2 pellet with one trail dot.
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - 1, y - 1, 2, 2);
        if(frame % 2) digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x - dir * 3, y);
        return;
    }
    int32_t r = size / 2;
    switch(attr) {
    case DigiviceAttrPortador:
        // Three concentric arcs: a ring wave.
        for(int32_t k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
            int32_t ax = x - dir * k * 3;
            int32_t h = r + k;
            digivice_draw_line(canvas, ax - dir, y - h, ax, y - h / 2);
            digivice_draw_line(canvas, ax, y - h / 2, ax, y + h / 2);
            digivice_draw_line(canvas, ax, y + h / 2, ax - dir, y + h);
        }
        break;
    case DigiviceAttrRuido: {
        // Jagged two-pixel bolt.
        int32_t px = x - dir * r * 3;
        int32_t py = y;
        for(int32_t i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            int32_t nx = px + dir * (r * 3 / 2 + 1);
            int32_t ny = y + (i % 2 ? r : -r);
            digivice_draw_line(canvas, px, py, nx, ny);
            digivice_draw_line(canvas, px, py + 1, nx, ny + 1);
            px = nx;
            py = ny;
        }
        break;
    }
    case DigiviceAttrCifrado:
        // Hollow packet with a blinking trail.
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - r, y - r, r * 2 + 1, r * 2 + 1);
        if(r > 1) {
            canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
            digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - r + 1, y - r + 1, r * 2 - 1, r * 2 - 1);
            canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
        }
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x, y);
        for(int32_t i = 1; i < 4; i++) {
            if((frame + (uint32_t)i) % 2) {
                digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x - dir * (r + i * 2), y + (i % 2 ? r : -r));
            }
        }
        break;
    default:
        // Nulo: a hollow circle.
        digivice_draw_ring(canvas, x, y, r + 1, r + 1);
        break;
    }
}

void digivice_draw_attr_icon(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceAttr attr, int32_t x, int32_t y) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    switch(attr) {
    case DigiviceAttrPortador:
        digivice_draw_ring(canvas, x + 3, y + 3, 3, 3);
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 1, y + 3);
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 2, y + 2);
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 3, y + 3);
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 4, y + 4);
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 5, y + 3);
        break;
    case DigiviceAttrRuido:
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 5, y, x + 1, y + 3);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 1, y + 3, x + 5, y + 3);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 5, y + 3, x + 1, y + 6);
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 6, y);
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x, y + 6);
        break;
    case DigiviceAttrCifrado:
        canvas_draw_frame(canvas, x, y, 7, 7);
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x + 2, y + 2, 3, 2);
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x + 3, y + 4, 1, 2);
        break;
    default:
        digivice_draw_ring(canvas, x + 3, y + 3, 3, 3);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 1, y + 5, x + 5, y + 1);
        break;
    }
}

void digivice_draw_burst(Canvas* canvas, int32_t cx, int32_t cy, int32_t r) {
    for(int32_t i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        digivice_draw_line(
            canvas,
            cx + cos8[i] * r * 45 / 6400,
            cy + sin8[i] * r * 45 / 6400,
            cx + cos8[i] * r / 64,
            cy + sin8[i] * r / 64);
    }
}

void digivice_draw_star5(Canvas* canvas, int32_t cx, int32_t cy) {
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, cx - 2, cy, 5, 1);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, cx, cy - 2, 1, 5);
    digivice_draw_dot(canvas, cx - 1, cy - 1);
    digivice_draw_dot(canvas, cx + 1, cy - 1);
    digivice_draw_dot(canvas, cx - 1, cy + 1);
    digivice_draw_dot(canvas, cx + 1, cy + 1);
}

void digivice_draw_shield(Canvas* canvas, int32_t x, int32_t y) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x, y, 5, 9);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, y, x + 4, y);
    digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, y, x, y + 7);
    digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 4, y, x + 4, y + 7);
    digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 1, y + 8, x + 3, y + 8);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x + 2, y + 2, 1, 5);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x + 1, y + 3, 3, 1);
}

void digivice_draw_exclam_bubble(Canvas* canvas, int32_t x, int32_t y) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x, y, 9, 11);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 1, y, x + 7, y);
    digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 1, y + 10, x + 7, y + 10);
    digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, y + 1, x, y + 9);
    digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 8, y + 1, x + 8, y + 9);
    // Tail towards the speaker, bottom left.
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x + 3, y + 10, 3, 1);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 3, y + 11);
    digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 4, y + 12);
    digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 5, y + 10);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x + 4, y + 2, 1, 5);
    digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 4, y + 8);
}

void digivice_draw_bandage(Canvas* canvas, int32_t x, int32_t y) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - 1, y + 1, 9, 5);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x + 1, y - 1, 5, 9);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x, y + 2, 7, 3);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x + 2, y, 3, 7);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 3, y + 3);
    digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 1, y + 3);
    digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 5, y + 3);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
}

void digivice_draw_invert(Canvas* canvas, int32_t x, int32_t y, int32_t w, int32_t h) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorXOR);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x, y, w, h);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
}

void digivice_draw_charge_meter(
    Canvas* canvas,
    int32_t x,
    int32_t y,
    uint8_t zone_start,
    uint8_t zone_w,
    uint8_t cursor) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    for(uint8_t i = 0; i < DIGIVICE_METER_SEGMENTS; i++) {
        int32_t sx = x + i * 5;
        if(i >= zone_start && i < zone_start + zone_w) {
            canvas_draw_frame(canvas, sx, y, 4, 5);
        } else {
            digivice_draw_box(canvas, sx, y + 3, 4, 2);
        }
    }
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, x + cursor * 5, y - 2, 4, 8);
}

void digivice_draw_ok_button(Canvas* canvas, int32_t x, int32_t y, bool down) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_fill_ellipse(canvas, x + 4, y + 4, 4, 4);
    if(!down) {
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
        digivice_draw_fill_ellipse(canvas, x + 4, y + 3, 3, 3);
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, x + 1, y + 1, "OK");
    } else {
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, x + 1, y + 2, "OK");
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    }
}

void digivice_draw_arrow(Canvas* canvas, int32_t x, int32_t y, bool up, bool on) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    if(up) {
        digivice_draw_fill_triangle(canvas, x, y + 4, x + 6, y + 4, x + 3, y);
    } else {
        digivice_draw_fill_triangle(canvas, x, y, x + 6, y, x + 3, y + 4);
    }
    if(on) return;
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    if(up) {
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 2, y + 3, x + 4, y + 3);
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 3, y + 2);
    } else {
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 2, y + 1, x + 4, y + 1);
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 3, y + 2);
    }
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
}

void digivice_draw_hazard(Canvas* canvas, int32_t y, uint32_t frame) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, 0, y, 128, 7);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    int32_t shift = (int32_t)((frame * 3) % 8);
    for(int32_t x = -8; x < 136; x += 8) {
        int32_t sx = x + shift;
        digivice_draw_fill_triangle(canvas, sx, y, sx + 4, y, sx - 3, y + 6);
        digivice_draw_fill_triangle(canvas, sx + 4, y, sx + 1, y + 6, sx - 3, y + 6);
    }
    digivice_draw_line(canvas, 0, y, 127, y);
    digivice_draw_line(canvas, 0, y + 6, 127, y + 6);
}
```

### Task 7: Draw kit: seeded area backgrounds with day, dusk and night

**Files:**
- Modify: `applications/services/digivice/digivice_draw.h`
- Create: `applications/services/digivice/digivice_draw_scene.c`
- Modify: `applications/services/digivice/application.fam`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Task 6): `digivice_draw_line`, `digivice_draw_box`, `digivice_draw_dot`, `digivice_draw_burst`, `digivice_draw_ring`, `digivice_draw_fill_ellipse`, `digivice_draw_text`; `DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT` (Task 2).
- Produces (used by Tasks 9 and 10):
  - `#define DIGIVICE_SCENE_W 192`, `#define DIGIVICE_SCENE_DECOS 12`
  - `typedef struct { uint32_t seed; uint8_t area; uint8_t far[192]; uint8_t near[192]; uint8_t deco_x[12]; uint8_t deco_v[12]; } DigiviceScene;` (about 420 bytes: heap only)
  - `void digivice_scene_init(DigiviceScene* scene, uint8_t area);` seed `0xD1610 ^ (area * 0x9E37)`, value noise every 16 px.
  - `void digivice_draw_scene(Canvas* canvas, const DigiviceScene* scene, uint32_t scroll, uint32_t frame, uint16_t day_minute);` (decision D13)

- [ ] **Step 1: Declare the scene API**

In `applications/services/digivice/digivice_draw.h`, replace:

```c
    int32_t top,
    DigiviceLang lang);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif
```

with:

```c
    int32_t top,
    DigiviceLang lang);

// digivice_draw_scene.c: seeded area backgrounds, cached once per area.

#define DIGIVICE_SCENE_W     192
#define DIGIVICE_SCENE_DECOS 12

/** About 420 bytes: allocate it on the app heap, never on a stack. */
typedef struct {
    uint32_t seed;
    uint8_t area;
    uint8_t far[DIGIVICE_SCENE_W]; /**< top row of the far layer per column */
    uint8_t near[DIGIVICE_SCENE_W]; /**< top row of the near layer per column */
    uint8_t deco_x[DIGIVICE_SCENE_DECOS]; /**< decoration columns, 0..DIGIVICE_SCENE_W-1 */
    uint8_t deco_v[DIGIVICE_SCENE_DECOS]; /**< decoration variant 0..3 */
} DigiviceScene;

/** Builds the height profiles and decorations from the area's constant seed. */
void digivice_scene_init(DigiviceScene* scene, uint8_t area);
/** Draws the area background from row 9 down. `scroll` moves the near layer 1 px per unit;
 * `day_minute` (0..1439, from the RTC) picks the day, dusk or night sky. */
void digivice_draw_scene(
    Canvas* canvas,
    const DigiviceScene* scene,
    uint32_t scroll,
    uint32_t frame,
    uint16_t day_minute);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Create the backgrounds**

Per frame the scene draws one call per layer per column (about 256 calls) plus at most 12 decorations, the ground line and a few dots. No per-pixel hash runs per frame: the profiles and decorations are generated once in `digivice_scene_init`, and the meadow's TV-snow and the stars reuse the cached seed.

Create `applications/services/digivice/digivice_draw_scene.c` with exactly this content:

```c
#include "digivice_draw.h"
#include "digivice_config.h"

#define SKY_TOP 9

typedef enum {
    DecoGrass,
    DecoTree,
    DecoMast,
    DecoIce,
    DecoMagnet,
    DecoTower,
    DecoPacket,
    DecoShard,
} DvDeco;

typedef enum {
    NearOutline,
    NearSolid,
    NearStripes,
} DvNear;

typedef struct {
    uint8_t far_base;
    uint8_t far_amp;
    uint8_t near_base;
    uint8_t near_amp;
    uint8_t far_step; /**< far layer: a fill line every far_step columns, dots between */
    uint8_t near; /**< DvNear */
    uint8_t ground; /**< ground line row */
    uint8_t deco; /**< DvDeco */
} DvLook;

// Ported from the mockup's bgMeadow ... bgNull, reduced to two cached profiles per area.
static const DvLook looks[DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT] = {
    {30, 4, 40, 3, 6, NearStripes, 47, DecoGrass},
    {26, 3, 41, 2, 6, NearOutline, 48, DecoTree},
    {28, 6, 43, 4, 3, NearSolid, 54, DecoMast},
    {30, 5, 40, 3, 6, NearOutline, 48, DecoIce},
    {30, 3, 38, 4, 6, NearStripes, 48, DecoMagnet},
    {40, 2, 46, 3, 6, NearSolid, 54, DecoTower},
    {28, 3, 52, 2, 3, NearStripes, 56, DecoPacket},
    {34, 4, 46, 3, 3, NearOutline, 50, DecoShard},
};

static uint32_t scene_rng(uint32_t* state) {
    uint32_t x = *state;
    x ^= x << 13;
    x ^= x >> 17;
    x ^= x << 5;
    *state = x;
    return x;
}

static void profile(uint8_t* out, uint8_t base, uint8_t amp, uint32_t* rng) {
    // Value noise: a control point every 16 columns, linear in between, wrapping at 192.
    uint8_t points[DIGIVICE_SCENE_W / 16];
    for(uint32_t i = 0; i < DIGIVICE_SCENE_W / 16; i++) {
        points[i] = (uint8_t)(base - amp + scene_rng(rng) % (2U * amp + 1U));
    }
    for(uint32_t x = 0; x < DIGIVICE_SCENE_W; x++) {
        int32_t a = points[x / 16];
        int32_t b = points[(x / 16 + 1) % (DIGIVICE_SCENE_W / 16)];
        out[x] = (uint8_t)(a + (b - a) * (int32_t)(x % 16) / 16);
    }
}

void digivice_scene_init(DigiviceScene* scene, uint8_t area) {
    if(area >= DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT) area = DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT - 1;
    const DvLook* look = &looks[area];
    scene->area = area;
    scene->seed = 0xD1610UL ^ ((uint32_t)area * 0x9E37UL);
    uint32_t rng = scene->seed;
    profile(scene->far, look->far_base, look->far_amp, &rng);
    profile(scene->near, look->near_base, look->near_amp, &rng);
    for(uint32_t i = 0; i < DIGIVICE_SCENE_DECOS; i++) {
        scene->deco_x[i] = (uint8_t)(i * 16 + scene_rng(&rng) % 12);
        scene->deco_v[i] = (uint8_t)(scene_rng(&rng) % 4);
    }
}

static void sky_day(Canvas* canvas, uint32_t scroll) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_ring(canvas, 110, 16, 4, 4);
    for(int32_t i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        int32_t x = (int32_t)(((uint32_t)i * 53 + 170 - scroll / 8 % 170) % 170) - 20;
        digivice_draw_ring(canvas, x, 13 + i * 4, 9, 3);
    }
}

static void sky_dusk(Canvas* canvas, uint8_t sun_row) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    for(int32_t y = SKY_TOP; y < SKY_TOP + 8; y += 2) {
        for(int32_t x = (y / 2) % 4; x < 128; x += 4)
            canvas_draw_dot(canvas, x, y);
    }
    digivice_draw_fill_ellipse(canvas, 100, sun_row, 5, 5);
}

static void sky_night(Canvas* canvas, const DigiviceScene* scene, uint32_t frame, uint8_t low) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_box(canvas, 0, SKY_TOP, 128, 64 - SKY_TOP);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    // 26 stars from the cached seed, a few twinkling off each frame.
    uint32_t rng = scene->seed | 1U;
    for(uint32_t i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
        uint32_t r = scene_rng(&rng);
        if((r >> 24) % 7 == frame % 7) continue;
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, (int32_t)(r % 128), SKY_TOP + 1 + (int32_t)((r >> 8) % low));
    }
    digivice_draw_fill_ellipse(canvas, 100, 15, 4, 4);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_fill_ellipse(canvas, 102, 14, 3, 3);
}

// One decoration standing on `base`, in `ink` over `paper`.
static void deco(
    Canvas* canvas,
    const DvLook* look,
    uint8_t v,
    int32_t x,
    int32_t base,
    uint32_t frame,
    bool dark) {
    Color ink = dark ? ColorWhite : ColorBlack;
    Color paper = dark ? ColorBlack : ColorWhite;
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ink);
    switch(look->deco) {
    case DecoGrass:
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, base, x - 1, base - 4);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 2, base, x + 3, base - 5);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 4, base, x + 4, base - 3);
        break;
    case DecoTree:
        // Wire tree with solder points.
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - 1, base - 18, 2, 18);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, base - 12, x - 6, base - 19);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, base - 15, x + 6, base - 22);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, base - 18, x - 3, base - 25);
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - 7, base - 20, 2, 2);
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x + 5, base - 23, 2, 2);
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - 4, base - 26, 2, 2);
        break;
    case DecoMast:
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, base - 20, x - 4, base);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, base - 20, x + 4, base);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x - 3, base - 6, x + 3, base - 12);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 3, base - 6, x - 3, base - 12);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, base - 24, x, base - 20);
        // Single-frame pulse on the 100 ms clock.
        if((frame + v) % 5 == 0) digivice_draw_burst(canvas, x, base - 25, 5);
        break;
    case DecoIce: {
        int32_t w = 7 + v * 2;
        canvas_set_color(canvas, paper);
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x, base - w, w, w);
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ink);
        canvas_draw_frame(canvas, x, base - w, w, w);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 2, base - 3, x + w - 3, base - w + 2);
        break;
    }
    case DecoMagnet:
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - 5, base - 9, 3, 9);
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x + 2, base - 9, 3, 9);
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - 5, base - 11, 10, 3);
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x - 8, base - 2 - (int32_t)(frame % 3));
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x + 8, base - 3 + (int32_t)(frame % 3));
        break;
    case DecoTower:
        if(v != 0) {
            digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, base - 10, x, base);
            digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x, base - 11);
            break;
        }
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x - 2, 14, x - 10, base);
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x + 2, 14, x + 10, base);
        for(int32_t y = 20; y < base; y += 8) {
            int32_t w = 2 + (y - 14) * 8 / (base - 14);
            digivice_draw_line(canvas, x - w, y, x + w, y + 8 < base ? y + 8 : base);
        }
        digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, 14, x, 8);
        if(frame % 6 < 3) {
            // Strobe: a single-frame toggle on the 100 ms clock.
            digivice_draw_fill_ellipse(canvas, x, 8, 2, 2);
            digivice_draw_burst(canvas, x, 8, 7);
        } else {
            digivice_draw_ring(canvas, x, 8, 2, 2);
        }
        break;
    case DecoPacket:
        canvas_set_color(canvas, paper);
        digivice_draw_box(canvas, x - 6, base - 12, 13, 9);
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ink);
        canvas_draw_frame(canvas, x - 6, base - 12, 13, 9);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, x - 3, base - 11, v % 2 ? "01" : "10");
        break;
    default: {
        // Floating shards of the null core, bobbing.
        int32_t y = 14 + v * 6 + (int32_t)((frame / 4 + v) % 3);
        canvas_draw_frame(canvas, x, y, 2 + v, 2 + v);
        break;
    }
    }
}

void digivice_draw_scene(
    Canvas* canvas,
    const DigiviceScene* scene,
    uint32_t scroll,
    uint32_t frame,
    uint16_t day_minute) {
    const DvLook* look = &looks[scene->area < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT ? scene->area : 0];
    bool night = day_minute >= 20 * 60 || day_minute < 6 * 60;
    bool dusk = !night && (day_minute >= 18 * 60 || day_minute < 7 * 60 + 30);
    // The null core is a negative world: always drawn with the night palette.
    bool dark = night || scene->area == DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT - 1;

    if(dark) {
        sky_night(canvas, scene, frame, (uint8_t)(look->far_base - SKY_TOP - 2));
    } else if(dusk) {
        sky_dusk(canvas, (uint8_t)(look->far_base - 1));
    } else {
        sky_day(canvas, scroll);
    }

    Color ink = dark ? ColorWhite : ColorBlack;
    uint32_t far_off = scroll / 3;
    for(int32_t x = 0; x < 128; x++) {
        int32_t fh = scene->far[(far_off + (uint32_t)x) % DIGIVICE_SCENE_W];
        int32_t nh = scene->near[(scroll + (uint32_t)x) % DIGIVICE_SCENE_W];
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ink);
        // The far layer only shows above the near one: no overdraw, one call per layer.
        if(fh < nh) {
            if(x % look->far_step == 0) {
                digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, fh, x, nh - 1);
            } else {
                digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x, fh);
            }
        }
        if(look->near == NearSolid || (look->near == NearStripes && x % 2 == 0)) {
            // At night the near ground stays dark and only its outline is light.
            canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
            digivice_draw_line(canvas, x, nh, x, look->ground);
            if(dark) {
                canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
                digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x, nh);
            }
        } else {
            digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x, nh);
        }
    }

    canvas_set_color(canvas, ink);
    digivice_draw_line(canvas, 0, look->ground, 127, look->ground);
    for(int32_t x = 5 - (int32_t)(scroll % 6); x < 128; x += 6)
        digivice_draw_dot(canvas, x, look->ground + 3 + (x / 6) % 3);
    if(look->deco == DecoGrass) {
        // TV-snow grass: the noise boils with the frame, not with a per-pixel hash.
        uint32_t rng = scene->seed ^ (frame * 0x9E3779B9UL);
        for(uint32_t i = 0; i < 24; i++) {
            uint32_t r = scene_rng(&rng);
            int32_t y = look->ground + 1 + (int32_t)((r >> 8) % (63U - look->ground));
            digivice_draw_dot(canvas, (int32_t)(r % 128), y);
        }
    }

    for(uint32_t i = 0; i < DIGIVICE_SCENE_DECOS; i++) {
        int32_t x = (int32_t)((scene->deco_x[i] + DIGIVICE_SCENE_W - scroll % DIGIVICE_SCENE_W) %
                              DIGIVICE_SCENE_W);
        if(x > 140) x -= DIGIVICE_SCENE_W;
        if(x < -12 || x > 140) continue;
        int32_t base = x >= 0 && x < 128 ? scene->near[(scroll + (uint32_t)x) % DIGIVICE_SCENE_W] :
                                           look->near_base;
        deco(canvas, look, scene->deco_v[i], x, base, frame, dark);
    }
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing battle tests**

Create `applications/services/digivice/test/digivice_battle_test.c` with exactly this content:

```c
// Host-side test for the battle rules. Run: applications/services/digivice/test/run.sh
#include "../digivice_battle.h"
#include "../digivice_config.h"

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

static int failures = 0;

#define CHECK(cond)                                                \
    do {                                                           \
        if(!(cond)) {                                              \
            printf("FAIL %s:%d: %s\n", __FILE__, __LINE__, #cond); \
            failures++;                                            \
        }                                                          \
    } while(0)

#define H(n)   ((uint32_t)(n) * 3600UL)
#define DAY(n) ((uint32_t)(n) * 86400UL)

// Day 10 at 08:00, a wide-awake starting point.
#define T0 (DAY(10) + H(8))

static DigiviceState with_form(uint8_t form) {
    DigiviceState s;
    digivice_rules_init(&s, T0);
    s.form = form;
    return s;
}

// A battle with fixed powers and attributes, as the hit-chance table in the spec.
static DigiviceBattle duel(uint16_t p, uint8_t pa, uint16_t q, uint8_t qa) {
    DigiviceBattle b;
    memset(&b, 0, sizeof(b));
    b.player_power = p;
    b.player_attr = pa;
    b.foe_power = q;
    b.foe_attr = qa;
    return b;
}

static void test_hit_pct(void) {
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(20, 10, 0) == 67);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(30, 10, 0) == 75);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(64, 59, 1) == 57);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(59, 64, -1) == 43);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(64, 131, 0) == 33);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(50, 50, 1) == 55);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(50, 50, -1) == 45);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(100, 1, 0) == DIGIVICE_HIT_MAX_PCT);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(100, 1, 1) == DIGIVICE_HIT_MAX_PCT);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(1, 100, 0) == DIGIVICE_HIT_MIN_PCT);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(1, 100, -1) == DIGIVICE_HIT_MIN_PCT);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_hit_pct(0, 0, 0) == 50);
}

static void test_power(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormByteegg);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_player_power(&s, T0) == 0);
    s.form = DigiviceFormBitling;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_player_power(&s, T0) == 8);
    s.form = DigiviceFormBytemon;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_player_power(&s, T0) == 20);
    s.form = DigiviceFormWavedrake;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_player_power(&s, T0) == 40);
    s.strength = 24;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_player_power(&s, T0) == 64);
    s.strength = 300;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_player_power(&s, T0) == 64);
    s.injured_until = T0 + 1;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_injured(&s, T0));
    CHECK(digivice_rules_player_power(&s, T0) == 48);
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_injured(&s, T0 + 1));
    CHECK(digivice_rules_player_power(&s, T0 + 1) == 64);
    // A corrupt Champion keeps its stage power.
    s.injured_until = 0;
    s.form = DigiviceFormVirubyte;
    s.prev_form = DigiviceFormTagdrake;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_player_power(&s, T0) == 64);

    CHECK(digivice_rules_charged_power(20, DigiviceChargeSmall) == 12);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charged_power(20, DigiviceChargeNormal) == 20);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charged_power(20, DigiviceChargeBig) == 30);
}

static void test_charge(void) {
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_zone_w(0) == 3);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_zone_w(8) == 4);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_zone_w(16) == 5);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_zone_w(24) == 6);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_zone_w(1000) == 6);
    for(uint8_t w = 3; w <= 6; w++) {
        bool low = false, high = false;
        for(uint32_t r = 0; r < 256; r++) {
            uint8_t start = digivice_rules_charge_zone_start(w, (uint8_t)r);
            CHECK(start >= 3 && start <= DIGIVICE_METER_SEGMENTS - w - 1);
            low |= start == 3;
            high |= start == DIGIVICE_METER_SEGMENTS - w - 1;
        }
        CHECK(low && high);
    }
    // Zone 8..11: inside is Big, two segments either side Normal, beyond Small.
    for(uint8_t c = 8; c <= 11; c++)
        CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_tier(c, 8, 4) == DigiviceChargeBig);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_tier(7, 8, 4) == DigiviceChargeNormal);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_tier(6, 8, 4) == DigiviceChargeNormal);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_tier(12, 8, 4) == DigiviceChargeNormal);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_tier(13, 8, 4) == DigiviceChargeNormal);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_tier(5, 8, 4) == DigiviceChargeSmall);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_tier(14, 8, 4) == DigiviceChargeSmall);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_tier(0, 3, 3) == DigiviceChargeSmall);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_charge_tier(1, 3, 3) == DigiviceChargeNormal);

    // The cursor ping-pongs; a timeout at frame 30 stops it wherever it is.
    CHECK(digivice_rules_meter_cursor(0) == 0);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_meter_cursor(19) == 19);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_meter_cursor(20) == 18);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_meter_cursor(38) == 0);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_meter_cursor(DIGIVICE_CHARGE_FRAMES) == 8);
}

static void test_guard(void) {
    static const uint32_t frames[5] = {0, 4, 5, 9, 10};
    static const uint8_t from_high[5] = {0, 0, 1, 1, 0};
    for(int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        CHECK(digivice_rules_guard_at(DigiviceAimHigh, frames[i]) == from_high[i]);
        CHECK(digivice_rules_guard_at(DigiviceAimLow, frames[i]) == !from_high[i]);
    }
    // Sampled 5 frames after the press: aiming high at frame 0 meets the low guard.
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_shot_blocked(DigiviceAimHigh, DigiviceAimHigh, 0));
    CHECK(digivice_rules_shot_blocked(DigiviceAimLow, DigiviceAimHigh, 0));
    CHECK(digivice_rules_shot_blocked(DigiviceAimHigh, DigiviceAimHigh, 5));
    CHECK(!digivice_rules_shot_blocked(DigiviceAimHigh, DigiviceAimHigh, 4));
}

static void test_shot_outcomes(void) {
    // P 20 vs Q 10: 67% unblocked, 33% blocked.
    DigiviceBattle b = duel(20, DigiviceAttrNulo, 10, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&b, DigiviceChargeNormal, false, 99, 66) ==
          DigiviceShotHit);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&b, DigiviceChargeNormal, false, 99, 67) ==
          DigiviceShotDodge);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&b, DigiviceChargeNormal, true, 99, 32) ==
          DigiviceShotGuardBroken);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&b, DigiviceChargeNormal, true, 99, 33) ==
          DigiviceShotBlocked);
    CHECK(b.player_hits == 2);
    // Big charge: P 30, 75%.
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&b, DigiviceChargeBig, false, 99, 74) == DigiviceShotHit);

    // A blocked shot never drops below 5%.
    DigiviceBattle weak = duel(1, DigiviceAttrNulo, 100, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&weak, DigiviceChargeNormal, true, 99, 4) ==
          DigiviceShotGuardBroken);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&weak, DigiviceChargeNormal, true, 99, 5) ==
          DigiviceShotBlocked);

    // A glitchy pet first rolls the 25% corruption miss.
    DigiviceBattle g = duel(100, DigiviceAttrNulo, 1, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    g.glitchy = true;
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&g, DigiviceChargeNormal, false, 24, 0) ==
          DigiviceShotDodge);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&g, DigiviceChargeNormal, false, 25, 0) ==
          DigiviceShotHit);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&g, DigiviceChargeNormal, true, 24, 0) ==
          DigiviceShotBlocked);
}

static void test_foe_turn_symmetry(void) {
    // Wavedrake (Portador, 64) against a Ruido foe of 59: 57% one way, 43% the other.
    DigiviceBattle b = duel(64, DigiviceAttrPortador, 59, DigiviceAttrRuido);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&b, DigiviceChargeNormal, false, 99, 56) ==
          DigiviceShotHit);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_player_shot(&b, DigiviceChargeNormal, false, 99, 57) ==
          DigiviceShotDodge);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_foe_shot(&b, 42) == DigiviceShotHit);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_foe_shot(&b, 43) == DigiviceShotDodge);
    CHECK(b.foe_hits == 1 && b.round == 2);
    // The foe uses the uncharged power: a Big charge last turn changes nothing.
    digivice_battle_player_shot(&b, DigiviceChargeBig, false, 99, 99);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_foe_shot(&b, 43) == DigiviceShotDodge);
}

static DigiviceBattleOutcome play(DigiviceBattle* b, const char* script) {
    // 'p' player hit, 'f' foe hit, '-' a miss on that side, in turn order.
    bool player_turn = true;
    for(const char* c = script; *c; c++) {
        if(player_turn) {
            digivice_battle_player_shot(b, DigiviceChargeNormal, false, 99, *c == 'p' ? 0 : 99);
        } else {
            digivice_battle_foe_shot(b, *c == 'f' ? 0 : 99);
        }
        player_turn = !player_turn;
        if(digivice_battle_outcome(b) != DigiviceBattleOngoing) break;
    }
    return digivice_battle_outcome(b);
}

static void test_three_hits(void) {
    DigiviceBattle b = duel(40, DigiviceAttrNulo, 40, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(play(&b, "p-p-p") == DigiviceBattleWon && b.player_hits == 3 && b.round == 2);

    b = duel(40, DigiviceAttrNulo, 40, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(play(&b, "pfpfp") == DigiviceBattleWon && b.foe_hits == 2);

    b = duel(40, DigiviceAttrNulo, 40, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(play(&b, "pfpf-f") == DigiviceBattleLost && b.player_hits == 2 && b.foe_hits == 3);

    // The battle ends at the shot that reaches 3: the foe never gets its turn.
    b = duel(40, DigiviceAttrNulo, 40, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    play(&b, "p-p-p");
    CHECK(b.round == 2 && digivice_battle_outcome(&b) == DigiviceBattleWon);
}

static void test_round_cap(void) {
    DigiviceBattle b = duel(40, DigiviceAttrNulo, 40, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(play(&b, "--------------------") == DigiviceBattleDrawn && b.round == 10);
    b = duel(40, DigiviceAttrNulo, 40, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(play(&b, "p-------------------") == DigiviceBattleWon && b.round == 10);
    b = duel(40, DigiviceAttrNulo, 40, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(play(&b, "pf-f----------------") == DigiviceBattleLost && b.round == 10);
    b = duel(40, DigiviceAttrNulo, 40, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(play(&b, "pfpf----------------") == DigiviceBattleDrawn);
    b = duel(40, DigiviceAttrNulo, 40, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    CHECK(play(&b, "------------------") == DigiviceBattleOngoing && b.round == 9);
}

static void test_shield_cancels_first_hit(void) {
    DigiviceBattle b = duel(40, DigiviceAttrNulo, 40, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    b.shield = true;
    CHECK(digivice_battle_foe_shot(&b, 0) == DigiviceShotShielded);
    CHECK(b.foe_hits == 0 && !b.shield && b.shield_used);
    CHECK(digivice_battle_foe_shot(&b, 0) == DigiviceShotHit && b.foe_hits == 1);
    // A miss leaves the Shield up.
    DigiviceBattle c = duel(40, DigiviceAttrNulo, 40, DigiviceAttrNulo);
    c.shield = true;
    CHECK(digivice_battle_foe_shot(&c, 99) == DigiviceShotDodge && c.shield);
}

static void test_battle_init(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormWavedrake);
    s.strength = 24;
    s.area = 4;
    s.enc_foe = 0;
    s.item = DigiviceItemShield;
    DigiviceBattle b;
    digivice_battle_init(&b, &s, DigiviceEncounterTrail, T0);
    CHECK(b.foe == DigiviceFoeFuzzcan && b.foe_attr == DigiviceAttrRuido);
    CHECK(b.foe_power == 59 && b.foe_shot == DIGIVICE_SHOT_CHAMP_FOE);
    CHECK(b.player_power == 64 && b.player_attr == DigiviceAttrPortador);
    CHECK(b.player_shot == 5 && b.shield && !b.glitchy && b.player_hits == 0);
    s.enc_foe = 1;
    digivice_battle_init(&b, &s, DigiviceEncounterTrail, T0);
    CHECK(b.foe == DigiviceFoeKorno && b.foe_power == 46 && b.foe_shot == 3);

    digivice_battle_init(&b, &s, DigiviceEncounterBoss, T0);
    CHECK(b.foe == 4 && b.foe_power == 64 && b.foe_shot == DIGIVICE_SHOT_BOSS);
    s.area = 7;
    digivice_battle_init(&b, &s, DigiviceEncounterBoss, T0);
    CHECK(b.foe_power == 131 && b.foe_shot == DIGIVICE_SHOT_BEAM);

    DigiviceState r = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    r.virux_type = DigiviceTypeSubGhz;
    r.corruption = 60;
    digivice_battle_init(&b, &r, DigiviceEncounterVirux, T0);
    CHECK(b.foe_power == 24 && b.foe_attr == DigiviceAttrPortador && b.foe_shot == 3);
    CHECK(b.player_shot == 3 && b.glitchy && !b.shield);
    r.form = DigiviceFormTagdrake;
    digivice_battle_init(&b, &r, DigiviceEncounterVirux, T0);
    CHECK(b.foe_power == 46 && b.foe_shot == 5);

    // The Baby fights trail foes with a 2x2 shot.
    DigiviceState baby = with_form(DigiviceFormBitling);
    baby.enc_foe = 0;
    digivice_battle_init(&b, &baby, DigiviceEncounterTrail, T0);
    CHECK(b.player_power == 8 && b.player_shot == 2 && b.foe_power == 10);
}

static void test_can_battle(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBitling);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_can_battle(&s, T0) == DigiviceResultOk);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_can_battle(&s, DAY(10) + H(23) + 60) == DigiviceResultAsleep);
    s.sick = 1;
    CHECK(digivice_rules_can_battle(&s, DAY(10) + H(23) + 60) == DigiviceResultSick);
    DigiviceState egg = with_form(DigiviceFormByteegg);
    CHECK(digivice_rules_can_battle(&egg, T0) == DigiviceResultTooYoung);
}

static void test_retreat(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
    for(uint8_t a = 0; a < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT; a++) {
        s.area = a;
        CHECK(digivice_battle_retreat_allowed(&s, DigiviceEncounterVirux));
        CHECK(digivice_battle_retreat_allowed(&s, DigiviceEncounterTrail) == (a < 4));
        CHECK(digivice_battle_retreat_allowed(&s, DigiviceEncounterBoss) == (a < 4));
    }
}

static DigiviceReward finish(
    DigiviceState* s,
    DigiviceEncounterKind kind,
    DigiviceBattleResult result,
    bool shield_used,
    uint8_t rng_pct) {
    return digivice_rules_battle_result(s, kind, result, shield_used, rng_pct, T0);
}

static void test_trail_rewards(void) {
    for(uint8_t a = 0; a < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT; a++) {
        DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
        s.area = a;
        s.enc_foe = 1;
        s.enc_expires = T0 + 60;
        DigiviceReward r = finish(&s, DigiviceEncounterTrail, DigiviceBattleResultWin, false, 99);
        CHECK(r.exp == 6 + 2 * (a + 1) && s.exp == r.exp && s.wins == 1);
        CHECK(r.item == DigiviceItemNone && s.enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE && s.enc_expires == 0);
    }
    DigiviceState d = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
    uint8_t drop = DIGIVICE_DROP_PCT - 1;
    DigiviceReward r = finish(&d, DigiviceEncounterTrail, DigiviceBattleResultWin, false, drop);
    CHECK(r.item == DigiviceItemShield && d.item == DigiviceItemShield);
    d.item = DigiviceItemNone;
    r = finish(&d, DigiviceEncounterTrail, DigiviceBattleResultWin, false, DIGIVICE_DROP_PCT);
    CHECK(r.item == DigiviceItemNone && d.item == DigiviceItemNone);
    // A held Shield is not stacked.
    d.item = DigiviceItemShield;
    r = finish(&d, DigiviceEncounterTrail, DigiviceBattleResultWin, false, 0);
    CHECK(r.item == DigiviceItemNone && d.item == DigiviceItemShield);
    // A Shield used in the fight is gone, and a drop can replace it.
    r = finish(&d, DigiviceEncounterTrail, DigiviceBattleResultWin, true, 99);
    CHECK(d.item == DigiviceItemNone);
    d.item = DigiviceItemShield;
    r = finish(&d, DigiviceEncounterTrail, DigiviceBattleResultWin, true, 0);
    CHECK(d.item == DigiviceItemShield && r.item == DigiviceItemShield);
}

static void test_boss_rewards(void) {
    for(uint8_t a = 0; a < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT; a++) {
        DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
        s.cleared = (uint8_t)((1U << a) - 1);
        s.area = a;
        s.distance = 0;
        DigiviceReward r = finish(&s, DigiviceEncounterBoss, DigiviceBattleResultWin, false, 99);
        CHECK(r.exp == 40 + 20 * (a + 1) && r.item == DigiviceItemShield);
        CHECK(s.cleared & (1U << a));
    }
    // A repeat clear pays half.
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
    s.cleared = 0x03;
    s.area = 0;
    s.distance = 0;
    s.item = DigiviceItemShield;
    DigiviceReward r = finish(&s, DigiviceEncounterBoss, DigiviceBattleResultWin, false, 99);
    CHECK(r.exp == 30 && r.item == DigiviceItemNone && s.area == 2 && s.distance == 720);
}

static void test_defeat_penalties(void) {
    for(uint8_t a = 0; a < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT; a++) {
        DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
        s.area = a;
        s.distance = 100;
        s.mid_state = 2;
        s.mood = 3;
        s.enc_foe = 0;
        s.enc_expires = T0 + 60;
        finish(&s, DigiviceEncounterTrail, DigiviceBattleResultLose, false, 0);
        CHECK(s.losses == 1 && s.corruption == DIGIVICE_CORRUPTION_LOSE_GAIN && s.mood == 2);
        CHECK(s.injured_until == T0 + DIGIVICE_INJURY_S && s.enc_foe == DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE);
        if(a >= 4) {
            CHECK(s.distance == digivice_rules_area_length(a) && s.mid_state == 0);
        } else {
            CHECK(s.distance == 100 && s.mid_state == 2);
        }
    }
    // A boss loss in areas 1 to 4 keeps the boss waiting.
    DigiviceState b = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
    b.distance = 0;
    finish(&b, DigiviceEncounterBoss, DigiviceBattleResultLose, false, 0);
    CHECK(b.distance == 0 && digivice_expedition_boss_ready(&b));
    // A loss can push corruption to 100 and transform the pet.
    DigiviceState c = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    c.corruption = 90;
    finish(&c, DigiviceEncounterTrail, DigiviceBattleResultLose, false, 0);
    CHECK(c.form == DigiviceFormVirubyte && c.prev_form == DigiviceFormBytemon);
    // The injury heals on the tick after it ends.
    c.form = DigiviceFormBytemon;
    digivice_rules_tick(&c, T0 + DIGIVICE_INJURY_S);
    CHECK(c.injured_until == 0);
}

static void test_virux_rewards(void) {
    DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    s.virux_type = DigiviceTypeUsb;
    s.virux_expires = T0 + 60;
    DigiviceReward r = finish(&s, DigiviceEncounterVirux, DigiviceBattleResultWin, false, 99);
    CHECK(r.exp == 2 * DIGIVICE_WIN_BASE_EXP && s.exp == r.exp);
    CHECK(s.data[DigiviceTypeUsb] == DIGIVICE_WIN_DATA && s.wins == 1);
    CHECK(s.virux_type == DigiviceTypeNone);
    // A VIRUX loss never resets an Expedition streak.
    s.area = 5;
    s.distance = 10;
    s.virux_type = DigiviceTypeNfc;
    finish(&s, DigiviceEncounterVirux, DigiviceBattleResultLose, false, 0);
    CHECK(s.distance == 10 && s.virux_type == DigiviceTypeNone && s.losses == 1);
}

static void test_win_without_virux_type_grants_exp_only(void) {
    // Guard bytes after the state catch a write through data[DigiviceTypeNone].
    struct {
        DigiviceState s;
        uint8_t guard[1024];
    } box;
    box.s = with_form(DigiviceFormBytemon);
    memset(box.guard, 0xAA, sizeof(box.guard));
    finish(&box.s, DigiviceEncounterVirux, DigiviceBattleResultWin, false, 99);
    CHECK(box.s.exp == 2 * DIGIVICE_WIN_BASE_EXP && box.s.wins == 1);
    for(uint8_t t = 0; t < DigiviceTypeCount; t++)
        CHECK(box.s.data[t] == 0);
    for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(box.guard); i++) {
        if(box.guard[i] != 0xAA) {
            CHECK(box.guard[i] == 0xAA);
            break;
        }
    }
}

static void test_draw_and_flee_change_nothing_else(void) {
    for(int result = DigiviceBattleResultFled; result <= DigiviceBattleResultDraw; result++) {
        DigiviceState s = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
        s.area = 6;
        s.distance = 50;
        s.enc_foe = 1;
        s.enc_expires = T0 + 60;
        DigiviceState before = s;
        DigiviceBattleResult res = (DigiviceBattleResult)result;
        DigiviceReward r = finish(&s, DigiviceEncounterTrail, res, false, 0);
        CHECK(r.exp == 0 && r.item == DigiviceItemNone);
        before.enc_foe = DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE;
        before.enc_expires = 0;
        CHECK(memcmp(&s, &before, sizeof(s)) == 0);
    }
    // A fled boss stays at distance 0.
    DigiviceState b = with_form(DigiviceFormTagdrake);
    b.distance = 0;
    finish(&b, DigiviceEncounterBoss, DigiviceBattleResultFled, false, 0);
    CHECK(b.distance == 0 && b.cleared == 0);
}

int main(void) {
    test_hit_pct();
    test_power();
    test_charge();
    test_guard();
    test_shot_outcomes();
    test_foe_turn_symmetry();
    test_three_hits();
    test_round_cap();
    test_shield_cancels_first_hit();
    test_battle_init();
    test_can_battle();
    test_retreat();
    test_trail_rewards();
    test_boss_rewards();
    test_defeat_penalties();
    test_virux_rewards();
    test_win_without_virux_type_grants_exp_only();
    test_draw_and_flee_change_nothing_else();

    if(failures) {
        printf("%d check(s) failed\n", failures);
        return EXIT_FAILURE;
    }
    printf("digivice_battle: all checks passed\n");
    return EXIT_SUCCESS;
}
```

Replace the whole content of `applications/services/digivice/test/run.sh` with exactly this content:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Builds and runs the host-side digivice tests with the system compiler.
set -e
here="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
services="$here/../.."
app="$services/../main/digivice"
out="${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"
flags="-std=c11 -Wall -Wextra -Werror -I $services"
rules="$here/../digivice_rules.c $here/../digivice_expedition.c $here/../digivice_battle.c"

for name in rules expedition battle; do
    # $rules is split on purpose: one argument per source file.
    cc $flags "$here/digivice_${name}_test.c" $rules -o "$out/digivice_${name}_test"
    "$out/digivice_${name}_test"
done

cc $flags "$here/digivice_i18n_test.c" "$here/../digivice_i18n.c" -o "$out/digivice_i18n_test"
# Every digivice source must stay ASCII-only: accents live in digivice_i18n.c as escapes.
"$out/digivice_i18n_test" "$here"/../*.c "$here"/../*.h "$here"/*.c "$app"/*.c "$app"/*.h \
    "$services/desktop/views/desktop_view_digivice.c"
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Create the battle header**

Create `applications/services/digivice/digivice_battle.h` with exactly this content:

```c
#pragma once

// Pure battle rules: no furi includes, so the host test can compile this file with plain cc.

#include "digivice_expedition.h"

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef enum {
    DigiviceBattleResultWin,
    DigiviceBattleResultLose,
    DigiviceBattleResultFled,
    DigiviceBattleResultDraw,
} DigiviceBattleResult;

typedef enum {
    DigiviceBattleOngoing,
    DigiviceBattleWon,
    DigiviceBattleLost,
    DigiviceBattleDrawn,
} DigiviceBattleOutcome;

typedef enum {
    DigiviceEncounterTrail,
    DigiviceEncounterBoss,
    DigiviceEncounterVirux,
    DigiviceEncounterCount,
} DigiviceEncounterKind;

typedef enum {
    DigiviceChargeSmall,
    DigiviceChargeNormal,
    DigiviceChargeBig,
    DigiviceChargeCount,
} DigiviceCharge;

/** Aim and guard heights share these values. */
typedef enum {
    DigiviceAimHigh,
    DigiviceAimLow,
} DigiviceAim;

typedef enum {
    DigiviceShotHit, /**< reached the target */
    DigiviceShotDodge, /**< missed: the target hopped away */
    DigiviceShotGuardBroken, /**< blocked height, but the halved roll still hit */
    DigiviceShotBlocked, /**< died on the guard */
    DigiviceShotShielded, /**< foe hit cancelled by the Shield */
} DigiviceShot;

typedef struct {
    uint16_t exp;
    uint8_t item; /**< DigiviceItem dropped, DigiviceItemNone when nothing dropped */
} DigiviceReward;

typedef struct {
    uint8_t kind; /**< DigiviceEncounterKind */
    uint8_t foe; /**< DigiviceFoe (trail), area (boss) or DigiviceType (VIRUX) */
    uint8_t player_attr; /**< DigiviceAttr */
    uint8_t foe_attr;
    uint8_t player_shot; /**< projectile size in px, DIGIVICE_SHOT_BEAM for the beam */
    uint8_t foe_shot;
    uint8_t player_hits;
    uint8_t foe_hits;
    uint8_t round; /**< completed rounds: one player shot and one foe shot each */
    bool shield; /**< the held Shield still waits for the first foe hit */
    bool shield_used;
    bool glitchy; /**< corruption >= 50: every player shot first rolls the corruption miss */
    uint16_t player_power; /**< uncharged power P */
    uint16_t foe_power; /**< Q */
} DigiviceBattle;

/** round(100*P/(P+Q)) + 5*adv, clamped to 10..90. */
uint8_t digivice_rules_hit_pct(uint16_t p, uint16_t q, int8_t adv);
/** Stage base power plus strength (capped at 24), x3/4 while injured. */
uint16_t digivice_rules_player_power(const DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now);
uint16_t digivice_rules_charged_power(uint16_t power, DigiviceCharge tier);
/** Zone width on the 20-segment charge meter: 3 + min(strength, 24) / 8. */
uint8_t digivice_rules_charge_zone_w(uint16_t strength);
/** Ping-pong cursor of the charge meter: 0..19..0, one segment per frame. */
uint8_t digivice_rules_meter_cursor(uint32_t frame);
/** Random zone start in [3, 20 - w - 1]. */
uint8_t digivice_rules_charge_zone_start(uint8_t zone_w, uint8_t rng);
/** Big inside the zone, Normal within 2 segments of it, Small otherwise. */
DigiviceCharge digivice_rules_charge_tier(uint8_t cursor, uint8_t zone_start, uint8_t zone_w);
/** Guard height at `frame` of the aim phase: flips every 5 frames from g0. */
uint8_t digivice_rules_guard_at(uint8_t g0, uint32_t frame);
/** The guard is sampled when the shot lands, 5 frames after the press. */
bool digivice_rules_shot_blocked(uint8_t aim, uint8_t g0, uint32_t press_frame);
bool digivice_rules_injured(const DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now);

void digivice_battle_init(
    DigiviceBattle* b,
    const DigiviceState* s,
    DigiviceEncounterKind kind,
    uint32_t now);
/** `miss_roll` feeds the corruption miss, `hit_roll` the hit chance; both 0..99. */
DigiviceShot digivice_battle_player_shot(
    DigiviceBattle* b,
    DigiviceCharge tier,
    bool blocked,
    uint8_t miss_roll,
    uint8_t hit_roll);
/** The automatic foe turn at the player's uncharged power; ends the round. */
DigiviceShot digivice_battle_foe_shot(DigiviceBattle* b, uint8_t hit_roll);
/** First to 3 hits; after round 10 the side with more hits wins, a tie is a draw. */
DigiviceBattleOutcome digivice_battle_outcome(const DigiviceBattle* b);
/** VIRUX and areas 1 to 4 allow a retreat; streak areas do not. */
bool digivice_battle_retreat_allowed(const DigiviceState* s, DigiviceEncounterKind kind);

/** Applies a finished battle and clears its encounter. `shield_used` drops the held Shield. */
DigiviceReward digivice_rules_battle_result(
    DigiviceState* s,
    DigiviceEncounterKind kind,
    DigiviceBattleResult result,
    bool shield_used,
    uint8_t rng_pct,
    uint32_t now);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Create the battle rules**

Create `applications/services/digivice/digivice_battle.c` with exactly this content:

```c
#include "digivice_battle.h"
#include "digivice_config.h"

#include <string.h>

static const uint16_t stage_power[DigiviceStageCount] = DIGIVICE_STAGE_POWER;
static const uint8_t stage_shot[DigiviceStageCount] = DIGIVICE_STAGE_SHOT;
static const uint16_t virux_power[DigiviceStageCount] = DIGIVICE_VIRUX_POWER;
static const uint8_t charge_x10[DigiviceChargeCount] = DIGIVICE_CHARGE_X10;

static void inc_u16(uint16_t* value) {
    if(*value < UINT16_MAX) (*value)++;
}

uint8_t digivice_rules_hit_pct(uint16_t p, uint16_t q, int8_t adv) {
    uint32_t sum = (uint32_t)p + q;
    int32_t pct = sum ? (int32_t)((200UL * p + sum) / (2UL * sum)) : 50;
    pct += DIGIVICE_ATTR_PCT * adv;
    if(pct < DIGIVICE_HIT_MIN_PCT) pct = DIGIVICE_HIT_MIN_PCT;
    if(pct > DIGIVICE_HIT_MAX_PCT) pct = DIGIVICE_HIT_MAX_PCT;
    return (uint8_t)pct;
}

bool digivice_rules_injured(const DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now) {
    return s->injured_until != 0 && now < s->injured_until;
}

uint16_t digivice_rules_player_power(const DigiviceState* s, uint32_t now) {
    uint16_t strength = s->strength < DIGIVICE_STRENGTH_CAP ? s->strength : DIGIVICE_STRENGTH_CAP;
    uint16_t power = stage_power[digivice_rules_stage(s)] + strength;
    if(digivice_rules_injured(s, now)) power = power * 3 / 4;
    return power;
}

uint16_t digivice_rules_charged_power(uint16_t power, DigiviceCharge tier) {
    if(tier >= DigiviceChargeCount) tier = DigiviceChargeNormal;
    return (uint16_t)((uint32_t)power * charge_x10[tier] / 10);
}

uint8_t digivice_rules_charge_zone_w(uint16_t strength) {
    uint16_t capped = strength < DIGIVICE_STRENGTH_CAP ? strength : DIGIVICE_STRENGTH_CAP;
    return (uint8_t)(DIGIVICE_ZONE_BASE_W + capped / 8);
}

uint8_t digivice_rules_meter_cursor(uint32_t frame) {
    uint32_t period = 2 * (DIGIVICE_METER_SEGMENTS - 1);
    uint32_t q = frame % period;
    return (uint8_t)(q < DIGIVICE_METER_SEGMENTS ? q : period - q);
}

uint8_t digivice_rules_charge_zone_start(uint8_t zone_w, uint8_t rng) {
    // Starts in [3, 20 - w - 1]: never flush with either end of the meter.
    uint8_t span = (uint8_t)(DIGIVICE_METER_SEGMENTS - zone_w - 3);
    return (uint8_t)(3 + rng % span);
}

DigiviceCharge digivice_rules_charge_tier(uint8_t cursor, uint8_t zone_start, uint8_t zone_w) {
    int32_t c = cursor;
    int32_t lo = zone_start;
    int32_t hi = zone_start + zone_w; // exclusive
    if(c >= lo && c < hi) return DigiviceChargeBig;
    if(c >= lo - DIGIVICE_NEAR_SEGMENTS && c < hi + DIGIVICE_NEAR_SEGMENTS) {
        return DigiviceChargeNormal;
    }
    return DigiviceChargeSmall;
}

uint8_t digivice_rules_guard_at(uint8_t g0, uint32_t frame) {
    return (uint8_t)((g0 ^ (frame / DIGIVICE_GUARD_FLIP_FRAMES)) & 1);
}

bool digivice_rules_shot_blocked(uint8_t aim, uint8_t g0, uint32_t press_frame) {
    return aim == digivice_rules_guard_at(g0, press_frame + DIGIVICE_IMPACT_LAG_FRAMES);
}

void digivice_battle_init(
    DigiviceBattle* b,
    const DigiviceState* s,
    DigiviceEncounterKind kind,
    uint32_t now) {
    memset(b, 0, sizeof(DigiviceBattle));
    DigiviceStage stage = digivice_rules_stage(s);
    b->kind = kind;
    b->player_attr = digivice_rules_form_attr(s);
    b->player_power = digivice_rules_player_power(s, now);
    b->player_shot = stage_shot[stage];
    b->shield = s->item == DigiviceItemShield;
    b->glitchy = digivice_rules_glitchy(s);

    if(kind == DigiviceEncounterBoss) {
        b->foe = s->area;
        b->foe_attr = digivice_expedition_boss_attr(s->area);
        b->foe_power = digivice_expedition_boss_power(s->area);
        b->foe_shot = s->area == DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT - 1 ? DIGIVICE_SHOT_BEAM : DIGIVICE_SHOT_BOSS;
    } else if(kind == DigiviceEncounterVirux) {
        b->foe = s->virux_type;
        b->foe_attr = digivice_rules_type_attr((DigiviceType)s->virux_type);
        b->foe_power = virux_power[stage];
        b->foe_shot = b->player_shot;
    } else {
        DigiviceFoe foe = digivice_expedition_pool_foe(s->area, s->enc_foe <= 1 ? s->enc_foe : 0);
        b->foe = foe;
        b->foe_attr = digivice_expedition_foe_attr(foe);
        b->foe_power = digivice_expedition_foe_power(s->area, foe);
        b->foe_shot = digivice_expedition_foe_champion(foe) ? DIGIVICE_SHOT_CHAMP_FOE :
                                                              DIGIVICE_SHOT_ROOKIE_FOE;
    }
}

DigiviceShot digivice_battle_player_shot(
    DigiviceBattle* b,
    DigiviceCharge tier,
    bool blocked,
    uint8_t miss_roll,
    uint8_t hit_roll) {
    uint8_t pct = digivice_rules_hit_pct(
        digivice_rules_charged_power(b->player_power, tier),
        b->foe_power,
        digivice_rules_attr_adv(b->player_attr, b->foe_attr));
    if(blocked) {
        pct /= 2;
        if(pct < DIGIVICE_BLOCK_MIN_PCT) pct = DIGIVICE_BLOCK_MIN_PCT;
    }
    bool corrupt_miss = b->glitchy && miss_roll < DIGIVICE_CORRUPTION_MISS_PCT;
    bool hit = !corrupt_miss && hit_roll < pct;
    if(hit) b->player_hits++;
    if(blocked) return hit ? DigiviceShotGuardBroken : DigiviceShotBlocked;
    return hit ? DigiviceShotHit : DigiviceShotDodge;
}

DigiviceShot digivice_battle_foe_shot(DigiviceBattle* b, uint8_t hit_roll) {
    uint8_t pct = digivice_rules_hit_pct(
        b->foe_power, b->player_power, digivice_rules_attr_adv(b->foe_attr, b->player_attr));
    DigiviceShot shot = DigiviceShotDodge;
    if(hit_roll < pct) {
        if(b->shield) {
            b->shield = false;
            b->shield_used = true;
            shot = DigiviceShotShielded;
        } else {
            b->foe_hits++;
            shot = DigiviceShotHit;
        }
    }
    b->round++;
    return shot;
}

DigiviceBattleOutcome digivice_battle_outcome(const DigiviceBattle* b) {
    if(b->player_hits >= DIGIVICE_HITS_TO_WIN) return DigiviceBattleWon;
    if(b->foe_hits >= DIGIVICE_HITS_TO_WIN) return DigiviceBattleLost;
    if(b->round < DIGIVICE_ROUND_CAP) return DigiviceBattleOngoing;
    if(b->player_hits > b->foe_hits) return DigiviceBattleWon;
    if(b->foe_hits > b->player_hits) return DigiviceBattleLost;
    return DigiviceBattleDrawn;
}

bool digivice_battle_retreat_allowed(const DigiviceState* s, DigiviceEncounterKind kind) {
    return kind == DigiviceEncounterVirux || !digivice_expedition_streak_area(s->area);
}

static void drop_shield(DigiviceState* s, DigiviceReward* reward) {
    if(s->item != DigiviceItemNone) return;
    s->item = DigiviceItemShield;
    reward->item = DigiviceItemShield;
}

DigiviceReward digivice_rules_battle_result(
    DigiviceState* s,
    DigiviceEncounterKind kind,
    DigiviceBattleResult result,
    bool shield_used,
    uint8_t rng_pct,
    uint32_t now) {
    DigiviceReward reward = {.exp = 0, .item = DigiviceItemNone};
    if(shield_used && s->item == DigiviceItemShield) s->item = DigiviceItemNone;
    uint8_t area = s->area < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT ? s->area : DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT - 1;

    if(result == DigiviceBattleResultWin) {
        inc_u16(&s->wins);
        if(kind == DigiviceEncounterTrail) {
            reward.exp = DIGIVICE_TRAIL_EXP_BASE + DIGIVICE_TRAIL_EXP_AREA * (area + 1);
            if(rng_pct < DIGIVICE_DROP_PCT) drop_shield(s, &reward);
        } else if(kind == DigiviceEncounterBoss) {
            reward.exp = DIGIVICE_BOSS_EXP_BASE + DIGIVICE_BOSS_EXP_AREA * (area + 1);
            if(s->cleared & (1U << area)) reward.exp /= 2;
            drop_shield(s, &reward);
            digivice_expedition_boss_won(s);
        } else {
            reward.exp = 2 * DIGIVICE_WIN_BASE_EXP;
            if(s->virux_type < DigiviceTypeCount) {
                uint32_t data = (uint32_t)s->data[s->virux_type] + DIGIVICE_WIN_DATA;
                s->data[s->virux_type] = data > UINT16_MAX ? UINT16_MAX : (uint16_t)data;
            }
        }
        s->exp += reward.exp;
    } else if(result == DigiviceBattleResultLose) {
        inc_u16(&s->losses);
        digivice_rules_add_corruption(s, DIGIVICE_CORRUPTION_LOSE_GAIN, now);
        if(s->mood > 0) s->mood--;
        s->injured_until = now + DIGIVICE_INJURY_S;
        if(kind != DigiviceEncounterVirux) digivice_expedition_streak_lost(s);
    }

    if(kind == DigiviceEncounterTrail) {
        s->enc_foe = DIGIVICE_ENC_NONE;
        s->enc_expires = 0;
    } else if(kind == DigiviceEncounterVirux) {
        s->virux_type = DigiviceTypeNone;
    }
    return reward;
}
```

```c
static bool digivice_trail_screen(DigiviceScreen screen) {
    return screen == DigiviceScreenEncounter || screen == DigiviceScreenBattle;
}

void digivice_app_set_screen(DigiviceApp* app, DigiviceScreen screen) {
    // The background cache only lives while a trail screen shows it.
    if(!digivice_trail_screen(screen) && app->scene) {
        free(app->scene);
        app->scene = NULL;
    }
    app->screen = screen;
    app->screen_frame = 0;
}

const DigiviceScene* digivice_app_scene(DigiviceApp* app) {
    if(!app->scene) {
        app->scene = malloc(sizeof(DigiviceScene));
        digivice_scene_init(app->scene, app->state.area);
    } else if(app->scene->area != app->state.area) {
        digivice_scene_init(app->scene, app->state.area);
    }
    return app->scene;
}
```
