- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite the battle screen**

Layout from `v3-combate.html`: HUD bar y 0..7, arena y 11..50 with the pet's feet at (30, 50) and the foe's at (98, 50), panel y 51..63 with text lines at y 53 and 59. A shot is 5 wind-up, 4 travel and 5 impact frames; the service call `digivice_battle_finish` is made once, as soon as the outcome is decided, before the result animation.

Replace the whole content of `applications/main/digivice/digivice_app_battle.c` with exactly this content:

```c
#include "digivice_app_i.h"
#include <digivice/digivice_notify.h>
#include <furi_hal.h>
#include <notification/notification_messages.h>

#include <stdio.h>

// Frame marks of the 100 ms clock.
#define ENCOUNTER_FRAMES 40
#define LOCK_FRAMES      5 // the tier shows after OK, before the aim
#define SHOT_WINDUP      5
#define SHOT_TRAVEL      4
#define SHOT_IMPACT      5
#define SHOT_FRAMES      (SHOT_WINDUP + SHOT_TRAVEL + SHOT_IMPACT)
#define FADE_FRAMES      12
#define CLEAR_START      20 // the boss "area cleared" line follows the victory banner

// Arena layout (combate.html): pet feet at (30, 50), foe feet at (98, 50).
#define PET_X   30
#define FOE_X   98
#define FEET_Y  50
#define PET_CY  39
#define MOUTH_Y 36
#define GUARD_X 70

static const DigiviceStr tier_str[DigiviceChargeCount] = {
    DigiviceStrBtlShotSmall,
    DigiviceStrBtlShotNormal,
    DigiviceStrBtlShotBig,
};

static void battle_exit(DigiviceApp* app) {
    digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenMain);
    digivice_app_refresh(app);
}

static const char* foe_name(const DigiviceApp* app) {
    if(app->enc_kind == DigiviceEncounterBoss) return digivice_expedition_boss_name(app->enc_area);
    if(app->enc_kind == DigiviceEncounterVirux) return "VIRUX";
    return digivice_expedition_foe_name((DigiviceFoe)app->battle.foe);
}

// Glyph-safe prefix: the byte length of the first `glyphs` UTF-8 glyphs of `text`.
static size_t utf8_prefix(const char* text, uint32_t glyphs) {
    size_t i = 0;
    while(text[i]) {
        if(((unsigned char)text[i] & 0xC0) != 0x80) {
            if(glyphs == 0) break;
            glyphs--;
        }
        i++;
    }
    return i;
}

static void foe_led(DigiviceApp* app) {
    static const NotificationSequence* const leds[DigiviceAttrCount] = {
        &sequence_set_only_blue_255,
        &sequence_set_only_red_255,
        &sequence_set_only_green_255,
        &sequence_blink_white_100,
    };
    uint8_t attr = app->battle.foe_attr < DigiviceAttrCount ? app->battle.foe_attr :
                                                               DigiviceAttrNulo;
    notification_message(app->notification, leds[attr]);
}

void digivice_encounter_start(DigiviceApp* app, DigiviceEncounterKind kind) {
    uint32_t now = furi_hal_rtc_get_timestamp();
    DigiviceResult result = digivice_rules_can_battle(&app->state, now);
    if(result != DigiviceResultOk) {
        digivice_app_refusal(app, result);
        return;
    }
    app->enc_kind = (uint8_t)kind;
    app->enc_area = app->state.area;
    digivice_battle_init(&app->battle, &app->state, kind, now);
    app->streak_lost = false;
    app->area_cleared = false;
    digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenEncounter);
    notification_message(app->notification, &digivice_sequence_encounter);
}

static void battle_set_phase(DigiviceApp* app, DigiviceBattlePhase phase) {
    app->phase = phase;
    app->screen_frame = 0;
    if(phase == DigiviceBattlePhaseCharge) {
        app->zone_w = digivice_rules_charge_zone_w(app->state.strength);
        app->zone_start =
            digivice_rules_charge_zone_start(app->zone_w, (uint8_t)furi_hal_random_get());
        app->cursor = 0;
        app->charge_locked = false;
        notification_message(app->notification, &digivice_sequence_charge);
    } else if(phase == DigiviceBattlePhaseAim) {
        app->guard0 = (uint8_t)(furi_hal_random_get() & 1);
        app->aim = DigiviceAimHigh;
        app->aim_locked = false;
    }
}

void digivice_encounter_input(DigiviceApp* app, const InputEvent* event) {
    // Declining before the first charge is always free, even in streak areas.
    if(event->type == InputTypeShort && event->key == InputKeyBack) {
        notification_message(app->notification, &sequence_reset_rgb);
        battle_exit(app);
    }
}

void digivice_encounter_tick(DigiviceApp* app) {
    uint32_t f = app->screen_frame;
    if(f == 16) foe_led(app);
    if(f >= ENCOUNTER_FRAMES) {
        notification_message(app->notification, &sequence_reset_rgb);
        digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenBattle);
        battle_set_phase(app, DigiviceBattlePhaseCharge);
    }
}

// The pet, the foe and their shadows; (ox, oy) is the arena shake.
static void draw_fighters(
    Canvas* canvas,
    DigiviceApp* app,
    int32_t ox,
    int32_t oy,
    int32_t pet_dx,
    int32_t pet_dy,
    int32_t foe_dx,
    int32_t foe_dy,
    DigivicePose pet_pose,
    DigivicePose foe_pose) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_ring(canvas, PET_X + ox, FEET_Y + 1 + oy, 14, 2);
    digivice_draw_ring(canvas, FOE_X + ox, FEET_Y + 1 + oy, 16, 2);
    digivice_draw_form(
        canvas,
        (DigiviceForm)app->state.form,
        PET_X + ox + pet_dx,
        PET_CY + oy + pet_dy,
        app->frame,
        pet_pose,
        false);
    int32_t fx = FOE_X + ox + foe_dx;
    int32_t fy = FEET_Y + oy + foe_dy;
    if(app->enc_kind == DigiviceEncounterBoss) {
        digivice_draw_boss(
            canvas, app->enc_area, (DigiviceForm)app->state.form, fx, fy, app->frame, false);
    } else if(app->enc_kind == DigiviceEncounterVirux) {
        digivice_draw_virux(canvas, fx, fy - 14, app->frame);
    } else {
        digivice_draw_foe(
            canvas, (DigiviceFoe)app->battle.foe, fx, fy, app->frame, foe_pose, true, false);
    }
}

static void draw_panel(Canvas* canvas) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    canvas_draw_box(canvas, 0, 51, 128, 13);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    canvas_draw_line(canvas, 0, 51, 127, 51);
}

static int8_t player_edge(const DigiviceApp* app) {
    return digivice_rules_attr_adv(app->battle.player_attr, app->battle.foe_attr);
}

void digivice_encounter_draw(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app) {
    uint32_t f = app->screen_frame;
    bool boss = app->enc_kind == DigiviceEncounterBoss;
    char line[48];

    if(f < 16) {
        int32_t shake = boss && f < 8 ? (int32_t)(f / 2) % 3 - 1 : 0;
        digivice_draw_scene(
            canvas, digivice_app_scene(app), 0, app->frame, digivice_app_day_minute());
        digivice_draw_form(
            canvas,
            (DigiviceForm)app->state.form,
            34 + shake,
            PET_CY,
            app->frame,
            DigivicePoseIdle,
            false);
        if(boss) {
            if(f >= 8) {
                // The silhouette rises from below.
                int32_t rise = (int32_t)(16 - f) * 6;
                digivice_draw_boss(
                    canvas,
                    app->enc_area,
                    (DigiviceForm)app->state.form,
                    96,
                    FEET_Y + rise,
                    app->frame,
                    true);
                if(f == 12) digivice_draw_invert(canvas, 0, 0, 128, 64);
            } else {
                digivice_draw_hazard(canvas, 9, app->frame);
                digivice_draw_hazard(canvas, 56, app->frame);
                if(f % 2 == 0) {
                    digivice_draw_label(canvas, 28, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrEncAlert));
                }
            }
        } else if(f < 8) {
            digivice_draw_exclam_bubble(canvas, 40, 14 - (int32_t)(f % 4 < 2));
        } else {
            // Wipe in 16 strips, with glitch rows and two inverted frames.
            for(int32_t k = 0; k < (int32_t)(f - 8) * 2; k++)
                digivice_draw_box(canvas, 0, k * 4, 128, 4);
            digivice_draw_glitch(canvas, app->frame, 3);
            if(f == 10 || f == 13) digivice_draw_invert(canvas, 0, 0, 128, 64);
        }
        return;
    }

    // From frame 16 on: the arena with the fighters sliding in.
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    for(int32_t y = 11; y < 50; y += 8)
        for(int32_t x = 4; x < 128; x += 8)
            canvas_draw_dot(canvas, x, y);
    int32_t slide = f < 22 ? 50 : f < 29 ? (int32_t)(29 - f) * 7 : 0;
    draw_fighters(canvas, app, 0, 0, -slide, 0, slide, 0, DigivicePoseIdle, DigivicePoseIdle);

    if(f < 22) {
        // VS band (boss: the JEFE banner) wiping in diagonally.
        int32_t w = (int32_t)(f - 15) * 22;
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
        digivice_draw_fill_triangle(canvas, 0, 18, w, 18, w - 12, 40);
        digivice_draw_fill_triangle(canvas, 0, 18, w - 12, 40, 0, 40);
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
        if(boss) {
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 6, 22, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrEncBoss));
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 6, 30, foe_name(app));
            snprintf(
                line,
                sizeof(line),
                "%s%u",
                digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrEncZone),
                (unsigned)(app->enc_area + 1));
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 50, 30, line);
        } else {
            canvas_set_font(canvas, FontPrimary);
            canvas_draw_str(canvas, 54, 34, "VS");
        }
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    }

    draw_panel(canvas);
    if(f >= 29) {
        uint32_t typed = (f - 29) * 3;
        if(boss) {
            snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%s", foe_name(app));
        } else {
            snprintf(
                line,
                sizeof(line),
                "%s%s%s",
                digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrEncWildPre),
                foe_name(app),
                digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrEncWildPost));
        }
        line[utf8_prefix(line, typed)] = '\0';
        digivice_draw_attr_icon(canvas, (DigiviceAttr)app->battle.foe_attr, 3, 53);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 13, 53, line);
        int8_t edge = player_edge(app);
        DigiviceStr edge_str = edge > 0 ? DigiviceStrEncEdge :
                               edge < 0 ? DigiviceStrEncDisedge :
                                          DigiviceStrEncNeutral;
        snprintf(
            line,
            sizeof(line),
            "%s%u",
            digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrEncPow),
            (unsigned)app->battle.foe_power);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, line);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 40, 59, digivice_app_str(app, edge_str));
    }
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 92, 59, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrEncRetreatHint));
}

static void battle_finish(DigiviceApp* app, DigiviceBattleResult result) {
    app->result = (uint8_t)result;
    uint8_t cleared_before = app->state.cleared;
    app->reward = digivice_battle_finish(
        app->digivice, (DigiviceEncounterKind)app->enc_kind, result, app->battle.shield_used);
    digivice_get_state(app->digivice, &app->state);
    app->streak_lost = result == DigiviceBattleResultLose &&
                       app->enc_kind != DigiviceEncounterVirux &&
                       digivice_expedition_streak_area(app->enc_area);
    app->area_cleared = app->enc_kind == DigiviceEncounterBoss &&
                        result == DigiviceBattleResultWin && app->state.cleared != cleared_before;
    if(result == DigiviceBattleResultWin) {
        notification_message(app->notification, &digivice_sequence_win);
    } else if(result == DigiviceBattleResultLose) {
        notification_message(app->notification, &digivice_sequence_lose);
    } else {
        notification_message(app->notification, &sequence_reset_rgb);
    }
    battle_set_phase(app, DigiviceBattlePhaseEnd);
}

static void battle_after_shot(DigiviceApp* app, bool player) {
    DigiviceBattleOutcome outcome = digivice_battle_outcome(&app->battle);
    if(outcome == DigiviceBattleWon) {
        battle_finish(app, DigiviceBattleResultWin);
    } else if(outcome == DigiviceBattleLost) {
        battle_finish(app, DigiviceBattleResultLose);
    } else if(outcome == DigiviceBattleDrawn) {
        battle_finish(app, DigiviceBattleResultDraw);
    } else if(player) {
        app->aim = (uint8_t)(furi_hal_random_get() & 1);
        app->shot = (uint8_t)digivice_battle_foe_shot(&app->battle, digivice_app_rng_pct());
        battle_set_phase(app, DigiviceBattlePhaseFoeShot);
    } else {
        battle_set_phase(app, DigiviceBattlePhaseCharge);
    }
}

static void battle_lock_charge(DigiviceApp* app) {
    app->charge_locked = true;
    app->charge = (uint8_t)digivice_rules_charge_tier(app->cursor, app->zone_start, app->zone_w);
    app->screen_frame = 0;
}

static void battle_fire(DigiviceApp* app, uint8_t aim) {
    app->aim = aim;
    app->aim_locked = true;
    app->aim_frame = app->screen_frame;
    bool blocked = digivice_rules_shot_blocked(aim, app->guard0, app->aim_frame);
    app->shot = (uint8_t)digivice_battle_player_shot(
        &app->battle,
        (DigiviceCharge)app->charge,
        blocked,
        digivice_app_rng_pct(),
        digivice_app_rng_pct());
    notification_message(app->notification, &sequence_reset_rgb);
    battle_set_phase(app, DigiviceBattlePhasePlayerShot);
}

static void battle_try_retreat(DigiviceApp* app) {
    if(digivice_battle_retreat_allowed(&app->state, (DigiviceEncounterKind)app->enc_kind)) {
        battle_finish(app, DigiviceBattleResultFled);
    } else {
        digivice_app_notice(app, DigiviceStrBtlNoEscape);
    }
}

void digivice_battle_input(DigiviceApp* app, const InputEvent* event) {
    if(event->type != InputTypeShort) return;
    switch(app->phase) {
    case DigiviceBattlePhaseCharge:
        if(event->key == InputKeyOk && !app->charge_locked) {
            battle_lock_charge(app);
        } else if(event->key == InputKeyBack) {
            battle_try_retreat(app);
        }
        break;
    case DigiviceBattlePhaseAim:
        if(event->key == InputKeyUp) {
            battle_fire(app, DigiviceAimHigh);
        } else if(event->key == InputKeyDown) {
            battle_fire(app, DigiviceAimLow);
        } else if(event->key == InputKeyBack) {
            battle_try_retreat(app);
        }
        break;
    case DigiviceBattlePhaseEnd:
        if(event->key == InputKeyOk || event->key == InputKeyBack) battle_exit(app);
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
}

void digivice_battle_tick(DigiviceApp* app) {
    uint32_t f = app->screen_frame;
    switch(app->phase) {
    case DigiviceBattlePhaseCharge:
        if(!app->charge_locked) {
            app->cursor = digivice_rules_meter_cursor(f);
            if(f >= DIGIVICE_CHARGE_FRAMES) battle_lock_charge(app);
        } else if(f >= LOCK_FRAMES) {
            battle_set_phase(app, DigiviceBattlePhaseAim);
        }
        break;
    case DigiviceBattlePhaseAim:
        if(f >= DIGIVICE_AIM_FRAMES) battle_fire(app, DigiviceAimHigh);
        break;
    case DigiviceBattlePhasePlayerShot:
    case DigiviceBattlePhaseFoeShot: {
        bool player = app->phase == DigiviceBattlePhasePlayerShot;
        bool landed = app->shot == DigiviceShotHit || app->shot == DigiviceShotGuardBroken;
        if(f == SHOT_WINDUP + SHOT_TRAVEL && landed) {
            notification_message(app->notification, &digivice_sequence_hit);
        }
        if(f >= SHOT_FRAMES) battle_after_shot(app, player);
        break;
    }
    case DigiviceBattlePhaseEnd:
        if(app->area_cleared && f >= CLEAR_START + FADE_FRAMES) battle_exit(app);
        break;
    }
}

static void draw_hud(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    canvas_draw_box(canvas, 0, 0, 128, 8);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    const char* pet = digivice_form_name((DigiviceForm)app->state.form);
    const char* foe = foe_name(app);
    int32_t pet_w = digivice_draw_text_width(canvas, pet);
    int32_t foe_x = 126 - digivice_draw_text_width(canvas, foe);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 2, 1, pet);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, foe_x, 1, foe);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 58, 1, "VS");
    // Three hit boxes per side, right after each name so 9-glyph names still fit.
    for(int32_t i = 0; i < DIGIVICE_HITS_TO_WIN; i++) {
        int32_t px = 2 + pet_w + 3 + i * 6;
        int32_t fx = foe_x - 8 - i * 6;
        if(i < app->battle.player_hits) {
            canvas_draw_box(canvas, px, 2, 5, 5);
        } else {
            canvas_draw_frame(canvas, px, 2, 5, 5);
        }
        if(i < app->battle.foe_hits) {
            canvas_draw_box(canvas, fx, 2, 5, 5);
        } else {
            canvas_draw_frame(canvas, fx, 2, 5, 5);
        }
    }
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
}

// Projectile path for the shot on screen: frames SHOT_WINDUP .. SHOT_WINDUP + SHOT_TRAVEL.
static void draw_shot(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app, bool player, uint32_t f) {
    int32_t from = player ? PET_X + 10 : FOE_X - 12;
    int32_t to = player ? (app->shot == DigiviceShotBlocked ? GUARD_X : FOE_X - 8) : PET_X + 6;
    int32_t ty = MOUTH_Y + (app->aim == DigiviceAimHigh ? -6 : 6);
    uint8_t size = player ? app->battle.player_shot : app->battle.foe_shot;
    if(player && app->charge == DigiviceChargeBig && size < DIGIVICE_SHOT_BEAM) size += 2;
    uint8_t attr = player ? app->battle.player_attr : app->battle.foe_attr;
    int8_t dir = player ? 1 : -1;
    bool missed = app->shot == DigiviceShotDodge;
    if(f < SHOT_WINDUP) {
        digivice_draw_dash_ring(canvas, from, MOUTH_Y, 4 + (int32_t)f, app->frame);
        return;
    }
    int32_t t = (int32_t)(f - SHOT_WINDUP);
    if(t < SHOT_TRAVEL) {
        int32_t x = from + (to - from) * t / SHOT_TRAVEL;
        int32_t y = MOUTH_Y + (ty - MOUTH_Y) * t / SHOT_TRAVEL;
        digivice_draw_shot(canvas, (DigiviceAttr)attr, x, y, size, dir, app->frame);
    } else if(missed) {
        // The shot flies on past the target with speed lines.
        int32_t x = to + dir * (t - SHOT_TRAVEL + 1) * 14;
        digivice_draw_shot(canvas, (DigiviceAttr)attr, x, ty, size, dir, app->frame);
        for(int32_t k = 1; k <= 3; k++) {
            int32_t ly = ty - 4 + k * 2;
            digivice_draw_line(canvas, x - dir * (6 + k * 4), ly, x - dir * (2 + k * 4), ly);
        }
    }
}

static void draw_impact(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app, bool player, uint32_t f) {
    int32_t t = (int32_t)f - (SHOT_WINDUP + SHOT_TRAVEL);
    if(t < 0) return;
    int32_t tx = player ? FOE_X : PET_X;
    int32_t ty = MOUTH_Y + (app->aim == DigiviceAimHigh ? -6 : 6);
    DigiviceShot shot = (DigiviceShot)app->shot;
    if(shot == DigiviceShotShielded) {
        digivice_draw_shield(canvas, PET_X + 12, 26);
        digivice_draw_burst(canvas, PET_X + 14, 30, 4 + t * 2);
        return;
    }
    if(shot == DigiviceShotBlocked || shot == DigiviceShotGuardBroken) {
        digivice_draw_burst(canvas, GUARD_X + 2, ty, 3 + t * 2);
        if(shot == DigiviceShotBlocked) return;
    }
    if(shot == DigiviceShotHit || shot == DigiviceShotGuardBroken) {
        if(t < 2) digivice_draw_invert(canvas, tx - 16, 14, 32, 37);
        digivice_draw_star5(canvas, tx, ty);
        digivice_draw_burst(canvas, tx, ty, 4 + t * 2);
        // "-1" floating up in a small box.
        int32_t ny = 14 - t * 2;
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
        canvas_draw_box(canvas, tx - 5, ny - 1, 11, 8);
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, tx - 4, ny, "-1");
    }
}

static void draw_shot_lines(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app, bool player, uint32_t f) {
    char line[24];
    DigiviceShot shot = (DigiviceShot)app->shot;
    bool impact = f >= SHOT_WINDUP + SHOT_TRAVEL;
    if(!player) {
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlFoeTurn));
        if(!impact) return;
        DigiviceStr s = shot == DigiviceShotHit      ? DigiviceStrBtlFoeHit :
                        shot == DigiviceShotShielded ? DigiviceStrBtlShieldHolds :
                                                       DigiviceStrBtlFoeMiss;
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, digivice_app_str(app, s));
        return;
    }
    if(!impact) {
        DigiviceStr s = app->aim == DigiviceAimHigh ? DigiviceStrBtlShotHigh :
                                                      DigiviceStrBtlShotLow;
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, s));
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, digivice_app_str(app, tier_str[app->charge]));
        return;
    }
    if(shot == DigiviceShotHit) {
        // Label + " n/3": the hit count is drawn by the app, not by the string table.
        snprintf(
            line,
            sizeof(line),
            "%s %u/%u",
            digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlHit),
            (unsigned)app->battle.player_hits,
            (unsigned)DIGIVICE_HITS_TO_WIN);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, line);
    } else if(shot == DigiviceShotGuardBroken) {
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlBroken));
    } else if(shot == DigiviceShotBlocked) {
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlBlocked));
    } else {
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlDodge));
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlWhiff));
    }
}

static void draw_end(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app) {
    uint32_t f = app->screen_frame;
    char line[24];
    DigiviceBattleResult result = (DigiviceBattleResult)app->result;
    bool won = result == DigiviceBattleResultWin;
    bool lost = result == DigiviceBattleResultLose;
    int32_t sink = lost ? 2 : 0;

    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    for(int32_t y = 11; y < 50; y += 8)
        for(int32_t x = 4; x < 128; x += 8)
            canvas_draw_dot(canvas, x, y);
    draw_fighters(
        canvas,
        app,
        0,
        0,
        0,
        sink,
        0,
        0,
        won ? DigivicePoseAttack : DigivicePoseIdle,
        lost ? DigivicePoseAttack : DigivicePoseHurt);
    if(won) {
        // The foe fades out under a white dither.
        uint8_t level = f < FADE_FRAMES ? (uint8_t)(f * 16 / FADE_FRAMES) : 16;
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
        digivice_draw_dither(canvas, FOE_X - 28, 8, 56, 43, level);
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
        digivice_draw_sparkle_ring(canvas, PET_X, PET_CY, 16, 10, app->frame);
        // The banner drops in over 5 frames; labels stay on screen, never at negative rows.
        int32_t by = f < 5 ? (int32_t)f * 2 : 10;
        digivice_draw_label(canvas, by, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlVictory));
        if(app->reward.item == DigiviceItemShield && f >= 8) {
            int32_t bounce = f < 14 ? (int32_t)((14 - f) % 4) : 0;
            digivice_draw_shield(canvas, 62, 22 - bounce);
        }
    } else if(lost) {
        int32_t lx = f < 5 ? 5 + (int32_t)f * 8 : 45;
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
        canvas_draw_box(canvas, lx - 3, 10, 40, 8);
        canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
        canvas_draw_frame(canvas, lx - 3, 10, 40, 8);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, lx, 11, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlDefeat));
        if(f >= 6) {
            digivice_draw_bandage(canvas, PET_X + 4, PET_CY - 12);
            int32_t r = 3 + (int32_t)(f - 6) * 2;
            if(f < 9) digivice_draw_burst(canvas, PET_X + 7, PET_CY - 9, r);
        }
    }

    draw_panel(canvas);
    if(won) {
        uint32_t shown = f < 10 ? app->reward.exp * f / 10 : app->reward.exp;
        snprintf(
            line,
            sizeof(line),
            "+%lu %s",
            (unsigned long)shown,
            digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrExp));
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, line);
        if(app->area_cleared && f >= CLEAR_START) {
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlAreaClear));
        } else if(app->reward.item == DigiviceItemShield && f >= 8) {
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlItemShield));
        }
    } else if(lost) {
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlHurt));
        if(app->streak_lost) {
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlStreakLost));
        }
    } else {
        DigiviceStr s = result == DigiviceBattleResultDraw ? DigiviceStrBtlDraw :
                                                             DigiviceStrBtlRetreated;
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, s));
    }
}

void digivice_battle_draw(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app) {
    if(app->phase == DigiviceBattlePhaseEnd) {
        draw_end(canvas, app);
        draw_hud(canvas, app);
        return;
    }
    uint32_t f = app->screen_frame;
    bool player = app->phase == DigiviceBattlePhasePlayerShot;
    bool shooting = player || app->phase == DigiviceBattlePhaseFoeShot;
    int32_t impact = shooting ? (int32_t)f - (SHOT_WINDUP + SHOT_TRAVEL) : -1;
    bool landed = app->shot == DigiviceShotHit || app->shot == DigiviceShotGuardBroken;

    // The arena shakes 2 px on the first impact frame and 1 px on the next three.
    int32_t ox = 0;
    int32_t oy = 0;
    if(landed && impact >= 0 && impact < 4) {
        int32_t amp = impact == 0 ? 2 : 1;
        ox = (app->frame % 2) ? amp : -amp;
        oy = (app->frame % 3) ? 0 : amp;
    }
    // A dodging target hops in a sine arc of up to 10 px.
    static const int8_t hop[SHOT_IMPACT] = {-4, -8, -10, -8, -4};
    int32_t pet_dy = 0;
    int32_t foe_dy = 0;
    if(app->shot == DigiviceShotDodge && impact >= 0 && impact < SHOT_IMPACT) {
        if(player) {
            foe_dy = hop[impact];
        } else {
            pet_dy = hop[impact];
        }
    }

    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    for(int32_t y = 11; y < 50; y += 8)
        for(int32_t x = 4; x < 128; x += 8)
            canvas_draw_dot(canvas, x + ox, y + oy);
    DigivicePose pet_pose = DigivicePoseIdle;
    DigivicePose foe_pose = DigivicePoseIdle;
    if(player && f < SHOT_WINDUP + SHOT_TRAVEL) pet_pose = DigivicePoseAttack;
    if(!player && shooting && f < SHOT_WINDUP + SHOT_TRAVEL) foe_pose = DigivicePoseAttack;
    if(player && landed && impact >= 0) foe_pose = DigivicePoseHurt;
    bool charged = app->phase == DigiviceBattlePhaseCharge && app->charge_locked;
    if(charged) pet_pose = DigivicePoseAttack;
    draw_fighters(canvas, app, ox, oy, 0, pet_dy, 0, foe_dy, pet_pose, foe_pose);

    if(charged) {
        // The dash ring around the mouth grows with the tier.
        int32_t r = 4 + app->charge * 3 + (int32_t)f;
        digivice_draw_dash_ring(canvas, PET_X + 10, MOUTH_Y, r, app->frame);
    }
    // The foe guard flickers during the aim and freezes where the shot lands.
    if(app->phase == DigiviceBattlePhaseAim || player) {
        uint32_t gf = app->phase == DigiviceBattlePhaseAim ?
                          f :
                          app->aim_frame + DIGIVICE_IMPACT_LAG_FRAMES;
        uint8_t guard = digivice_rules_guard_at(app->guard0, gf);
        bool burst = player && app->shot == DigiviceShotGuardBroken && impact >= 0;
        int32_t gy = (guard == DigiviceAimHigh ? 18 : 34) + oy;
        if(!burst) digivice_draw_shield(canvas, GUARD_X + ox, gy);
    }
    if(shooting) {
        draw_shot(canvas, app, player, f);
        draw_impact(canvas, app, player, f);
    }

    draw_panel(canvas);
    if(app->phase == DigiviceBattlePhaseCharge) {
        // FUERZA and one heart per zone segment above the minimum.
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 76, 12, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrProfileStrength));
        for(int32_t i = 0; i <= app->zone_w - DIGIVICE_ZONE_BASE_W; i++)
            digivice_draw_heart(canvas, 101 + i * 6, 12);
        if(app->charge_locked) {
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, tier_str[app->charge]));
        } else {
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlCharge));
        }
        digivice_draw_charge_meter(canvas, 3, 59, app->zone_start, app->zone_w, app->cursor);
        digivice_draw_ok_button(canvas, 109, 53, app->charge_locked);
    } else if(app->phase == DigiviceBattlePhaseAim) {
        bool blink = (app->frame % 4) < 2;
        digivice_draw_arrow(canvas, 3, 14, true, blink);
        digivice_draw_arrow(canvas, 3, 40, false, blink);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlAim));
        if(digivice_battle_retreat_allowed(&app->state, (DigiviceEncounterKind)app->enc_kind)) {
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrEncRetreatHint));
        } else {
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrBtlRule));
        }
    } else {
        if(player && f < SHOT_WINDUP) {
            // The pressed arrow stays filled through the wind-up.
            bool high = app->aim == DigiviceAimHigh;
            digivice_draw_arrow(canvas, 3, high ? 14 : 40, high, true);
        }
        draw_shot_lines(canvas, app, player, f);
    }
    if(app->notice_frames) digivice_draw_label(canvas, 20, digivice_app_str(app, app->notice));
    draw_hud(canvas, app);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Remove the v1 battle strings**

In `applications/services/digivice/digivice_i18n.h`, replace:

```c
    DigiviceStrTrainHit,
    DigiviceStrTrainMiss,

    DigiviceStrBattleAttack,
    DigiviceStrBattleDefend,
    DigiviceStrBattleFlee,
    DigiviceStrBattleAim,
    DigiviceStrBattleFoeStrikes,
    DigiviceStrBattleFled,
    DigiviceStrBattleCrit,
    DigiviceStrBattleGlancing,
    DigiviceStrBattleMiss,
    DigiviceStrBattleAppeared,
    DigiviceStrBattleWon,
    DigiviceStrBattleLost,

    DigiviceStrCleanDone,
    DigiviceStrLightYawn,
    DigiviceStrLightNight,
```

with:

```c
    DigiviceStrTrainHit,
    DigiviceStrTrainMiss,

    DigiviceStrCleanDone,
    DigiviceStrLightYawn,
    DigiviceStrLightNight,
```

In `applications/services/digivice/digivice_i18n.c`, replace:

```c
    [DigiviceStrTrainHit] = "FUERZA +1  " A_ACUTE "NIMO +1",
    [DigiviceStrTrainMiss] = "FALLASTE...",

    [DigiviceStrBattleAttack] = "ATACAR",
    [DigiviceStrBattleDefend] = "DEFENDER",
    [DigiviceStrBattleFlee] = "HUIR",
    [DigiviceStrBattleAim] = EXCL_INV "PULSA OK A TIEMPO!",
```

```c
}

void digivice_draw_hp_box(
    Canvas* canvas,
    int32_t x,
    int32_t y,
    const char* name,
    uint16_t hp,
    uint16_t hp_max) {
    const int32_t w = 58;
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    canvas_draw_box(canvas, x, y, w, 13);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_brackets(canvas, x, y, x + w - 1, y + 12, 3);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, x + 3, y + 1, name);

    uint32_t pct = hp_max ? (uint32_t)hp * 100 / hp_max : 0;
    // Sized for any 32-bit value: gcc's -Wformat-truncation can't see that pct <= 100.
    char text[12];
    snprintf(text, sizeof(text), "%lu%%", (unsigned long)pct);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, x + w - 3 - digivice_draw_text_width(canvas, text), y + 1, text);

    uint32_t segs = hp_max ? ((uint32_t)hp * 10 + hp_max - 1) / hp_max : 0;
    for(uint32_t i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        int32_t sx = x + 3 + (int32_t)i * 5;
        if(i < segs) {
            canvas_draw_box(canvas, sx, y + 8, 4, 3);
        } else {
            canvas_draw_box(canvas, sx, y + 10, 4, 1);
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: CI/device check (controller)**

The controller now pushes Tasks 8 and 9 together and watches CI: both jobs green. Device check, in Hacker mode with a Rookie or older pet:
1. Read an NFC card or a Sub-GHz signal until a VIRUX spawns, then open the app: the BATALLA icon starts the encounter. Frames: "!" bubble, strip wipe with two inverted frames, VS band, both fighters slide in, the typed "¡UN VIRUX APARECE!" with attribute icon, POT and VENTAJA / DESVENTAJA / NEUTRO.
2. Back during the transition returns to the main screen and nothing is lost.
3. Charge: the cursor ping-pongs, the zone is hollow, FUERZA and hearts top right, OK locks the tier ("¡TIRO GRANDE!" inside the zone) and a dash ring grows.
4. Aim: the guard flickers high/low every half second; Up or Down fires; a matched guard shows "¡BLOQUEADO!" or "¡GUARDIA ROTA!".
5. A hit inverts the target, shakes the arena 2 px then 1 px with a short buzz; a dodge makes the target hop and the shot fly off with speed lines.
6. First to 3 hits: victory banner, "+20 EXP", green LED and tune; or defeat with "DERROTA", a bandage, "¡HERIDO! -1 ÁNIMO", red LED and the falling two-note phrase.
7. Back during Charge or Aim retreats ("HUISTE SIN DAÑO"); FICHA shows the EXP, wins and losses.

- [ ] **Step 4: Create the trail, map, Hike, midway and gate screens**

Layouts from `v3-expedicion.html`: trail view (decision D12), map nodes at `(10,44) (25,25) (41,44) (56,23) (72,43) (88,22) (104,40) (118,20)`, Hike notes scrolling 4 px per frame into the target box at x 60..65, y 57..62. OK on the trail view does the first applicable action: pending encounter, ready boss (fight or gate), available midway, else Hike. The app polls the service once per second because distance is never published.

Create `applications/main/digivice/digivice_app_expedition.c` with exactly this content:

```c
#include "digivice_app_i.h"
#include <digivice/digivice_notify.h>
#include <furi_hal.h>
#include <notification/notification_messages.h>

#include <stdio.h>

#define TAG "DigiviceTrail"

// Progress line of the trail view (expedicion.html, trail scene).
#define LINE_X0 8
#define LINE_X1 116
#define LINE_Y  30

#define HIKE_TARGET_X 60
#define HIKE_FLASH    2

// Map nodes on a zig-zag, one per area.
static const uint8_t node_x[DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT] = {10, 25, 41, 56, 72, 88, 104, 118};
static const uint8_t node_y[DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT] = {44, 25, 44, 23, 43, 22, 40, 20};

static const DigiviceStr next_str[DigiviceMidwayCount] = {
    DigiviceStrExpNextSpring,
    DigiviceStrExpNextCache,
    DigiviceStrExpNextShortcut,
};

static const DigiviceStr mid_title[DigiviceMidwayCount] = {
    DigiviceStrMidTitleSpring,
    DigiviceStrMidTitleCache,
    DigiviceStrMidTitleShortcut,
};

static bool trail_walking(const DigiviceApp* app, uint32_t now) {
    return digivice_expedition_can_walk(&app->state) && !digivice_rules_is_asleep(now);
}

// Why the pet is not walking, or 0 (DigiviceStrMenuFeed) when it is.
static DigiviceStr idle_reason(const DigiviceApp* app, uint32_t now) {
    if(app->state.sick) return DigiviceStrExpIdleSick;
    if(digivice_rules_is_asleep(now)) return DigiviceStrExpIdleAsleep;
    if(digivice_rules_stage(&app->state) < DigiviceStageBaby) return DigiviceStrExpIdleYoung;
    return DigiviceStrMenuFeed;
}

void digivice_expedition_enter(DigiviceApp* app) {
    digivice_get_state(app->digivice, &app->state);
    notification_message(app->notification, &sequence_reset_rgb);
    digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenExpedition);
    digivice_app_refresh(app);
}

void digivice_expedition_auto_start(DigiviceApp* app) {
    const DigiviceState* s = &app->state;
    uint32_t now = furi_hal_rtc_get_timestamp();
    if(app->state.evo_from != DigiviceFormNone) return;
    if(digivice_rules_can_battle(s, now) != DigiviceResultOk) return;
    // Pending trail or VIRUX first, then an open boss; midway and gated bosses never auto-start.
    if(digivice_expedition_trail_pending(s, now)) {
        if(app->offered_kind == DigiviceEncounterTrail && app->offered_expires == s->enc_expires)
            return;
        digivice_encounter_start(app, DigiviceEncounterTrail);
    } else if(digivice_rules_virux_active(s, now)) {
        if(app->offered_kind == DigiviceEncounterVirux && app->offered_expires == s->virux_expires)
            return;
        digivice_encounter_start(app, DigiviceEncounterVirux);
    } else if(digivice_expedition_boss_ready(s) && digivice_expedition_boss_gate_open(s)) {
        if(app->offered_kind == DigiviceEncounterBoss && app->offered_expires == s->cleared)
            return;
        digivice_encounter_start(app, DigiviceEncounterBoss);
    }
}

static void hike_enter(DigiviceApp* app) {
    uint32_t now = furi_hal_rtc_get_timestamp();
    DigiviceResult result = digivice_rules_can_train(&app->state, now);
    if(result == DigiviceResultOk && digivice_rules_stage(&app->state) < DigiviceStageBaby) {
        result = DigiviceResultTooYoung;
    }
    if(result != DigiviceResultOk) {
        digivice_app_refusal(app, result);
        return;
    }
    if(!digivice_expedition_can_walk(&app->state)) return;
    app->hike_left = digivice_expedition_hike_left(&app->state, now);
    app->hike_good = 0;
    app->hike_paid = 0;
    app->hike_granted = 0;
    app->hike_flash = 0;
    // A capped hour shows the limit and starts no session.
    app->hike_done = app->hike_left == 0;
    app->hike_start = furi_get_tick();
    digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenHike);
}

static uint32_t hike_elapsed_ms(const DigiviceApp* app) {
    return (furi_get_tick() - app->hike_start) * 1000UL / furi_kernel_get_tick_frequency();
}

static void hike_finish(DigiviceApp* app) {
    app->hike_granted = digivice_hike_finish(app->digivice, app->hike_good);
    digivice_get_state(app->digivice, &app->state);
    app->hike_done = true;
}

static void midway_enter(DigiviceApp* app) {
    app->midway.kind = digivice_expedition_midway_kind(app->state.area);
    app->midway.ok = false;
    app->midway_claimed = false;
    digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenMidway);
    // Spring and cache pay out at once; only the shortcut asks first.
    if(app->midway.kind != DigiviceMidwayShortcut) {
        app->midway = digivice_midway_claim(app->digivice, true);
        app->midway_claimed = true;
        digivice_get_state(app->digivice, &app->state);
    }
}

static void trail_action(DigiviceApp* app) {
    const DigiviceState* s = &app->state;
    uint32_t now = furi_hal_rtc_get_timestamp();
    if(digivice_expedition_trail_pending(s, now)) {
        digivice_encounter_start(app, DigiviceEncounterTrail);
    } else if(digivice_rules_virux_active(s, now)) {
        digivice_encounter_start(app, DigiviceEncounterVirux);
    } else if(digivice_expedition_boss_ready(s)) {
        if(digivice_expedition_boss_gate_open(s)) {
            digivice_encounter_start(app, DigiviceEncounterBoss);
        } else {
            digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenGate);
        }
    } else if(s->mid_state == 1) {
        midway_enter(app);
    } else if(!digivice_expedition_all_clear(s)) {
        hike_enter(app);
    }
}

static void trail_input(DigiviceApp* app, const InputEvent* event) {
    if(event->type != InputTypeShort) return;
    if(event->key == InputKeyOk) {
        trail_action(app);
    } else if(event->key == InputKeyUp) {
        app->map_sel = app->state.area;
        digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenMap);
    } else if(event->key == InputKeyBack) {
        digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenMain);
    }
}

static void map_input(DigiviceApp* app, const InputEvent* event) {
    if(event->type != InputTypeShort && event->type != InputTypeRepeat) return;
    if(event->key == InputKeyLeft && app->map_sel > 0) {
        app->map_sel--;
    } else if(event->key == InputKeyRight && app->map_sel < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT - 1) {
        app->map_sel++;
    } else if(event->type == InputTypeShort && event->key == InputKeyOk) {
        if(digivice_select_area(app->digivice, app->map_sel)) {
            digivice_get_state(app->digivice, &app->state);
            digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenExpedition);
        } else {
            digivice_app_notice(app, DigiviceStrNoticeAreaLocked);
        }
    } else if(event->type == InputTypeShort && event->key == InputKeyBack) {
        digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenExpedition);
    }
}

static void hike_input(DigiviceApp* app, const InputEvent* event) {
    if(event->type != InputTypeShort) return;
    if(app->hike_done) {
        if(event->key == InputKeyOk || event->key == InputKeyBack) digivice_expedition_enter(app);
        return;
    }
    if(event->key == InputKeyBack) {
        hike_finish(app);
    } else if(event->key == InputKeyOk) {
        uint8_t beat = 0;
        int32_t phase = digivice_expedition_hike_phase(hike_elapsed_ms(app), &beat);
        // Each beat pays once; a bad or extra tap does nothing.
        if(digivice_expedition_hike_tap_good(phase) && !(app->hike_paid & (1UL << beat))) {
            app->hike_paid |= 1UL << beat;
            app->hike_good++;
            app->hike_flash = HIKE_FLASH;
            notification_message(app->notification, &sequence_blink_green_100);
        }
    }
}

static void midway_input(DigiviceApp* app, const InputEvent* event) {
    if(event->type != InputTypeShort) return;
    if(app->midway_claimed) {
        if(event->key == InputKeyOk || event->key == InputKeyBack) digivice_expedition_enter(app);
        return;
    }
    if(event->key == InputKeyOk || event->key == InputKeyBack) {
        app->midway = digivice_midway_claim(app->digivice, event->key == InputKeyOk);
        app->midway_claimed = true;
        digivice_get_state(app->digivice, &app->state);
    }
}

static void gate_input(DigiviceApp* app, const InputEvent* event) {
    if(event->type != InputTypeShort) return;
    if(event->key == InputKeyLeft || event->key == InputKeyBack) {
        digivice_app_set_screen(app, DigiviceScreenMain);
    } else if(event->key == InputKeyRight) {
        uint32_t now = furi_hal_rtc_get_timestamp();
        DigiviceResult result = digivice_rules_can_train(&app->state, now);
        if(result == DigiviceResultOk) {
            digivice_train_enter(app);
        } else {
            digivice_app_refusal(app, result);
        }
    }
}

void digivice_expedition_input(DigiviceApp* app, const InputEvent* event) {
    switch(app->screen) {
    case DigiviceScreenExpedition:
        trail_input(app, event);
        break;
    case DigiviceScreenMap:
        map_input(app, event);
        break;
    case DigiviceScreenHike:
        hike_input(app, event);
        break;
    case DigiviceScreenMidway:
        midway_input(app, event);
        break;
    case DigiviceScreenGate:
        gate_input(app, event);
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
}

void digivice_expedition_tick(DigiviceApp* app) {
    uint32_t now = furi_hal_rtc_get_timestamp();
    // Distance is bookkeeping and never published: poll the service once per second.
    if(app->screen != DigiviceScreenHike && app->frame % 10 == 0) digivice_app_refresh(app);
    if(app->screen == DigiviceScreenExpedition || app->screen == DigiviceScreenHike) {
        if(app->screen == DigiviceScreenHike || trail_walking(app, now)) app->trail_scroll++;
    }
    if(app->screen != DigiviceScreenHike || app->hike_done) return;
    if(app->hike_flash) app->hike_flash--;
    uint32_t end_ms = DIGIVICE_HIKE_BEATS * DIGIVICE_HIKE_BEAT_MS + DIGIVICE_HIKE_BEAT_MS;
    if(hike_elapsed_ms(app) >= end_ms) hike_finish(app);
}

static void draw_top_bar(Canvas* canvas, const char* left, const char* right) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    canvas_draw_box(canvas, 0, 0, 128, 9);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 2, 2, left);
    if(right) digivice_draw_text(canvas, 126 - digivice_draw_text_width(canvas, right), 2, right);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
}

static void draw_panel(Canvas* canvas) {
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    canvas_draw_box(canvas, 0, 51, 128, 13);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    canvas_draw_line(canvas, 0, 51, 127, 51);
}

static const char* area_name(const DigiviceApp* app, uint8_t area) {
    return digivice_app_str(app, (DigiviceStr)(DigiviceStrArea1 + area));
}

static void draw_trail(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app) {
    const DigiviceState* s = &app->state;
    uint32_t now = furi_hal_rtc_get_timestamp();
    bool walking = trail_walking(app, now);
    uint16_t len = digivice_rules_area_length(s->area);
    uint16_t done = len - s->distance;
    // "D" plus 5 digits for any uint16_t, then the NUL.
    char text[24];

    uint32_t start = furi_get_tick();
    digivice_draw_scene(
        canvas, digivice_app_scene(app), app->trail_scroll, app->frame, digivice_app_day_minute());
    uint32_t draw_ms = (furi_get_tick() - start) * 1000UL / furi_kernel_get_tick_frequency();
    // Device check: the scene must stay under 30 ms per 100 ms frame.
    if(app->frame % 50 == 0) FURI_LOG_I(TAG, "Scene drawn in %lu ms", (unsigned long)draw_ms);

    snprintf(text, sizeof(text), "D%04u", (unsigned)s->distance);
    draw_top_bar(canvas, area_name(app, s->area), text);
    if(s->item == DigiviceItemShield) digivice_draw_shield(canvas, 92, 0);
    if(digivice_rules_injured(s, now)) digivice_draw_bandage(canvas, 82, 1);

    // Progress strip: start post, midway flag, skull at the end, the pet marker walking.
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    canvas_draw_box(canvas, 0, 18, 128, 16);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    canvas_draw_line(canvas, LINE_X0, LINE_Y, LINE_X1, LINE_Y);
    for(int32_t x = LINE_X0; x < LINE_X1; x += 2)
        canvas_draw_dot(canvas, x, LINE_Y + 2);
    int32_t px = LINE_X0 + (LINE_X1 - LINE_X0) * done / (len ? len : 1);
    canvas_draw_line(canvas, LINE_X0, LINE_Y + 1, px, LINE_Y + 1);
    canvas_draw_box(canvas, LINE_X0 - 2, 22, 1, 9);
    canvas_draw_box(canvas, LINE_X0 - 1, 22, 4, 3);
    int32_t mx = (LINE_X0 + LINE_X1) / 2;
    canvas_draw_line(canvas, mx, 21, mx, LINE_Y);
    if(s->mid_state == 0) {
        canvas_draw_frame(canvas, mx + 1, 21, 5, 4);
    } else {
        canvas_draw_box(canvas, mx + 1, 21, 5, 4);
    }
    digivice_draw_skull(canvas, LINE_X1 + 2, 24);
    int32_t bob = walking && (app->frame / 2) % 2 ? 1 : 0;
    digivice_draw_ring(canvas, px, 25 - bob, 3, 3);
    digivice_draw_fill_triangle(canvas, px - 2, 22 - bob, px, 22 - bob, px - 2, 19 - bob);
    digivice_draw_fill_triangle(canvas, px + 1, 22 - bob, px + 3, 22 - bob, px + 3, 19 - bob);

    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    canvas_draw_box(canvas, 0, 34, 128, 17);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    snprintf(
        text,
        sizeof(text),
        "%s %u/%u",
        digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrExpPaces),
        (unsigned)done,
        (unsigned)len);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 4, 37, text);
    snprintf(
        text,
        sizeof(text),
        "%s %u",
        digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrExpLeft),
        (unsigned)s->distance);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 4, 44, text);
    if(walking) {
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 78, 37, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrExpRate));
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 78, 44, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrExpAwake));
    }

    draw_panel(canvas);
    DigiviceStr next = DigiviceStrExpNextBoss;
    if(digivice_expedition_all_clear(s)) {
        next = DigiviceStrExpAllClear;
    } else if(s->mid_state == 0) {
        next = next_str[digivice_expedition_midway_kind(s->area)];
    }
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, next));

    DigiviceStr hint = DigiviceStrExpHintIdle;
    DigiviceStr reason = idle_reason(app, now);
    if(digivice_expedition_trail_pending(s, now) || digivice_rules_virux_active(s, now)) {
        hint = DigiviceStrExpHintFight;
    } else if(digivice_expedition_boss_ready(s)) {
        hint = digivice_expedition_boss_gate_open(s) ? DigiviceStrExpHintBoss :
                                                       DigiviceStrExpHintGate;
    } else if(s->mid_state == 1) {
        hint = DigiviceStrExpHintMidway;
    } else if(reason != DigiviceStrMenuFeed) {
        hint = reason;
    }
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, digivice_app_str(app, hint));
}

static void draw_map(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app) {
    const DigiviceState* s = &app->state;
    char text[40];
    draw_top_bar(canvas, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrMapTitle), NULL);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    for(uint8_t i = 0; i + 1 < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT; i++) {
        // Dotted path between the nodes.
        for(int32_t k = 1; k < 8; k += 2) {
            int32_t x = node_x[i] + (node_x[i + 1] - node_x[i]) * k / 8;
            int32_t y = node_y[i] + (node_y[i + 1] - node_y[i]) * k / 8;
            canvas_draw_dot(canvas, x, y);
        }
    }
    for(uint8_t i = 0; i < DIGIVICE_AREA_COUNT; i++) {
        int32_t x = node_x[i];
        int32_t y = node_y[i];
        bool done = s->cleared & (1U << i);
        bool here = s->area == i;
        bool open = digivice_expedition_area_unlocked(s, i);
        if(here && (app->frame % 6) < 2) {
            // The current node blinks.
        } else if(done) {
            digivice_draw_fill_ellipse(canvas, x, y, 4, 4);
            canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
            canvas_draw_line(canvas, x - 2, y, x - 1, y + 1);
            canvas_draw_line(canvas, x - 1, y + 1, x + 2, y - 2);
            canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
        } else {
            digivice_draw_ring(canvas, x, y, 4, 4);
            if(!open) digivice_draw_text(canvas, x - 1, y - 2, "?");
            if(here) digivice_draw_fill_ellipse(canvas, x, y, 1, 1);
        }
        if(digivice_expedition_streak_area(i)) {
            digivice_draw_brackets(canvas, x - 6, y - 6, x + 6, y + 6, 2);
        }
    }
    int32_t sx = node_x[app->map_sel];
    int32_t sy = node_y[app->map_sel];
    digivice_draw_brackets(canvas, sx - 8, sy - 8, sx + 8, sy + 8, 3);

    draw_panel(canvas);
    uint8_t a = app->map_sel;
    snprintf(text, sizeof(text), "%u %s", (unsigned)(a + 1), area_name(app, a));
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, text);
    DigiviceStr status = s->area == a                             ? DigiviceStrMapHere :
                         (s->cleared & (1U << a))                 ? DigiviceStrMapDone :
                         !digivice_expedition_area_unlocked(s, a) ? DigiviceStrMapLocked :
                                                                    DigiviceStrMenuFeed;
    if(status != DigiviceStrMenuFeed) {
        const char* label = digivice_app_str(app, status);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 126 - digivice_draw_text_width(canvas, label), 53, label);
    }
    snprintf(
        text,
        sizeof(text),
        "%u %s",
        (unsigned)digivice_rules_area_length(a),
        digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrExpPaces));
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, text);
    const char* rule = digivice_app_str(
        app, digivice_expedition_streak_area(a) ? DigiviceStrMapStreak : DigiviceStrMapRetreatOk);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 126 - digivice_draw_text_width(canvas, rule), 59, rule);
    if(app->notice_frames) digivice_draw_label(canvas, 12, digivice_app_str(app, app->notice));
}

static void draw_hike(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app) {
    char text[24];
    snprintf(
        text,
        sizeof(text),
        "%u/%u",
        (unsigned)app->hike_good,
        (unsigned)DIGIVICE_HIKE_BEATS);
    digivice_draw_scene(
        canvas, digivice_app_scene(app), app->trail_scroll, app->frame, digivice_app_day_minute());
    draw_top_bar(canvas, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrHikeTitle), text);
    digivice_draw_form(
        canvas,
        (DigiviceForm)app->state.form,
        64,
        34 - (int32_t)((app->frame / 2) % 2),
        app->frame,
        DigivicePoseIdle,
        false);

    draw_panel(canvas);
    if(app->hike_done) {
        if(app->hike_left == 0) {
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrHikeCapped));
        } else {
            snprintf(
                text,
                sizeof(text),
                "%s %u",
                digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrHikeGained),
                (unsigned)app->hike_granted);
            digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, text);
        }
        return;
    }
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 52, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrHikeHint));
    snprintf(text, sizeof(text), "%u/%u", (unsigned)app->hike_left, (unsigned)DIGIVICE_HIKE_CAP);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 126 - digivice_draw_text_width(canvas, text), 52, text);
    // Notes scroll left 4 px per 100 ms frame towards the target box.
    int32_t elapsed = (int32_t)hike_elapsed_ms(app);
    for(int32_t k = 0; k < DIGIVICE_HIKE_BEATS; k++) {
        int32_t beat_ms = (k + 1) * DIGIVICE_HIKE_BEAT_MS;
        int32_t x = HIKE_TARGET_X + 2 + (beat_ms - elapsed) / 25;
        bool paid = app->hike_paid & (1UL << k);
        if(x >= 0 && x < 128 && !paid) canvas_draw_box(canvas, x, 58, 2, 4);
    }
    canvas_draw_frame(canvas, HIKE_TARGET_X, 57, 6, 6);
    if(app->hike_flash) {
        canvas_draw_box(canvas, HIKE_TARGET_X + 1, 58, 4, 4);
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, HIKE_TARGET_X + 10, 58, "+1");
    }
}

static void draw_midway(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app) {
    char text[24];
    uint8_t kind = app->midway.kind < DigiviceMidwayCount ? app->midway.kind : 0;
    draw_top_bar(canvas, digivice_app_str(app, mid_title[kind]), NULL);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    if(kind == DigiviceMidwaySpring) {
        // A spring with droplets rising and falling.
        digivice_draw_ring(canvas, 92, 42, 14, 4);
        for(int32_t i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            int32_t q = (int32_t)((app->frame + (uint32_t)i * 3) % 12);
            canvas_draw_dot(canvas, 86 + i * 4, 38 - (q < 6 ? q : 12 - q) * 2);
        }
    } else if(kind == DigiviceMidwayCache) {
        canvas_draw_frame(canvas, 82, 30, 20, 14);
        canvas_draw_line(canvas, 82, 35, 101, 35);
        canvas_draw_box(canvas, 90, 33, 4, 4);
        if(app->midway_claimed && app->midway.ok) digivice_draw_shield(canvas, 104, 20);
    } else {
        // A fork in the path.
        canvas_draw_line(canvas, 70, 46, 90, 30);
        canvas_draw_line(canvas, 90, 30, 118, 22);
        canvas_draw_line(canvas, 90, 30, 116, 44);
    }
    digivice_draw_form(
        canvas, (DigiviceForm)app->state.form, 40, 34, app->frame, DigivicePoseIdle, false);

    draw_panel(canvas);
    if(!app->midway_claimed) {
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrMidShortcutAsk));
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrMidShortcutKeys));
        return;
    }
    DigiviceStr line = DigiviceStrMidSkipped;
    if(kind == DigiviceMidwaySpring) {
        line = DigiviceStrMidSpring;
    } else if(kind == DigiviceMidwayCache) {
        line = app->midway.ok ? DigiviceStrMidCacheShield : DigiviceStrMidCacheExp;
    } else if(app->midway.kind == DigiviceMidwayShortcut) {
        // Skip and lose both leave ok false: the distance tells them apart.
        line = app->midway.ok ? DigiviceStrMidShortcutWin : DigiviceStrMidShortcutLose;
    }
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, line));
    if(kind == DigiviceMidwayCache && !app->midway.ok) {
        snprintf(
            text,
            sizeof(text),
            "+%u %s",
            (unsigned)DIGIVICE_CACHE_EXP,
            digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrExp));
        digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, text);
    }
}

static void draw_gate(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app) {
    draw_top_bar(
        canvas,
        digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrEncBoss),
        digivice_expedition_boss_name(app->state.area));
    digivice_draw_boss(
        canvas,
        app->state.area,
        (DigiviceForm)app->state.form,
        96,
        50,
        app->frame,
        true);
    digivice_draw_form(
        canvas, (DigiviceForm)app->state.form, 30, 39, app->frame, DigivicePoseIdle, false);
    draw_panel(canvas);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 53, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrGateNeed));
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 59, "<");
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 9, 59, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrGateRetreat));
    const char* train = digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrMenuTrain);
    int32_t tx = 120 - digivice_draw_text_width(canvas, train);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, tx, 59, train);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 123, 59, ">");
    // The scratch line explains the gate while the pet is still a Rookie or younger.
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorWhite);
    canvas_draw_box(canvas, 0, 10, 128, 8);
    canvas_set_color(canvas, ColorBlack);
    digivice_draw_text(canvas, 3, 11, digivice_app_str(app, DigiviceStrGateScratch));
    if(app->notice_frames) digivice_draw_label(canvas, 20, digivice_app_str(app, app->notice));
}

void digivice_expedition_draw(Canvas* canvas, DigiviceApp* app) {
    switch(app->screen) {
    case DigiviceScreenExpedition:
        draw_trail(canvas, app);
        if(app->notice_frames) digivice_draw_label(canvas, 12, digivice_app_str(app, app->notice));
        break;
    case DigiviceScreenMap:
        draw_map(canvas, app);
        break;
    case DigiviceScreenHike:
        draw_hike(canvas, app);
        break;
    case DigiviceScreenMidway:
        draw_midway(canvas, app);
        break;
    case DigiviceScreenGate:
        draw_gate(canvas, app);
        break;
    default:
        break;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: CI/device check (controller)**

The controller pushes and watches CI: both jobs green. Device check (spec "Device check"):
1. The RUTA icon opens the trail view in Normal and in Hacker mode; "SOLO EN MODO HACKER" appears nowhere.
2. Leave the pet awake about 10 minutes: `D` falls by about 10 and the background scrolls. Asleep (23:00 to 07:00) and sick it freezes and the hint says why; an Egg shows "CRECE PARA CAMINAR".
3. Hike: a 12 s session grants about the number of good taps; the third session in one hour stops at 60 in total and then shows "LÍMITE POR HORA".
4. An encounter alerts (LED and vibration), expires after 30 minutes with no penalty, and opening the app goes straight into it; Back declines and the app does not insist. Retreat works in areas 1 to 4 and is refused with "¡NO HAY ESCAPE!" in 5 to 8 (select a later area only after clearing; or test with an edited save).
5. Midway: spring (all full), cache (Shield, or +20 EXP when one is held) and shortcut (OK tries, Back skips).
6. Boss: at `D0000` a Rookie sees the gate ("NECESITAS UN CAMPEÓN", RETIRARSE / ENTRENAR); a Champion fights; a win shows "¡ZONA COMPLETADA!" and the trail view shows the next area. Map: done nodes ticked, the current one blinks, locked ones show `?`, OK on a locked one shows "ZONA BLOQUEADA".
7. All 8 backgrounds by day and at night (20:00 to 23:00); the log shows `Scene drawn in N ms` with N < 30; no stutter on the map.
8. Hacker mode: each read deed adds 3 paces (30 per hour) and can still spawn a VIRUX.

### Task 12: Final verification, flash budget and push

**Files:** none changed.

**Interfaces:** Consumes all previous tasks. Produces the pushed `digivice-v3.1` branch, the flash delta and the CI artifact for the full device check.

- [ ] **Step 1: Host tests from a clean tree**

```bash
git status --short
applications/services/digivice/test/run.sh
```

Expected: no modified tracked files, then the four "all checks passed" lines (rules, expedition, battle, i18n).

- [ ] **Step 2: Review the branch history**

```bash
git log --oneline digivice-v2.1..digivice-v3.1
git log --format='%B' digivice-v2.1..digivice-v3.1 | rg -i 'co-authored|claude|generated'
```

Expected: eleven Conventional Commits (Task 1's docs commit and one per task 2 to 11) above the branch's first commit `docs(digivice): add v3 research, design mockups and status handoff`, and no output from the second command.

- [ ] **Step 3: Check that no stray file changed**

```bash
git diff --stat digivice-v2.1..digivice-v3.1
```

Expected: only the files in the File Structure table and `docs/`. In particular no change to `applications/services/desktop/views/desktop_view_digivice.c` or any other stock file.

- [ ] **Step 4: Flash budget**

From the latest green CI run, read the "Report firmware size" line and subtract the Task 1 baseline (`text + data`). Expected: at most 40,960 bytes added (about 33 KB expected). Above that, stop and re-scope with the user before the device check.

- [ ] **Step: push (controller only)**

Run only when the controller explicitly says so:

```bash
git push digivice digivice-v3.1
```

The controller then watches CI:

```bash
gh run list --repo pausf/flipper-digivice --branch digivice-v3.1 --limit 1
gh run watch --repo pausf/flipper-digivice <run-id>
```

Expected: `rules-tests` and `firmware` both succeed, the size step prints the final numbers, and the `digivice-update-package` artifact is uploaded.

- [ ] **Step 5: Device check (user)**

Flash `digivice-update-package` over USB with `scripts/selfupdate.py` (or qFlipper), then check the spec's device list:
1. The existing pet survives the update (form, EXP, mode, language) and keeps its progress; no paces appear for the time off. A v1 save, if one is available, also loads.
2. The trail icon opens the Expedition in both modes; "SOLO EN MODO HACKER" is gone.
3. Paces: about 10 in 10 awake minutes; frozen asleep and sick.
4. Hike: about the good taps per session, capped at 60 per hour.
5. Encounters: LED and vibration, 30-minute expiry without penalty, auto-start on open, decline with Back, retreat allowed in areas 1 to 4 and refused in 5 to 8.
6. A full fight: charge, aim, guard flicker, hit flash with shake, dodge, 3 hits to win, a Shield drop, defeat with injury and bandage.
7. One midway event of each kind, and a boss in an early area, including the gate as a Rookie.
8. All 8 backgrounds, day and night, at 10 fps; `Scene drawn in N ms` below 30; no stutter on the map.
9. Hacker mode: scans add 3 paces each (30 per hour) and still spawn VIRUX.
10. `Stack free after a catch-up tick` (after the Flipper was off for an hour or more) is at least 512.
11. The flash delta is within 40 KB.
12. The stock Flipper still works: main menu, NFC, Sub-GHz, the desktop lock, the dolphin animations under other apps, and the Hacker deeds.

| Expedition, map, Hike, encounter transition, battle screen, result screens, controls | Tasks 9, 10 |
